{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_015enwrJ45hRbqf4iV96L53f","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# CanvasChain Symphony – Proof-of-Inspiration (PoI) Consensus  \n     3→*Version 1.3 – Status: **Draft-Stable***  \n     4→\n     5→---\n     6→\n     7→## 1  Overview\n     8→Proof-of-Inspiration (PoI) is CanvasChain Symphony’s bespoke, stake-weighted, VRF-driven consensus that empowers **composer nodes** to publish on-chain creative updates (“movements”).  \n     9→PoI combines:\n    10→\n    11→* **Delegated Proof-of-Stake (DPoS)** – capital-efficient security and democratic representation.  \n    12→* **Verifiable Random Functions (VRF)** – unbiased, publicly verifiable randomness to ∀ participants.  \n    13→* **On-chain Inspiration Score** – dynamic modifier fueled by historical creative performance, rewarding nodes that consistently contribute high-value artwork.\n    14→\n    15→The result is a fast-finality, low-energy, creative-centric protocol optimized for **interactive NFT evolution**.\n    16→\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## 2  Design Goals\n    20→\n    21→| Goal                   | Rationale                                               |\n    22→|------------------------|---------------------------------------------------------|\n    23→| Deterministic yet unpredictable leader election | Prevent censorship & ensure fairness. |\n    24→| Human-readable on-chain proofs                 | Facilitate curator audits & gallery integrations. |\n    25→| Upgrade-friendly crypto curves (Strategy Pattern) | Support Ed25519, BLS12-381, PQC curves. |\n    26→| Slashing for inactivity or malicious art spam | Maintain network & aesthetic integrity. |\n    27→\n    28→---\n    29→\n    30→## 3  Terminology\n    31→\n    32→| Term               | Meaning                                                             |\n    33→|--------------------|---------------------------------------------------------------------|\n    34→| Composer Node      | Validator eligible to mint a **Movement Block** during a round.     |\n    35→| Stake Pool         | Set of delegators backing a validator’s stake.                      |\n    36→| Inspiration Score  | Exponential moving average of community upvotes on past work.      |\n    37→| Movement Block     | L2 block holding multi-layer NFT diffs + governance meta.           |\n    38→| Epoch              | 4 960 blocks (≈ 24 h) after which stake snapshots & rewards settle. |\n    39→| Round              | Smallest consensus unit – one Movement Block opportunity.          |\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→## 4  Stake Weighting Formula\n    44→\n    45→```\n    46→effective_stake(v) =\n    47→    base_stake(v) +\n    48→    Σ delegator_balances(v)  ×  loyalty_multiplier(d) +\n    49→    inspiration_score(v) × β\n    50→```\n    51→\n    52→*`β` is set by governance (default 1 ‰ of total supply).  \n    53→*Loyalty multiplier is 1.0 → 1.2 based on delegation age.*\n    54→\n    55→---\n    56→\n    57→## 5  VRF-Based Composer Selection\n    58→\n    59→Each validator performs:\n    60→\n    61→1. Compute `seed = H(epoch_randomness ‖ round_no)`.\n    62→2. Evaluate `vrf_out, vrf_proof = VRF_sk(seed)`.\n    63→3. Calculate `threshold = 2^256 × effective_stake / total_effective_stake`.\n    64→4. If `vrf_out < threshold` → **win** composer slot.\n    65→\n    66→Because `vrf_out` is ∈ [0,2²⁵⁶), the probability of winning is proportional to stake.  \n    67→The `vrf_proof` is gossiped with the candidate Movement Block allowing any node to verify:\n    68→\n    69→```rust\n    70→pub fn verify_candidate(\n    71→    pk: &PublicKey,\n    72→    proof: &VrfProof,\n    73→    seed: &[u8; 32],\n    74→    threshold: U256,\n    75→) -> bool {\n    76→    match pk.verify_vrf(seed, proof) {\n    77→        Ok(vrf_out) => U256::from_big_endian(&vrf_out) < threshold,\n    78→        Err(_) => false,\n    79→    }\n    80→}\n    81→```\n    82→\n    83→The Strategy Pattern enables drop-in `verify_vrf` impls for *Ed25519-Dalek*, *blst*, or future PQC curves.\n    84→\n    85→---\n    86→\n    87→## 6  Round Lifecycle\n    88→\n    89→```mermaid\n    90→sequenceDiagram\n    91→    participant V as Validator\n    92→    participant P as PeerValidators\n    93→    participant EB as EventBus(gRPC)\n    94→    V->>V: Generate VRF proof\n    95→    V-->>P: Gossip { Movement Block, vrf_proof }\n    96→    P->>P: Verify vrf, check stake threshold\n    97→    P-->>EB: Emit RoundValidated\n    98→    EB-->>SmartContracts: Relay NFT diffs\n    99→    EB-->>Wallets: Stream ownership events\n   100→```\n   101→\n   102→1. **VRF Generation** – Validators lock in their intent ≤ 400 ms.  \n   103→2. **Proposal Gossip** – Only one valid Movement Block per round is accepted.  \n   104→3. **Fast Finality** – BFT pre-vote + pre-commit (< 2 s).  \n   105→4. **Event Hooks** – Once finalized, the Event Bus notifies microservices.  \n   106→\n   107→---\n   108→\n   109→## 7  Slashing & Rewards\n   110→\n   111→| Violation                     | Penalty                         |\n   112→|-------------------------------|---------------------------------|\n   113→| Double Movement (same round)  | 5 % stake slash + freeze 1 epoch |\n   114→| Invalid VRF proof             | 100 % block reward burned       |\n   115→| Inactivity ≥ 256 rounds       | 1 % stake slash                 |\n   116→\n   117→Block rewards are split:\n   118→\n   119→* 90 % → Composer + delegators (pro-rata)  \n   120→* 10 % → Inspiration Fund for community grants  \n   121→\n   122→---\n   123→\n   124→## 8  Reference Rust Implementation Snippet\n   125→\n   126→```rust\n   127→//! PoI core engine – extracts domain logic, free of networking details.\n   128→\n   129→use crate::crypto::{CurveKind, VrfKeypair};\n   130→use crate::staking::{StakeLedger, InspirationRegistry};\n   131→use crate::types::{EpochRandomness, RoundNumber, MovementBlock};\n   132→use anyhow::{anyhow, Result};\n   133→use uint::construct_uint;\n   134→\n   135→construct_uint! {\n   136→    /// 256-bit unsigned integer.\n   137→    pub struct U256(4);\n   138→}\n   139→\n   140→pub struct ComposerSelector<'a> {\n   141→    stake_ledger: &'a StakeLedger,\n   142→    inspiration:  &'a InspirationRegistry,\n   143→    curve:        CurveKind,\n   144→}\n   145→\n   146→impl<'a> ComposerSelector<'a> {\n   147→    pub fn new(\n   148→        stake_ledger: &'a StakeLedger,\n   149→        inspiration:  &'a InspirationRegistry,\n   150→        curve:        CurveKind,\n   151→    ) -> Self {\n   152→        Self { stake_ledger, inspiration, curve }\n   153→    }\n   154→\n   155→    /// Determine whether `keypair` is eligible to compose `round`.\n   156→    pub fn evaluate(\n   157→        &self,\n   158→        keypair: &VrfKeypair,\n   159→        epoch_rand: &EpochRandomness,\n   160→        round: RoundNumber,\n   161→    ) -> Result<Option<(U256 /*vrf_out*/, Vec<u8> /*proof*/)>> {\n   162→        // 1. Build seed\n   163→        let seed = blake3::hash(&[epoch_rand.as_ref(), &round.to_le_bytes()].concat());\n   164→        // 2. Evaluate VRF\n   165→        let (vrf_out, proof) = keypair\n   166→            .vrf_sign(&seed.as_bytes())\n   167→            .map_err(|e| anyhow!(\"vrf error: {e}\"))?;\n   168→        // 3. Compute threshold\n   169→        let effective = self.stake_ledger.effective_stake(&keypair.public)?;\n   170→        let total    = self.stake_ledger.total_effective_stake();\n   171→        if total.is_zero() {\n   172→            return Err(anyhow!(\"total stake is zero\"));\n   173→        }\n   174→        let threshold = (U256::MAX / total) * effective; // proportional.\n   175→        // 4. Decide\n   176→        let out = U256::from_big_endian(&vrf_out);\n   177→        if out < threshold {\n   178→            Ok(Some((out, proof)))\n   179→        } else {\n   180→            Ok(None)\n   181→        }\n   182→    }\n   183→}\n   184→```\n   185→\n   186→The snippet is dependency-free (besides `blake3`, `uint`, `anyhow`) and demonstrates Strategy-enabled `CurveKind`.\n   187→\n   188→---\n   189→\n   190→## 9  Integration Touch-Points\n   191→\n   192→1. **gRPC Service** `composer.proto` – publishes `SubmitMovement(Block, Proof)` RPC.  \n   193→2. **Event Bus Topics**  \n   194→   • `movement.finalized` – L2 block header.  \n   195→   • `staking.slash`      – Slashing verdicts.  \n   196→3. **Smart-Contract Hooks**  \n   197→   • `on_movement()` – Allow NFTs to react to fresh traits.  \n   198→\n   199→---\n   200→\n   201→## 10  Future Work\n   202→\n   203→* **MEV-Resistant Inspiration Auctions** – experimental.  \n   204→* **Composable ZK-VRF** – hide stake amounts while preserving verifiability.  \n   205→* **PQC Migration Path** – NIST finalist lattice-based VRF.\n   206→\n   207→---\n   208→\n   209→## 11  Glossary\n   210→\n   211→* **Movement** – incremental state diff of a multilayer NFT.  \n   212→* **Inspiration Fund** – treasury for artists, seeded by protocol inflation.  \n   213→* **Strategy Pattern** – runtime-selectable cryptographic backend.\n   214→\n   215→---\n   216→\n   217→© 2024 CanvasChain Contributors – Licensed under Apache-2.0\n   218→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Overview\n    13→2. Responsibilities\n    14→3. High-Level Architecture\n    15→4. Public gRPC API\n    16→5. Event Bus Channels\n    17→6. Build & Run\n    18→7. Configuration\n    19→8. Code Walk-Through\n    20→9. Error Semantics\n    21→10. Observability\n    22→11. Extending the Service\n    23→12. Security Considerations\n    24→13. License\n    25→\n    26→---\n    27→\n    28→## 1   Overview\n    29→The **Minting Service** orchestrates turning creative artifacts into on-chain, multilayer NFTs.  \n    30→It provides:\n    31→\n    32→* Streaming & batch minting endpoints\n    33→* Pluggable cryptographic curves (Strategy Pattern)\n    34→* Proxy contracts for gas-optimized NFT spawning\n    35→* Event sourcing for auditability\n    36→* Integration hooks for DeFi staking rewards\n    37→\n    38→The service is fully stateless; blockchain interactions are delegated to a **Wallet Adapter** module, while persistence is achieved via append-only **EventStore** snapshots in `PostgreSQL` (for rollups) and **IPFS/Arweave** for content addressable storage.\n    39→\n    40→---\n    41→\n    42→## 2   Responsibilities\n    43→• Validate mint requests (editions, royalties, metadata)  \n    44→• Generate deterministic metadata hashes using VRF seed (Proof-of-Inspiration)  \n    45→• Deploy **ERC-721**/**ERC-1155** compliant proxy contracts via Factory pattern  \n    46→• Emit `nft.minted` events for downstream services (marketplace, royalty-stream)  \n    47→• Throttle minting rate per composer node to prevent congestion  \n    48→\n    49→---\n    50→\n    51→## 3   High-Level Architecture\n    52→\n    53→```text\n    54→┌────────────┐      gRPC       ┌─────────────────┐    NATS     ┌─────────────┐\n    55→│  Composer   │ ─────────────► │  Minting Service │───────────►│ Event Bus   │\n    56→│  Node       │                │   (this repo)   │            │ (JetStream) │\n    57→└────────────┘                 └─────────────────┘            └────┬────────┘\n    58→      ▲                                ▲                              │\n    59→      │ VRF seed / stake               │                              │\n    60→      │                                │ Wallet Tx / audit events     ▼\n    61→┌────────────┐                   ┌────────────┐                ┌────────────┐\n    62→│  Wallet    │◄──────────────────│  Ethereum  │◄───────────────│  Indexer   │\n    63→│  Adapter   │ JSON-RPC / EIP-155│  /EVM L2   │  block events  │  Service   │\n    64→└────────────┘                   └────────────┘                └────────────┘\n    65→```\n    66→\n    67→---\n    68→\n    69→## 4   Public gRPC API\n    70→\n    71→`proto/minting/v1/minting.proto`\n    72→\n    73→```proto\n    74→service MintingService {\n    75→    rpc Mint (MintRequest) returns (MintResponse);\n    76→    rpc MintStream (stream MintRequest) returns (stream MintResponse);\n    77→    rpc Health (HealthCheckRequest) returns (HealthCheckResponse);\n    78→}\n    79→```\n    80→\n    81→Key messages:\n    82→\n    83→* `MintRequest`\n    84→  * `artist_id`, `asset_uri`, `supply`, `royalty_bps`, `signature`\n    85→* `MintResponse`\n    86→  * `token_id`, `tx_hash`, `status`\n    87→\n    88→See  |\n    95→| ---------------------- | --------- | ----------------------------------- |\n    96→| `nft.minted`           | publish   | `MintedEvent` (JSON)                |\n    97→| `nft.mint.failed` 7   Configuration (`Config.toml`)\n   123→\n   124→```toml\n   125→[grpc]\n   126→listen_addr = \"0.0.0.0:9002\"\n   127→max_concurrent_streams = 128\n   128→\n   129→[event_bus]\n   130→nats_url = cmd/minting_service/main.rs\n   152→#![forbid(unsafe_code)]\n   153→use minting_service::{config::Config, server::MintingServer};\n   154→use anyhow::Result;\n   155→use tracing::{info, error};\n   156→use tracing_subscriber::EnvFilter;\n   157→\n   158→#[tokio::main]\n   159→async fn main() -> Result<()> {\n   160→    // Initialise logging\n   161→    tracing_subscriber::fmt()\n   162→        .with_env_filter(EnvFilter::from_default_env())\n   163→        .init();\n   164→\n   165→    // Load configuration\n   166→    let cfg = Config::from_env()?;\n   167→    info!(?cfg, \"Launching Minting Service\");\n   168→\n   169→    // Run gRPC server\n   170→    if let Err(e) = MintingServer::run(cfg).await {\n   171→        error!(error = %e, \"MintingService terminated with error\");\n   172→        std::process::exit(1);\n   173→    }\n   174→    Ok(())\n   175→}\n   176→```\n   177→\n   178→Highlights:\n   179→\n   180→* `Strategy Pattern` – `crypto::Signer` trait with curve-specific back-ends (Ed25519/BLS/…)\n   181→* `Proxy Pattern` – contract factory `Factory<x>::deploy_proxy(…)`\n   182→* `Event Driven` – `EventPublisher` & `EventSubscriber` interfaces over `async_nats`\n   183→* `State Machine` – `MintJobState` (Queued → PendingTx → Confirmed | Failed)\n   184→\n   185→---\n   186→\n   187→## 9   Error Semantics\n   188→\n   189→| gRPC code | Meaning                        | Retry |\n   190→| --------- | ----------------------------- | ----- |\n   191→| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | Bad input (metadata URI missing, royalty > 100%) | No |\n   192→| `UNAUTHENTICATED`  | Invalid artist signature / expired nonce         | No |\n   193→| `RESOURCE_EXHAUSTED` | Rate limit exceeded (composer node)          | Yes, after `Retry-After` header |\n   194→| `INTERNAL`         | Unexpected error, check `error_id`              | Maybe |\n   195→\n   196→A structured error (`ErrorBody`) is serialized in `details`.\n   197→\n   198→---\n   199→\n   200→## 10   Observability\n   201→\n   202→* **Metrics** – `OpenTelemetry` exporter (`/metrics` Prometheus scrape)  \n   203→* **Tracing** – Distributed `trace_id` propagated via gRPC metadata  \n   204→* **Health** – `/livez` & `/readyz` HTTP endpoints (serve on `:9003`)  \n   205→* **Structured Logs** – JSON by default, switch to pretty with `RUST_LOG_STYLE=pretty`\n   206→\n   207→---\n   208→\n   209→## 11   Extending the Service\n   210→\n   211→* Add new crypto curves by implementing `crypto::Signer` + registering in `crypto::registry`.\n   212→* Support new NFT standards (e.g., ERC-3525) via the `contract::factory` module.\n   213→* Custom throttling strategies can be injected using the `RateLimiter` trait.\n   214→\n   215→---\n   216→\n   217→## 12   Security Considerations\n   218→\n   219→* Re-entrancy guarded via **OpenZeppelin** templates\n   220→* Nonce replay protection for mint signatures\n   221→* `supply` and `royalty_bps` sanity checks\n   222→* All secrets loaded {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01G16bFS5Vhft9EKbwYkbZMX","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# CanvasChain Symphony – Marketplace Service\n     3→`services/marketplace_service`\n     4→\n     5→The Marketplace Service powers the primary and secondary exchange of multilayer NFTs\n     6→(“Instruments”) on CanvasChain Symphony.  It exposes a gRPC interface for listing,\n     7→bidding, buying, and royalty-settlement, publishes domain events on the mesh-bus,\n     8→and persists state changes in PostgreSQL using Diesel.  \n     9→The service is designed to be **stateless**; business state is maintained in the\n    10→database and propagated through events.  \n    11→High-throughput order-matching is achieved with an _in-memory order-book_ backed\n    12→by **MVCC snapshots** so reads never block writes.\n    13→\n    14→---\n    15→\n    16→## Table of Contents\n    17→1. Responsibilities & Guarantees  \n    18→2. Architecture Diagram  \n    19→3. gRPC Service Definition  \n    20→4. Event-Bus Contracts  \n    21→5. Domain Model (Rust)  \n    22→6. Database Migrations  \n    23→7. Configuration & Environment  \n    24→8. Running Locally  \n    25→9. Integration Test Example  \n    26→10. Extending / Plug-Ins  \n    27→11. Troubleshooting & FAQ  \n    28→\n    29→---\n    30→\n    31→## 1. Responsibilities & Guarantees\n    32→| Capability                     | SLA / Contract                                             |\n    33→|--------------------------------|------------------------------------------------------------|\n    34→| List NFT for sale              | ≤ 150 ms p95 latency                                       |\n    35→| Purchase NFT                   | Atomic ownership + fund transfer on-chain                  |\n    36→| Accept bid / auction settlement| Deterministic matching, replayable via event log           |\n    37→| Royalty stream distribution    | ≤ 30 s settlement delay, idempotent payouts                |\n    38→| Data Consistency               | Strict serializable transactions for write paths           |\n    39→| Observability                  | OpenTelemetry tracing + Prometheus metrics                 |\n    40→\n    41→---\n    42→\n    43→## 2. Architecture Diagram\n    44→```text\n    45→┌───────────────┐      gRPC       ┌────────────────┐\n    46→│   REST Proxy  │ ───────────────►│ MarketplaceSvc │────┐\n    47→└───────────────┘                 └────────────────┘    │\n    48→        ▲                               ▲              │\n    49→        │                               │ Diesel ORM   │\n    50→        │                               ▼              │\n    51→  Web / Mobile                  ┌────────────────┐     │\n    52→    Clients                     │  PostgreSQL     │◄───┘\n    53→                                └────────────────┘\n    54→                                       ▲\n    55→                                       │ Event(bincoded)\n    56→                                       ▼\n    57→                                 ┌───────────────┐\n    58→                                 │ Canvas Bus    │\n    59→                                 └───────────────┘\n    60→```\n    61→\n    62→---\n    63→\n    64→## 3. gRPC Service Definition\n    65→```proto\n    66→// proto/marketplace.proto\n    67→syntax = \"proto3\";\n    68→package canvas.marketplace;\n    69→\n    70→service Marketplace {\n    71→  rpc ListInstrument(ListInstrumentRequest) returns (ListInstrumentResponse);\n    72→  rpc BuyInstrument(BuyInstrumentRequest)   returns (BuyInstrumentResponse);\n    73→  rpc PlaceBid(PlaceBidRequest)             returns (PlaceBidResponse);\n    74→  rpc AcceptBid(AcceptBidRequest)           returns (AcceptBidResponse);\n    75→  rpc StreamEvents(EventsRequest)           returns (stream MarketplaceEvent);\n    76→}\n    77→\n    78→message ListInstrumentRequest {\n    79→  string instrument_id = 1;\n    80→  uint64 price_microunit = 2;\n    81→  uint32 expiration_secs = 3;\n    82→}\n    83→\n    84→message BuyInstrumentRequest  { string listing_id = 1; string buyer_wallet = 2; }\n    85→message BuyInstrumentResponse { string tx_hash     = 1; }\n    86→\n    87→message MarketplaceEvent {\n    88→  oneof event {\n    89→    ListingCreated   listing_created   = 1;\n    90→    InstrumentSold   instrument_sold   = 2;\n    91→    BidPlaced        bid_placed        = 3;\n    92→    BidAccepted      bid_accepted      = 4;\n    93→    RoyaltySettled   royalty_settled   = 5;\n    94→  }\n    95→}\n    96→```\n    97→\n    98→The Rust implementation is generated with `tonic-build` during `build.rs`.\n    99→\n   100→---\n   101→\n   102→## 4. Event-Bus Contracts\n   103→All domain events are published to the **Canvas Mesh-Bus** (NATS JetStream) under the\n   104→subject hierarchy: `marketplace.<event>`.  Payloads are CBOR-encoded for forward-compatibility.\n   105→\n   106→| Subject                      | Schema (Rust Struct)                  |\n   107→|------------------------------|---------------------------------------|\n   108→| marketplace.listing.created  | `ListingCreatedV1`                    |\n   109→| marketplace.instrument.sold  | `InstrumentSoldV1`                    |\n   110→| marketplace.bid.placed       | `BidPlacedV1`                         |\n   111→| marketplace.royalty.settled  | `RoyaltySettledV1`                    |\n   112→\n   113→Event versions are semver’d; newer readers **must** handle unknown fields.\n   114→\n   115→---\n   116→\n   117→## 5. Domain Model (Rust)\n   118→```rust\n   119→use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\n   120→use uuid::Uuid;\n   121→use bigdecimal::BigDecimal;\n   122→\n   123→#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]\n   124→pub enum OrderType {\n   125→    FixedPrice,\n   126→    EnglishAuction,\n   127→    DutchAuction { start_price: BigDecimal, end_price: BigDecimal },\n   128→}\n   129→\n   130→#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]\n   131→pub struct Listing {\n   132→    pub id:           Uuid,\n   133→    pub instrument_id: Uuid,\n   134→    pub seller:       String, // bech32 wallet addr\n   135→    pub order_type:   OrderType,\n   136→    pub price:        BigDecimal,\n   137→    pub expires_at:   DateTime<Utc>,\n   138→    pub created_at:   DateTime<Utc>,\n   139→}\n   140→\n   141→#[derive(Debug, Clone, serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]\n   142→pub struct Bid {\n   143→    pub id:         Uuid,\n   144→    pub listing_id: Uuid,\n   145→    pub bidder:     String,\n   146→    pub amount:     BigDecimal,\n   147→    pub placed_at:  DateTime<Utc>,\n   148→}\n   149→```\n   150→The structs above are mapped to database tables using `diesel::Queryable` &\n   151→`diesel::Insertable` (see `src/schema.rs`).\n   152→\n   153→---\n   154→\n   155→## 6. Database Migrations\n   156→```sql\n   157→-- migrations/2024-05-20-220000_create_listings/up.sql\n   158→CREATE TABLE listings (\n   159→    id              UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n   160→    instrument_id   UUID NOT NULL,\n   161→    seller          TEXT NOT NULL,\n   162→    order_type      JSONB NOT NULL,\n   163→    price           NUMERIC(36, 18) NOT NULL,\n   164→    expires_at      TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n   165→    created_at      TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()\n   166→);\n   167→\n   168→CREATE INDEX idx_listings_instrument ON listings(instrument_id);\n   169→\n   170→-- migrations/2024-05-20-220100_create_bids/up.sql\n   171→CREATE TABLE bids (\n   172→    id           UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n   173→    listing_id   UUID REFERENCES listings(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,\n   174→    bidder       TEXT NOT NULL,\n   175→    amount       NUMERIC(36, 18) NOT NULL,\n   176→    placed_at    TIMESTAMPTZ DEFAULT now()\n   177→);\n   178→\n   179→CREATE INDEX idx_bids_listing ON bids(listing_id);\n   180→```\n   181→\n   182→---\n   183→\n   184→## 7. Configuration & Environment\n   185→| Variable                       | Default          | Description                                   |\n   186→|--------------------------------|------------------|-----------------------------------------------|\n   187→| `MARKETPLACE__PG_URL`          | postgres://...   | PostgreSQL connection URI                     |\n   188→| `MARKETPLACE__BUS_NATS_URL`    | nats://127.0.0.1 | Mesh-Bus broker URL                           |\n   189→| `MARKETPLACE__GRPC_ADDR`       | 0.0.0.0:6001     | gRPC bind address                             |\n   190→| `MARKETPLACE__SIGNING_KEY`     | (none)           | Hex-encoded secp256k1 key for on-chain txs    |\n   191→| `RUST_LOG`                     | info             | Log level (uses `tracing_subscriber`)         |\n   192→\n   193→Configuration is loaded via [`config`](https://docs.rs/config) merging:\n   194→`default.toml` → `$(ENV).toml` → environment variables.\n   195→\n   196→---\n   197→\n   198→## 8. Running Locally\n   199→```bash\n   200→# 1. Start dependencies\n   201→docker compose -f infra/docker-compose.yml up -d postgres nats\n   202→\n   203→# 2. Run database migrations\n   204→cargo install diesel_cli --no-default-features --features postgres\n   205→DATABASE_URL=postgres://canvas:canvas@localhost:5432/canvas diesel migration run\n   206→\n   207→# 3. Start the service with hot-reload\n   208→cargo watch -x 'run --package marketplace_service'\n   209→```\n   210→\n   211→---\n   212→\n   213→## 9. Integration Test Example\n   214→```rust\n   215→#[tokio::test]\n   216→async fn it_lists_and_buys() {\n   217→    let mut client = MarketplaceClient::connect(\"http://[::1]:6001\").await.unwrap();\n   218→\n   219→    // 1. List an NFT\n   220→    let list_res = client.list_instrument(ListInstrumentRequest {\n   221→        instrument_id: Uuid::new_v4().to_string(),\n   222→        price_microunit: 1_000_000u64,\n   223→        expiration_secs: 86_400,\n   224→    }).await.unwrap().into_inner();\n   225→\n   226→    // 2. Buy it\n   227→    let buy_res = client.buy_instrument(BuyInstrumentRequest {\n   228→        listing_id: list_res.listing_id,\n   229→        buyer_wallet: \"wallet1p5...\".into(),\n   230→    }).await.unwrap().into_inner();\n   231→\n   232→    assert!(buy_res.tx_hash.starts_with(\"0x\"));\n   233→}\n   234→```\n   235→\n   236→Run with:\n   237→```bash\n   238→cargo test -p marketplace_service -- --nocapture\n   239→```\n   240→\n   241→---\n   242→\n   243→## 10. Extending / Plug-Ins\n   244→The Marketplace leverages the **Strategy Pattern** for _fee calculation_:\n   245→\n   246→```rust\n   247→pub trait FeePolicy: Send + Sync {\n   248→    fn taker_fee(&self, price: &BigDecimal) -> BigDecimal;\n   249→    fn maker_fee(&self, price: &BigDecimal) -> BigDecimal;\n   250→}\n   251→\n   252→pub struct FlatFee {\n   253→    pub bps: u32 // basis points, e.g. 25 = 0.25%\n   254→}\n   255→\n   256→impl FeePolicy for FlatFee {\n   257→    fn taker_fee(&self, price: &BigDecimal) -> BigDecimal {\n   258→        price * BigDecimal::from(self.bps) / BigDecimal::from(10_000u32)\n   259→    }\n   260→    fn maker_fee(&self, price: &BigDecimal) -> BigDecimal { BigDecimal::zero() }\n   261→}\n   262→```\n   263→Drop your own policy in `src/fees/` and register via Rocket’s `Figment` or\n   264→`feature = \"dynamic-fees\"`.\n   265→\n   266→---\n   267→\n   268→## 11. Troubleshooting & FAQ\n   269→Q: _“I’m seeing `deadlock detected` errors in logs”_  \n   270→A: Ensure you are on PostgreSQL ≥ 14.2; the advisory lock upgrade reduces deadlocks\n   271→in high-concurrency auctions.\n   272→\n   273→Q: _“Events are not replaying on cold start”_  \n   274→A: Verify `JetStream` stream `marketplace.*` is configured with `max_msgs=-1` and\n   275→`retention=workqueue`.\n   276→\n   277→---\n   278→\n   279→© 2024 CanvasChain Foundation – Licensed under Apache-2.0\n   280→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. Peer-to-peer networking (libp2p over QUIC + noise)\n    11→2. Proof-of-Inspiration (PoI) leader election\n    12→3. Transaction / block propagation\n    13→4. State–machine replication (+ snapshotting)\n    14→5. gRPC façade exposing high-level APIs to the remaining micro-services\n    15→6. Event bus bridge (NATS) to fan-out new blocks & governance votes\n    16→\n    17→---\n    18→\n    19→## 🔖 Feature Flags\n    20→\n    21→| Cargo Feature            | Description                                |\n    22→|--------------------------|--------------------------------------------|\n    23→| `in-memory-db`           | Use an ephemeral RocksDB substitute for tests |\n    24→| `bls-signatures`         | Switch from Ed25519 to BLS12-381 curve     |\n    25→| `experimental-post-quantum` | Enable Dilithium (PQ-crypto) signatures |\n    26→\n    27→Activate mutually exclusive curves via:\n    28→\n    29→```bash\n    30→cargo build --release --no-default-features --features bls-signatures\n    31→```\n    32→\n    33→---\n    34→\n    35→## 🗄️ Directory Layout\n    36→\n    37→```\n    38→services/node_service\n    39→├── Cargo.toml                # Crate metadata + feature flags\n    40→├── build.rs                  # Build-time generation of protobuf & WASM\n    41→├── src/\n    42→│   ├── lib.rs                # Public SDK\n    43→│   ├── config.rs             # Strictly-typed TOML/YAML config\n    44→│   ├── grpc/\n    45→│   │   ├── mod.rs            # tonic-generated stubs (auto-generated)\n    46→│   ├── consensus/\n    47→│   │   ├── mod.rs\n    48→│   │   └── proof_of_inspiration.rs\n    49→│   ├── network/\n    50→│   ├── storage/\n    51→│   ├── event_bus/\n    52→│   └── node.rs               # Binary entry-point\n    53→└── README.md                 # You are here\n    54→```\n    55→\n    56→---\n    57→\n    58→## ⚙️ Configuration\n    59→\n    60→`node.yaml` (default look-up paths: `$PWD`, `$HOME/.canvaschain`, `/etc/canvaschain/`)\n    61→\n    62→```yaml\n    63→node_id: \"node-01-mainnet\"\n    64→network:\n    65→  listen_multiaddr: \"/ip4/0.0.0.0/udp/30333/quic-v1\"\n    66→  bootstrap_peers:\n    67→    - \"/dns4/boot.canvaschain.art/udp/30333/quic-v1\"\n    68→consensus:\n    69→  staking_contract: \"0x92…a4\"\n    70→  epoch_duration_sec: 6\n    71→storage:\n    72→  path: \"/var/lib/canvaschain\"\n    73→grpc:\n    74→  bind: \"0.0.0.0:50051\"\n    75→event_bus:\n    76→  nats_url: \"nats://localhost:4222\"\n    77→```\n    78→\n    79→Load it with the public SDK:\n    80→\n    81→```rust\n    82→use node_service::config::NodeConfig;\n    83→\n    84→let cfg = NodeConfig::from_path(\"node.yaml\")?;\n    85→println!(\"Node ID: {}\", cfg.node_id);\n    86→```\n    87→\n    88→---\n    89→\n    90→## 🛰️ gRPC API\n    91→\n    92→All protobuf definitions live in `proto/` and are compiled by `build.rs` using `tonic_build`.\n    93→\n    94→Example service snippet (simplified):\n    95→\n    96→```proto\n    97→service CanvasNode {\n    98→  rpc SubmitTransaction (SignedTransaction) returns (TxReceipt);\n    99→  rpc GetBlockByHash     (BlockHash)        returns (Block);\n   100→  rpc SubscribeEvents    (EventFilter)      returns (stream Event);\n   101→}\n   102→```\n   103→\n   104→### Error Model\n   105→\n   106→Errors map to canonical gRPC codes:\n   107→\n   108→| gRPC Code | Meaning                                    |\n   109→|-----------|--------------------------------------------|\n   110→| `INVALID_ARGUMENT` | Input failed schema or signature validation |\n   111→| `UNAVAILABLE`      | Not currently the leader / syncing |\n   112→| `FAILED_PRECONDITION` | Consensus rejected the tx (e.g. nonce) |\n   113→| `INTERNAL`         | Unexpected panic → logged + Sentry |\n   114→\n   115→---\n   116→\n   117→## 🚀 Quick Start\n   118→\n   119→```bash\n   120→# 1. Clone repo\n   121→git clone https://github.com/canvaschain/symphony.git\n   122→cd symphony/services/node_service\n   123→\n   124→# 2. Build with default Ed25519 curve\n   125→cargo build --release\n   126→\n   127→# 3. Generate keys (stored in ./keys/)\n   128→cargo run --bin keygen ed25519\n   129→\n   130→# 4. Run a standalone dev-node with an in-memory DB and verbose logs\n   131→RUST_LOG=debug cargo run --features in-memory-db\n   132→```\n   133→\n   134→---\n   135→\n   136→## 🧑‍💻 Embedding the SDK\n   137→\n   138→Use the SDK in another micro-service (e.g. composition_service):\n   139→\n   140→```toml\n   141→[dependencies]\n   142→canvas_node_sdk = { path = \"../services/node_service\", default-features = false }\n   143→prost = \"0.12\"\n   144→tonic = { version = \"0.10\", features=[\"transport\"] }\n   145→```\n   146→\n   147→```rust\n   148→use canvas_node_sdk::grpc::canvas_node_client::CanvasNodeClient;\n   149→use tonic::transport::Channel;\n   150→\n   151→#[tokio::main]\n   152→async fn main() -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {\n   153→    let mut client = CanvasNodeClient::connect(\"http://localhost:50051\").await?;\n   154→\n   155→    let tx = build_artist_transaction();\n   156→    let receipt = client.submit_transaction(tx).await?.into_inner();\n   157→\n   158→    println!(\"🎉 Mined in block {}\", receipt.block_number);\n   159→    Ok(())\n   160→}\n   161→```\n   162→\n   163→---\n   164→\n   165→## 🏛️ Consensus in 60 Seconds\n   166→\n   167→1. Epoch is fixed (#blocks = `epoch_duration_sec / slot_time_sec`)\n   168→2. VRF leader randomness is derived from last block hash + validator stake\n   169→3. Committee = top-N validators by stake (configurable)\n   170→4. Fork-choice = GHOST for liveness; blocks must include VRF proof\n   171→5. Finality via BFT overlay (HotStuff variant) after 2 rounds\n   172→\n   173→See `src/consensus/proof_of_inspiration.rs` for full implementation & unit tests.\n   174→\n   175→---\n   176→\n   177→## 📈 Metrics & Observability\n   178→\n   179→Prometheus exporter is enabled by default at `/metrics` (port 9103).\n   180→\n   181→Key metrics:\n   182→• `canvas_block_height`\n   183→• `canvas_peer_count`\n   184→• `canvas_tx_pool_size`\n   185→• `canvas_leader_election_duration_seconds`\n   186→\n   187→Enable Jaeger tracing:\n   188→\n   189→```bash\n   190→JAEGER_ENDPOINT=http://localhost:14268/api/traces RUST_LOG=info,tower_http=debug \\\n   191→    cargo run --release\n   192→```\n   193→\n   194→---\n   195→\n   196→## 🔒 Security Checklist\n   197→\n   198→- AES-GCM sealed storage for private keys (`KeyVault`)\n   199→- All peer traffic encrypted via Noise_KK\n   200→- Strict Protobuf schema validation (`prost-types` + `schemars`)\n   201→- Comprehensive fuzzing harness: `cargo fuzz run tx_deserialize`\n   202→\n   203→---\n   204→\n   205→## 📝 Contributing\n   206→\n   207→1. {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01FagsQSdPYVM3bTC3FuEYD1","type":"tool_result","content":"     1→```markdown\n     2→# CanvasChain Symphony – Staking Service\n     3→\n     4→_**Service directory:** `services/staking_service`_  \n     5→_**Crate name:** `ccs_staking_service`_  \n     6→_**Micro-framework:** [`tonic`](https://github.com/hyperium/tonic) + [`sqlx`](https://github.com/launchbadge/sqlx) + [`tracing`](https://crates.io/crates/tracing)_  \n     7→_**Runtime:** `tokio`_  \n     8→_**Persistent store:** Postgres (with TimescaleDB extension for on-chain metrics)_  \n     9→_**Crypto provider:** Pluggable through the Strategy Pattern (`Ed25519` → `BLS12-381` → `Dilithium`)_  \n    10→\n    11→---\n    12→## 1. Motivation\n    13→\n    14→The _Staking Service_ powers the **Proof-of-Inspiration (PoI)** consensus layer.  \n    15→Participants lock the governance token `$BRUSH` to:\n    16→\n    17→1. Secure the network and participate in artist election rounds.\n    18→2. Earn staking rewards streamed via the DeFi micro-service.\n    19→3. Influence trait evolution probabilities of evolving NFTs.\n    20→\n    21→The service exposes a **gRPC API** and publishes domain events to **NATS** (`staking.events.*`) so that other micro-services (e.g. _governance_, _marketplace_) can react in real-time.\n    22→\n    23→---\n    24→## 2. High-Level Architecture\n    25→\n    26→```text\n    27→┌────────────┐\n    28→│  Clients   │──gRPC─────────────────────┐\n    29→└────────────┘                          ▼\n    30→                                      ┌─────────────────┐\n    31→                                      │ Staking Service │\n    32→                                      └─────────────────┘\n    33→                                     ▲ ▲         ▲ ▲\n    34→     NATS event bus  ────────────────┘ │         │ └─────────────── gRPC  ────────────────┐\n    35→                                       │         │                                      ▼\n    36→                                 ┌───────────────┴──────┐                      ┌──────────────────┐\n    37→                                 │  Governance Service  │                      │  DeFi Service    │\n    38→                                 └──────────────────────┘                      └──────────────────┘\n    39→```\n    40→\n    41→Key internal modules:\n    42→\n    43→* `engine` – state machine handling deposits, unbonding, slashing  \n    44→* `reward` – epoch accounting & APY calculation  \n    45→* `crypto` – pluggable signature verifier (`Strategy` pattern)  \n    46→* `repository` – async Postgres access (write-behind cache)  \n    47→* `api` – gRPC + service layer  \n    48→* `event` – NATS publisher / subscriber  \n    49→\n    50→---\n    51→## 3. gRPC Protocol\n    52→\n    53→> File: `proto/staking/v1/staking.proto`\n    54→\n    55→```proto\n    56→syntax = \"proto3\";\n    57→\n    58→package staking.v1;\n    59→\n    60→import \"google/protobuf/timestamp.proto\";\n    61→\n    62→service StakingService {\n    63→  rpc Stake   (StakeRequest)   returns (StakeResponse);\n    64→  rpc Unstake (UnstakeRequest) returns (UnstakeResponse);\n    65→  rpc Slash   (SlashRequest)   returns (SlashResponse);\n    66→  rpc Query   (QueryRequest)   returns (QueryResponse);\n    67→}\n    68→\n    69→message StakeRequest {\n    70→  string account_id = 1;\n    71→  uint64 amount     = 2; // in minimal units\n    72→}\n    73→\n    74→message StakeResponse {\n    75→  string tx_id = 1;\n    76→ 91→  uint64 amount       = 2;\n    92→  string reason       = 3;\n    93→}\n    94→\n    95→message SlashResponse {\n    96→  string tx_id = 1;\n    97→}\n    98→\n    99→message QueryRequest {\n   100→  string account_id = 1;\n   101→}\n   102→\n   103→message QueryResponse {\n   104→  uint64 staked       = 1;\n   105→  uint64 pending      = 2;\n   106→  uint64 reward_accum = 3;\n   107→}\n   108→```\n   109→\n   110→Regenerate Rust stubs:\n   111→\n   112→```bash\n   113→cargo xtask proto-gen\n   114→```\n   115→\n   116→---\n   117→## 4. Core Rust Types\n   118→\n   119→> File: `src/domain.rs`\n   120→\n   121→```rust\n   122→use chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\n   123→use rust_decimal::Decimal;\n   124→use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n   125→use uuid::Uuid;\n   126→\n   127→/// Unique identifier for an on-chain token account (alias to `uuid` for now).\n   128→pub type AccountId = Uuid;\n   129→\n   130→/// High-precision monetary type (matches on-chain 1e18 `u128`).\n   131→pub type Amount = Decimal;\n   132→\n   133→/// Internal representation of a staking position.\n   134→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\n   135→pub struct StakePosition {\n   136→    pub id: Uuid,\n   137→    pub account_id: AccountId,\n   138→    pub amount: Amount,\n   139→    pub bonded_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n   140→    pub status: PositionStatus,\n   141→}\n   142→\n   143→#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize, Eq, PartialEq)]\n   144→pub enum PositionStatus {\n   145→    Bonded,\n   146→    Unbonding { completes_at: DateTime<Utc> },\n   147→    Slashed { reason: String },\n   148→}\n   149→```\n   150→\n   151→---\n   152→## 5. Staking Engine (State Machine)\n   153→\n   154→> File: `src/engine/mod.rs`\n   155→\n   156→```rust\n   157→//! FINITE-STATE-MACHINE IMPLEMENTATION\n   158→//! Bonded ──Unbond──► Unbonding ──Timeout──► Withdrawn\n   159→//!   │                      │\n   160→//!   └──────Slash──────────┘\n   161→\n   162→use crate::domain::{Amount, PositionStatus, StakePosition};\n   163→use crate::error::{EngineError, Result};\n   164→use chrono::{Duration, Utc};\n   165→use uuid::Uuid;\n   166→\n   167→pub struct Engine {\n   168→    unbonding_period: Duration,\n   169→}\n   170→\n   171→impl Engine {\n   172→    pub fn new(unbonding_period: Duration) -> Self {\n   173→        Self { unbonding_period }\n   174→    }\n   175→\n   176→    /// Adds stake to the ledger, returning the created `StakePosition`.\n   177→    pub fn stake(&self, account_id: Uuid, amount: Amount) -> Result<StakePosition> {\n   178→        if amount <= Amount::ZERO {\n   179→            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAmount);\n   180→        }\n   181→\n   182→        Ok(StakePosition {\n   183→            id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n   184→            account_id,\n   185→            amount,\n   186→            bonded_at: Utc::now(),\n   187→            status: PositionStatus::Bonded,\n   188→        })\n   189→    }\n   190→\n   191→    /// Moves a bonded position into `Unbonding`.\n   192→    pub fn unbond(&self, mut pos: StakePosition, req_amount: Amount) -> Result<StakePosition> {\n   193→        if pos.status != PositionStatus::Bonded {\n   194→            return Err(EngineError::InvalidState);\n   195→        }\n   196→        if req_amount != pos.amount {\n   197→            return Err(EngineError::PartialUnbondUnsupported);\n   198→        }\n   199→\n   200→        pos.status = PositionStatus::Unbonding {\n   201→            completes_at: Utc::now() + self.unbonding_period,\n   202→        };\n   203→        Ok(pos)\n   204→    }\n   205→\n   206→    /// Applies slash penalty and moves position into `Slashed`.\n   207→    pub fn slash(&self, mut pos: StakePosition, reason: String) -> Result<StakePosition> {\n   208→        if matches!(pos.status, PositionStatus::Slashed { .. }) {\n   209→            return Err(EngineError::InvalidState);\n   210→        }\n   211→        pos.amount = Amount::ZERO;\n   212→        pos.status = PositionStatus::Slashed { reason };\n   213→        Ok(pos)\n   214→    }\n   215→}\n   216→```\n   217→\n   218→---\n   219→## 6. Error Handling\n   220→\n   221→> File: `src/error.rs`\n   222→\n   223→```rust\n   224→use thiserror::Error;\n   225→\n   226→pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, EngineError>;\n   227→\n   228→#[derive(Error, Debug)]\n   229→pub enum EngineError {\n   230→    #[error(\"invalid amount\")]\n   231→    InvalidAmount,\n   232→    #[error(\"state transition not allowed\")]\n   233→    InvalidState,\n   234→    #[error(\"partial unbond not supported yet\")]\n   235→    PartialUnbondUnsupported,\n   236→    #[error(\"database error: {0}\")]\n   237→    Db(#[from] sqlx::Error),\n   238→    #[error(\"cryptography error: {0}\")]\n   239→    Crypto(String),\n   240→}\n   241→```\n   242→\n   243→---\n   244→## 7. Service Layer\n   245→\n   246→> File: `src/api/service.rs`\n   247→\n   248→```rust\n   249→use crate::domain::*;\n   250→use crate::engine::Engine;\n   251→use crate::error::EngineError;\n   252→use crate::repository::StakeRepository;\n   253→use staking::v1::{\n   254→    staking_service_server::StakingService, QueryRequest, QueryResponse, SlashRequest,\n   255→    SlashResponse, StakeRequest, StakeResponse, UnstakeRequest, UnstakeResponse,\n   256→};\n   257→use tonic::{Request, Response, Status};\n   258→use tracing::{info_span, Instrument};\n   259→\n   260→pub struct StakingSvc<R: StakeRepository> {\n   261→    engine: Engine,\n   262→    repo: R,\n   263→}\n   264→\n   265→#[tonic::async_trait]\n   266→impl<R> StakingService for StakingSvc<R>\n   267→where\n   268→    R: StakeRepository + Send + Sync + 'static,\n   269→{\n   270→    async fn stake(\n   271→        &self,\n   272→        req: Request<StakeRequest>,\n   273→    ) -> Result<Response<StakeResponse>, Status> {\n   274→        let span = info_span!(\"stake\", ?req);\n   275→        async move {\n   276→            let body = req.into_inner();\n   277→            let account_id = body.account_id.parse().map_err(bad_uuid)?;\n   278→            let amount = Amount::from(body.amount);\n   279→\n   280→            let pos = self.engine.stake(account_id, amount).map_err(to_status)?;\n   281→            self.repo.insert(&pos).await.map_err(to_status)?;\n   282→\n   283→            Ok(Response::new(StakeResponse {\n   284→                tx_id: pos.id.to_string(),\n   285→                bonded_at: Some(pos.bonded_at.into()),\n   286→            }))\n   287→        }\n   288→        .instrument(span)\n   289→        .await\n   290→    }\n   291→\n   292→    async fn unstake(\n   293→        &self,\n   294→        req: Request<UnstakeRequest>,\n   295→    ) -> Result<Response<UnstakeResponse>, Status> {\n   296→        let span = info_span!(\"unstake\", ?req);\n   297→        async move {\n   298→            let body = req.into_inner();\n   299→            let pos = self.repo.find_active(&body.account_id).await.map_err(to_status)?;\n   300→\n   301→            let updated = self\n   302→                .engine\n   303→                .unbond(pos, Amount::from(body.amount))\n   304→                .map_err(to_status)?;\n   305→            self.repo.update(&updated).await.map_err(to_status)?;\n   306→\n   307→            Ok(Response::new(UnstakeResponse {\n   308→                tx_id: updated.id.to_string(),\n   309→                unbonding_complete_at: match updated.status {\n   310→                    PositionStatus::Unbonding { completes_at } => Some(completes_at.into()),\n   311→                    _ => None,\n   312→                },\n   313→            }))\n   314→        }\n   315→        .instrument(span)\n   316→        .await\n   317→    }\n   318→\n   319→    async fn slash(\n   320→        &self,\n   321→        req: Request<SlashRequest>,\n   322→    ) -> Result<Response<SlashResponse>, Status> {\n   323→        let span = info_span!(\"slash\", ?req);\n   324→        async move {\n   325→            let body = req.into_inner();\n   326→            let pos = self.repo.find_validator(&body.validator_id).await.map_err(to_status)?;\n   327→\n   328→            let updated = self.engine.slash(pos, body.reason).map_err(to_status)?;\n   329→            self.repo.update(&updated).await.map_err(to_status)?;\n   330→\n   331→            Ok(Response::new(SlashResponse {\n   332→                tx_id: updated.id.to_string(),\n   333→            }))\n   334→        }\n   335→        .instrument(span)\n   336→        .await\n   337→    }\n   338→\n   339→    async fn query(\n   340→        &self,\n   341→        req: Request<QueryRequest>,\n   342→    ) -> Result<Response<QueryResponse>, Status> {\n   343→        let span = info_span!(\"query\", ?req);\n   344→        async move {\n   345→            let body = req.into_inner();\n   346→            let positions = self.repo.list_by_account(&body.account_id).await.map_err(to_status)?;\n   347→            let staked = positions.iter().map(|p| p.amount).sum::<Amount>();\n   348→\n   349→            Ok(Response::new(QueryResponse {\n   350→                staked: staked.mantissa() as u64,\n   351→                pending: 0,\n   352→                reward_accum: 0,\n   353→            }))\n   354→        }\n   355→        .instrument(span)\n   356→        .await\n   357→    }\n   358→}\n   359→\n   360→fn bad_uuid(_: uuid::Error) -> Status {\n   361→    Status::invalid_argument(\"bad account_id\")\n   362→}\n   363→\n   364→fn to_status(err: EngineError) -> Status {\n   365→    Status::internal(err.to_string())\n   366→}\n   367→```\n   368→\n   369→---\n   370→## 8. Prerequisites\n     8→\n     9→```bash\n    10→# 🐡 Rust toolchain (latest stable + wasm32 target)\n    11→curl https://sh.rustup.rs -sSf | sh\n    12→rustup target add wasm32-unknown-unknown\n    13→\n    14→# 📦 Protocol Buffers compiler\n    15→brew install protobuf       # macOS\n    16→apt-get install -y protobuf-compiler # Debian/Ubuntu\n    17→\n    18→# 🐳 Docker & Docker-Compose (for quick-start orchestration)\n    19→brew install --cask docker  # macOS\n    20→apt-get install -y docker.io docker-compose # Debian/Ubuntu\n    21→\n    22→# 🗝️  OpenSSL development headers (crypto back-ends & TLS)\n    23→apt-get install -y libssl-dev pkg-config\n    24→```\n    25→\n    26→> **Minimum Rust version:** `1.71.0` – validated via CI.\n    27→\n    28→---\n    29→\n    30→## 2. Repository Layout\n    31→\n    32→```text\n    33→.\n    34→├── docs/                    # You are here\n    35→├── proto/                   # *.proto files (gRPC interface)\n    36→├── services/                # 10 micro-services (each independent binary crate)\n    37→│   ├── composer/\n    38→│   ├── conductor/\n    39→│   ├── curator/\n    40→│   └── …\n    41→├── gateway/                 # API gateway (GraphQL + REST façade)\n    42→├── pallets/                 # Substrate runtime modules (WASM)\n    43→├── tooling/                 # Developer CLI & code-gen helpers\n    44→├── examples/                # End-to-end demos + integration tests\n    45→└── Cargo.toml               # Workspace manifest\n    46→```\n    47→\n    48→---\n    49→\n    50→## 3. One-Command Quick-Start\n    51→\n    52→Spin up a fully-fledged local cluster:\n    53→\n    54→```bash\n    55→make devnet\n    56→```\n    57→\n    58→`make devnet` performs the following under the hood:\n    59→\n    60→1. Generates gRPC bindings (`prost`).\n    61→2. Builds all Rust binaries in `release` mode.\n    62→3. Stitches services together with `docker-compose.override.yml`.\n    63→4. Boots a 4-validator Substrate network with `Proof-of-Inspiration`.\n    64→5. Exposes:\n    65→   - gRPC: `localhost:50051`\n    66→   - GraphQL: `http://localhost:8080/graphql`\n    67→   - Polkadot-JS UI: `http://localhost:8000`\n    68→\n    69→Stop and remove containers:\n    70→\n    71→```bash\n    72→make down     # or: docker-compose down -v\n    73→```\n    74→\n    75→---\n    76→\n    77→## 4. Manual Build (Non-Docker)\n    78→\n    79→### 4.1 Compile All Binaries\n    80→\n    81→```bash\n    82→cargo build --workspace --release\n    83→```\n    84→\n    85→Artifacts land in `target/release/`.\n    86→\n    87→### 4.2 Launch Core Services\n    88→\n    89→In separate terminals:\n    90→\n    91→```bash\n    92→# ① Blockchain node (Substrate)\n    93→./target/release/canvas-node --dev --tmp --rpc-methods=Unsafe\n    94→\n    95→# ② Event Bus (NATS)\n    96→nats-server -DV\n    97→\n    98→# ③ Composer Microservice\n    99→RUST_LOG=info \\\n   100→COMPOSER_NODE_URL=127.0.0.1:9944 \\\n   101→cargo run -p composer --release\n   102→```\n   103→\n   104→Use `just` recipes (`just --list`) for shortcuts.\n   105→\n   106→---\n   107→\n   108→## 5. Regenerating gRPC Stubs\n   109→\n   110→Whenever you modify files under `proto/`:\n   111→\n   112→```bash\n   113→protoc \\\n   114→  --proto_path=proto \\\n   115→  --prost_out=services \\\n   116→  --tonic_out=services \\\n   117→  proto/**/*.proto\n   118→```\n   119→\n   120→Or simply:\n   121→\n   122→```bash\n   123→make proto\n   124→```\n   125→\n   126→The workspace makes heavy use of [tonic-build] with Prost, mapping Protobuf well-known types to `chrono` and `uuid` seamlessly.\n   127→\n   128→---\n   129→\n   130→## 6. End-to-End Smoke Test\n   131→\n   132→```bash\n   133→cargo test --workspace --all-features\n   134→```\n   135→\n   136→Expect output similar to:\n   137→\n   138→```\n   139→running 192 tests\n   140→test composer::tests::mint_flow ... ok\n   141→test defi::tests::apy_math ... ok\n   142→…\n   143→```\n   144→\n   145→---\n   146→\n   147→## 7. Creating & Deploying a Custom NFT Instrument\n   148→\n   149→Below is a minimal factory template:\n   150→\n   151→```rust\n   152→// pallets/instrument-factory/src/lib.rs\n   153→pub struct GlitchSynthFactory;\n   154→\n   155→impl InstrumentFactory for GlitchSynthFactory {\n   156→    fn instantiate(config: InstrumentConfig) -> Result<InstrumentId, DispatchError> {\n   157→        let id = Self::reserve_id()?;\n   158→        <Instruments<T>>::insert(\n   159→            id,\n   160→            Instrument {\n   161→                owner: config.owner,\n   162→                curve: CurveKind::Bls12381,\n   163→                metadata: config.metadata,\n   164→            },\n   165→        );\n   166→        Self::deposit_event(Event::InstrumentCreated { id, owner: config.owner });\n   167→        Ok(id)\n   168→    }\n   169→}\n   170→```\n   171→\n   172→Compile to WASM and deploy:\n   173→\n   174→```bash\n   175→cargo +nightly contract build -p instrument-factory\n   176→canvas-cli wasm deploy \\\n   177→  --wasm target/ink/instrument-factory.wasm \\\n   178→  --rpc-url ws://127.0.0.1:9944 \\\n   179→  --suri //Alice\n   180→```\n   181→\n   182→---\n   183→\n   184→## 8. Advanced: Swapping Cryptographic Strategies\n   185→\n   186→All crypto operations are abstracted behind the `SignatureScheme` trait:\n   187→\n   188→```rust\n   189→pub trait SignatureScheme {\n   190→    type Public;\n   191→    type Private;\n   192→    type Signature;\n   193→    fn sign(msg: &[u8], sk: &Self::Private) -> Self::Signature;\n   194→    fn verify(msg: &[u8], sig: &Self::Signature, pk: &Self::Public) -> bool;\n   195→}\n   196→\n   197→pub enum DefaultScheme {\n   198→    Ed25519,\n   199→}\n   200→\n   201→impl SignatureScheme for DefaultScheme { … }\n   202→```\n   203→\n   204→Compile-time selection:\n   205→\n   206→```bash\n   207→# Ed25519 (default)\n   208→cargo build --features curve-ed25519\n   209→\n   210→# BLS12-381 (threshold support)\n   211→cargo build --features curve-bls12-381\n   212→\n   213→# XMSS (post-quantum)\n   214→cargo build --features curve-xmss\n   215→```\n   216→\n   217→---\n   218→\n   219→## 9. Environment Variables\n   220→\n   221→| Variable                    | Purpose                                   | Default                |\n   222→|-----------------------------|-------------------------------------------|------------------------|\n   223→| `NODE_RPC_URL`             | Rust node RPC endpoint                    | `ws://localhost:9944` |\n   224→| `COMPOSER_SEED`            | sr25519 seed phrase for composer node     | `//Alice`             |\n   225→| `EVENT_BUS_URL`            | NATS server address                       | `nats://127.0.0.1:4222`|\n   226→| `DATABASE_URL`             | Postgres connection string                | `postgres://canvas:canvas@localhost/canvas` |\n   227→| `RUST_LOG`                 | Log level (`info`, `debug`, `trace`)      | `info`                |\n   228→\n   229→Load via direnv or `.env`:\n   230→\n   231→```dotenv\n   232→NODE_RPC_URL=ws://127.0.0.1:9944\n   233→COMPOSER_SEED=bottom drive obey lake curtain smoke basket hold race lonely fit walk\n   234→RUST_LOG=trace\n   235→```\n   236→\n   237→---\n   238→\n   239→## 10. Troubleshooting\n   240→\n   241→| Symptom                                | Fix |\n   242→|----------------------------------------|-----|\n   243→| `error: linking with cc failed`        | Ensure `libssl-dev` & `clang` are installed |\n   244→| gRPC returns `UNIMPLEMENTED`           | Version mismatch – regenerate proto stubs |\n   245→| `PoolTimedOut` from `sqlx`             | Increase `MAX_CONNECTIONS` or use psql ≥ 14 |\n   246→| NFT not minting, tx stuck in `Ready`   | Check that `composer` service is elected (see logs) |\n   247→\n   248→---\n   249→\n   250→## 11. Contribution & Code Style\n   251→\n   252→1. `rustfmt --check` enforced via CI.\n   253→2. Clippy `-D warnings`.\n   254→3. Conventional Commits (`feat:`, `fix:`, `chore:`…).\n   255→\n   256→Create feature branches off `develop`, then open PRs targeting `develop`.\n   257→\n   258→---\n   259→\n   260→## 12. Glossary\n   261→\n   262→- **Proof-of-Inspiration:** Hybrid PoS + VRF consensus selecting creatives to push on-chain art updates.\n   263→- **Instrument:** A composable NFT template (e.g., synthesizer, brush stroke engine).\n   264→- **Movement:** Discrete generative art variation emitted by a composer node.\n   265→- **Royalty Stream:** Continuous, time-based payout leveraging Superfluid-like DeFi contracts.\n   266→\n   267→---\n   268→\n   269→Happy hacking — _unleash your inner composer!_ 🎨🎶\n   270→```\n\n<system-reminder>\nWhenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. ARCHITECTURE DECISION RECORDS (ADRs)\n\n#### **ADR 001: Monorepo for Microservices** (Approved, 2024-06-17)\n- **Decision**: Single Git repository with Cargo workspaces instead of 10 independent repos\n- **Rationale**:\n  - Atomic protocol upgrades across services\n  - Unified tooling and linting (`cargo fmt`, `clippy`)\n  - Shared CI cache (~55% build time reduction)\n  - Consistent semantic versioning per crate\n- **Structure**: `crates/` for libraries, `services/` for binaries\n- **Tradeoffs**: Larger repo (~2 GB projected), potential merge conflicts mitigated by conventional commits\n\n#### **ADR 002: gRPC + NATS JetStream** (Accepted, 2023-11-12)\n- **gRPC for Sync RPC**: Service-to-service request/response via `tonic`\n  - Binary protobuf encoding (efficient for large art layers)\n  - Bidirectional streaming for real-time auctions\n  - mTLS security, tower middlewares\n- **NATS JetStream for Async Events**: Publish/subscribe pattern\n  - Subject hierarchy: `domain.context.version.resource.event` (e.g., `nft.composer.v1.artwork.minted`)\n  - Exactly-once semantics for critical events, fire-and-forget for telemetry\n  - Transactional Outbox pattern for at-least-once delivery\n- **Security**: JWT claims propagated via headers, subject ACLs, replay protection via nonces\n\n#### **ADR 003: Pluggable Cryptography Strategy** (Proposed, 2024-05-05)\n- **Pattern**: `CryptoSuite` trait abstraction allowing runtime curve selection\n- **Supported Curves**:\n  - Ed25519 (default, mainstream wallets)\n  - BLS12-381 (threshold signatures, aggregation)\n  - Dilithium (post-quantum, experimental)\n  - Falcon (post-quantum, future)\n- **Implementation**: No-std compatible, crate per algorithm, registry-based loading at startup\n- **Governance**: On-chain allow-list enables/disables suites without hard-forks\n- **Security**: Constant-time verification, key zeroization via `zeroize` crate\n\n---\n\n### 6. CORE ARCHITECTURE & DESIGN PATTERNS\n\n#### **High-Level Data Flow**\n```\n┌─────────────────┐\n│  Composer Node  │ (PoI consensus, VRF elected leader)\n│  (node_service) │\n└────────┬────────┘\n         │ publishes: \"movement.finalized\" event\n         ▼\n    ┌────────────────┐\n    │  Event Bus     │ (NATS JetStream)\n    │  (pub/sub)     │\n    └────────┬───────┘\n             │\n    ┌────────┴──────────────────┬──────────────────┐\n    │                           │                  │\n    ▼                           ▼                  ▼\n┌──────────┐            ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐\n│ Minting  │ gRPC       │ Marketplace  │     │ Royalty      │\n│ Service  │ calls      │ Service      │     │ Service      │\n│          │◄──────────►│              │     │              │\n└──────────┘ (sync)     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘\n```\n\n#### **Key Design Patterns**\n1. **Factory Pattern**: NFT instrument deployment via factory contracts\n2. **Proxy Pattern**: Upgradeable smart contract proxies for gas optimization\n3. **Strategy Pattern**: Pluggable crypto curves, fee policies, burn policies\n4. **Observer Pattern**: Event listeners on NFT evolution, governance votes\n5. **State Machine**: NFT lifecycle (Seeded → Dormant → Flourishing → Fragmented → Archived)\n6. **CQRS**: Command/query separation in marketplace (write to PostgreSQL, read from MVCC snapshots)\n7. **Event Sourcing**: Append-only event log for auditability\n\n---\n\n### 7. NFT STATE MACHINE\n\nFrom `/docs/nft_evolution.md`:\n\n```\nSeeded (newly minted, base DNA + creator signature)\n  ↓\nDormant (frozen to preserve snapshot)\n  ↓\nFlourishing (fully mutable, traits append/replace/decay)\n  ├→ Evolve* (traits update, once per epoch)\n  ├→ Fragmented (split into derivative pieces for remixing)\n  │  └→ Merge (recombine fragmented tokens)\n  └→ Archived (finalized, no transitions possible)\n```\n\n**gRPC Evolution APIs** (from `nft_evolution.proto`):\n- `Inspire(nft_id, vrf_proof)` - Trigger evolution after PoI composer election\n- `Freeze(nft_id, signatures)` - Lock from further changes\n- `Evolve(nft_id, patch)` - Apply layer mutations\n- `Split(nft_id, parts)` - Create fractional pieces\n- `Merge(child_ids[])` - Recombine fragments\n- `Burn(nft_id)` - Irreversible finalization (pluggable burn policy)\n\n---\n\n### 8. PROOF-OF-INSPIRATION (PoI) CONSENSUS\n\nFrom `/docs/proof_of_inspiration.md`:\n\n**Formula for Composer Selection**:\n```\neffective_stake(validator) = \n    base_stake +\n    Σ delegator_balances × loyalty_multiplier +\n    inspiration_score × β\n```\n\n**VRF Election Process**:\n1. Seed = H(epoch_randomness || round_no)\n2. VRF output ∈ [0, 2²⁵⁶) evaluated by each validator\n3. Threshold = 2²⁵⁶ × (effective_stake / total_stake)\n4. If VRF_output < threshold → **Validator wins composer slot**\n\n**Slashing & Rewards**:\n- Double movement (same round): 5% slash + 1 epoch freeze\n- Invalid VRF proof: 100% block reward burned\n- Inactivity (≥256 rounds): 1% slash\n- Block rewards: 90% to composer + delegators, 10% to Inspiration Fund\n\n---\n\n### 9. GOVERNANCE PROTOCOL\n\nFrom `/docs/governance_protocol.md`:\n\n**Token**: `$BRUSH` (ERC-20-compatible, stake-weighted voting)\n\n**Proposal Lifecycle**:\n```\nDraft (∞) → Review (48h) → Voting (72h) → Enactment (24h) → Executed/Rejected\n```\n\n**Conviction Voting** (Polkadot-style):\n- 0 blocks: ×1 multiplier\n- 80k blocks (~1 week): ×2\n- 320k blocks: ×3\n- 1.28M blocks: ×4\n\n**Smart Contract** (ink! on Substrate):\n- `submit_proposal(ipfs_hash)` - Requires 0.01 BRUSH bond\n- `vote(proposal_id, kind, weight)` - Cast vote with conviction\n- `advance_stage()` - Progress through lifecycle\n- `veto_upgrade()` - ≥30% stake can abort scheduled upgrades\n\n---\n\n### 10. COMMUNICATION & OBSERVABILITY\n\n**Inter-Service Communication**:\n- **gRPC (sync)**: Request/response over Tonic with TLS\n- **NATS JetStream (async)**: Event pub/sub with durable consumers\n- **Transactional Outbox**: Dual-write hazard prevention between DB and event bus\n\n**Observability Stack** (from `docker-compose.yml`):\n- **Logs**: OpenTelemetry + Jaeger tracing\n- **Metrics**: Prometheus exporter on `/metrics` (port 8080 on each service)\n- **Dashboards**: Grafana (port 3000)\n- **Traces**: Tempo (port 4317)\n\n**Health Checks**:\n- All services expose `/health` REST endpoint\n- Docker compose HEALTHCHECK every 10s with 5 retries\n\n---\n\n### 11. Comprehensive documentation\n│   ├── adr/                  # Architecture Decision Records (3 ADRs)\n│   │   ├── 001-monorepo-for-microservices.md\n│   │   ├── 002-grpc-and-event-bus.md\n│   │   ├── 003-pluggable-cryptography.md\n│   │   └── api/              # API documentation\n│   ├── architecture.md       # Detailed technical architecture guide\n│   ├── nft_evolution.md      # NFT state machine & lifecycle\n│   ├── governance_protocol.md # On-chain governance specs\n│   ├── proof_of_inspiration.md # PoI consensus deep-dive\n│   ├── setup_guide.md        # Development environment setup\n│   └── README.md             # Quick reference guide\n│\n├── config/                    # Configuration templates\n├── scripts/                   # Build & deployment utilities\n├── Cargo.toml               # Root workspace manifest\n├── docker-compose.yml       # Full stack orchestration\n└── CONTRIBUTING.md & README.md\n```\n\n---\n\n### 2. ALL SERVICES IN `/services/` DIRECTORY\n\n| Service | Port | Primary Responsibility | Key Patterns |\n|---------|------|------------------------|--------------|\n| **api_gateway** | 8080/REST | REST-to-gRPC bridge for clients | Gateway pattern, API aggregation |\n| **governance_service** | 7007 | On-chain proposal voting, DAO governance | State machine, gRPC server |\n| **marketplace_service** | 7004 | NFT listing, bidding, order-book matching | MVCC snapshots, CQRS, Diesel ORM |\n| **minting_service** | 7002 | NFT factory, proxy contract deployment | Factory pattern, Proxy pattern |\n| **node_service** | 50051 | Consensus node, PoI leader election, blockchain core | DPoS, VRF consensus, P2P networking |\n| **royalty_service** | 7005 | Real-time royalty distribution (Superfluid-like) | Event-driven streaming, continuous settlement |\n| **staking_service** | 7008 | Lock tokens for PoI participation, earn rewards | State machine (Bonded→Unbonding→Slashed), DeFi |\n| **wallet_service** | 7006 | Key management, signature verification, auth | Strategy pattern (multi-curve), cryptographic bridge |\n\n---\n\n### 3. SHARED CRATES IN `/crates/` DIRECTORY\n\n| Crate | Purpose | Key Exports |\n|-------|---------|------------|\n| **ccs_common** | Shared utilities across all services | Error types, logging macros, common domain models |\n| **ccs_consensus** | Proof-of-Inspiration (PoI) algorithm | `ComposerSelector`, VRF verification, epoch scheduling |\n| **ccs_crypto** | Pluggable cryptography strategy | `SigningStrategy` trait, Ed25519/BLS12-381/Dilithium support, registry |\n| **ccs_db** | Database abstraction layer | PostgreSQL async client, migrations, query builders |\n| **ccs_proto** | Protocol Buffer definitions | All `.proto` files compiled to Rust via `prost` & `tonic_build` |\n\n---\n\n### 4. KEY PROTO FILES & gRPC SERVICES\n\nLocated in `/app/project/crates/ccs_proto/proto/`:\n\n1. **common.proto** (219 lines)\n   - Shared enums: `CurveType`, `ErrorCode`, `NetworkEnvironment`\n   - Value objects: `Address`, `Uuid`, `ChainTimestamp`, `BigUInt`, `BigInt`\n   - Crypto primitives: `PublicKey` (multi-curve), `Signature` (detached)\n   - Envelope structures: `RequestHeader`, `ResponseEnvelope`, `ResponseStatus`\n\n2. **governance.proto** (6,991 bytes)\n   - Service: `Governance` with RPC methods: `SubmitProposal`, `CastVote`, `Subscribe`\n   - Message types: `ProposalStage` (Draft→Review→Voting→Enactment→Executed/Rejected)\n   - Voting: `VoteKind` (Aye, Nay, Abstain), conviction-weighted voting\n\n3. **marketplace.proto** (8,241 bytes)\n   - Service: `Marketplace` with RPC: `ListInstrument`, `BuyInstrument`, `PlaceBid`, `AcceptBid`, `StreamEvents`\n   - Order types: `FixedPrice`, `EnglishAuction`, `DutchAuction`\n   - Events: `ListingCreated`, `InstrumentSold`, `BidPlaced`, `RoyaltySettled`\n\n4. **minting.proto** (7,156 bytes)\n   - Service: `MintingService` with RPC: `Mint`, `MintStream`, `Health`\n   - Messages: `MintRequest` (artist_id, asset_uri, royalty_bps), `MintResponse` (token_id, tx_hash, status)\n   - Curve support via `CurveType` enum\n\n5. **node.proto** (6,456 bytes)\n   - Service: `CanvasNode` with RPC: `SubmitTransaction`, `GetBlockByHash`, `SubscribeEvents`\n   - Message types for Tx receipts, blocks, events\n\n6. **staking.proto** (6,196 bytes)\n   - Service: `StakingService` with RPC: `Stake`, `Unstake`, `Slash`, `Query`\n   - Messages: `StakeRequest/Response`, `UnstakeRequest/Response`, `SlashRequest/Response`\n\n7. **wallet.proto** (7,410 bytes)\n   - Signature verification, wallet operations, account management\n\n---\n\n### 5. ARCHITECTURE DECISION RECORDS (ADRs)\n\n#### **ADR 001: Monorepo for Microservices** (Approved, 2024-06-17)\n- **Decision**: Single Git repository with Cargo workspaces instead of 10 independent repos\n- **Rationale**:\n  - Atomic protocol upgrades across services\n  - Unified tooling and linting (`cargo fmt`, `clippy`)\n  - Shared CI cache (~55% build time reduction)\n  - Consistent semantic versioning per crate\n- **Structure**: `crates/` for libraries, `services/` for binaries\n- **Tradeoffs**: Larger repo (~2 GB projected), potential merge conflicts mitigated by conventional commits\n\n#### **ADR 002: gRPC + NATS JetStream** (Accepted, 2023-11-12)\n- **gRPC for Sync RPC**: Service-to-service request/response via `tonic`\n  - Binary protobuf encoding (efficient for large art layers)\n  - Bidirectional streaming for real-time auctions\n  - mTLS security, tower middlewares\n- **NATS JetStream for Async Events**: Publish/subscribe pattern\n  - Subject hierarchy: `domain.context.version.resource.event` (e.g., `nft.composer.v1.artwork.minted`)\n  - Exactly-once semantics for critical events, fire-and-forget for telemetry\n  - Transactional Outbox pattern for at-least-once delivery\n- **Security**: JWT claims propagated via headers, subject ACLs, replay protection via nonces\n\n#### **ADR 003: Pluggable Cryptography Strategy** (Proposed, 2024-05-05)\n- **Pattern**: `CryptoSuite` trait abstraction allowing runtime curve selection\n- **Supported Curves**:\n  - Ed25519 (default, mainstream wallets)\n  - BLS12-381 (threshold signatures, aggregation)\n  - Dilithium (post-quantum, experimental)\n  - Falcon (post-quantum, future)\n- **Implementation**: No-std compatible, crate per algorithm, registry-based loading at startup\n- **Governance**: On-chain allow-list enables/disables suites without hard-forks\n- **Security**: Constant-time verification, key zeroization via `zeroize` crate\n\n---\n\n### 6. CORE ARCHITECTURE & DESIGN PATTERNS\n\n#### **High-Level Data Flow**\n```\n┌─────────────────┐\n│  Composer Node  │ (PoI consensus, VRF elected leader)\n│  (node_service) │\n└────────┬────────┘\n         │ publishes: \"movement.finalized\" event\n         ▼\n    ┌────────────────┐\n    │  Event Bus     │ (NATS JetStream)\n    │  (pub/sub)     │\n    └────────┬───────┘\n             │\n    ┌────────┴──────────────────┬──────────────────┐\n    │                           │                  │\n    ▼                           ▼                  ▼\n┌──────────┐            ┌──────────────┐     ┌──────────────┐\n│ Minting  │ gRPC       │ Marketplace  │     │ Royalty      │\n│ Service  │ calls      │ Service      │     │ Service      │\n│          │◄──────────►│              │     │              │\n└──────────┘ (sync)     └──────────────┘     └──────────────┘\n```\n\n#### **Key Design Patterns**\n1. **Factory Pattern**: NFT instrument deployment via factory contracts\n2. **Proxy Pattern**: Upgradeable smart contract proxies for gas optimization\n3. **Strategy Pattern**: Pluggable crypto curves, fee policies, burn policies\n4. **Observer Pattern**: Event listeners on NFT evolution, governance votes\n5. **State Machine**: NFT lifecycle (Seeded → Dormant → Flourishing → Fragmented → Archived)\n6. **CQRS**: Command/query separation in marketplace (write to PostgreSQL, read from MVCC snapshots)\n7. **Event Sourcing**: Append-only event log for auditability\n\n---\n\n### 7. NFT STATE MACHINE\n\nFrom `/docs/nft_evolution.md`:\n\n```\nSeeded (newly minted, base DNA + creator signature)\n  ↓\nDormant (frozen to preserve snapshot)\n  ↓\nFlourishing (fully mutable, traits append/replace/decay)\n  ├→ Evolve* (traits update, once per epoch)\n  ├→ Fragmented (split into derivative pieces for remixing)\n  │  └→ Merge (recombine fragmented tokens)\n  └→ Archived (finalized, no transitions possible)\n```\n\n**gRPC Evolution APIs** (from `nft_evolution.proto`):\n- `Inspire(nft_id, vrf_proof)` - Trigger evolution after PoI composer election\n- `Freeze(nft_id, signatures)` - Lock from further changes\n- `Evolve(nft_id, patch)` - Apply layer mutations\n- `Split(nft_id, parts)` - Create fractional pieces\n- `Merge(child_ids[])` - Recombine fragments\n- `Burn(nft_id)` - Irreversible finalization (pluggable burn policy)\n\n---\n\n### 8. PROOF-OF-INSPIRATION (PoI) CONSENSUS\n\nFrom `/docs/proof_of_inspiration.md`:\n\n**Formula for Composer Selection**:\n```\neffective_stake(validator) = \n    base_stake +\n    Σ delegator_balances × loyalty_multiplier +\n    inspiration_score × β\n```\n\n**VRF Election Process**:\n1. Seed = H(epoch_randomness || round_no)\n2. VRF output ∈ [0, 2²⁵⁶) evaluated by each validator\n3. Threshold = 2²⁵⁶ × (effective_stake / total_stake)\n4. If VRF_output < threshold → **Validator wins composer slot**\n\n**Slashing & Rewards**:\n- Double movement (same round): 5% slash + 1 epoch freeze\n- Invalid VRF proof: 100% block reward burned\n- Inactivity (≥256 rounds): 1% slash\n- Block rewards: 90% to composer + delegators, 10% to Inspiration Fund\n\n---\n\n### 9. GOVERNANCE PROTOCOL\n\nFrom `/docs/governance_protocol.md`:\n\n**Token**: `$BRUSH` (ERC-20-compatible, stake-weighted voting)\n\n**Proposal Lifecycle**:\n```\nDraft (∞) → Review (48h) → Voting (72h) → Enactment (24h) → Executed/Rejected\n```\n\n**Conviction Voting** (Polkadot-style):\n- 0 blocks: ×1 multiplier\n- 80k blocks (~1 week): ×2\n- 320k blocks: ×3\n- 1.28M blocks: ×4\n\n**Smart Contract** (ink! on Substrate):\n- `submit_proposal(ipfs_hash)` - Requires 0.01 BRUSH bond\n- `vote(proposal_id, kind, weight)` - Cast vote with conviction\n- `advance_stage()` - Progress through lifecycle\n- `veto_upgrade()` - ≥30% stake can abort scheduled upgrades\n\n---\n\n### 10. COMMUNICATION & OBSERVABILITY\n\n**Inter-Service Communication**:\n- **gRPC (sync)**: Request/response over Tonic with TLS\n- **NATS JetStream (async)**: Event pub/sub with durable consumers\n- **Transactional Outbox**: Dual-write hazard prevention between DB and event bus\n\n**Observability Stack** (from `docker-compose.yml`):\n- **Logs**: OpenTelemetry + Jaeger tracing\n- **Metrics**: Prometheus exporter on `/metrics` (port 8080 on each service)\n- **Dashboards**: Grafana (port 3000)\n- **Traces**: Tempo (port 4317)\n\n**Health Checks**:\n- All services expose `/health` REST endpoint\n- Docker compose HEALTHCHECK every 10s with 5 retries\n\n---\n\n### 11. DEPLOYMENT & ORCHESTRATION\n\n**Docker Compose Stack** (`docker-compose.yml`):\n- 1× NATS event-bus (messaging)\n- 1× PostgreSQL 15 (persistence, volumes)\n- 1× Substrate consensus-node (RPC on 9944, P2P on 30333)\n- 8× Microservices (REST on 6001-6008, gRPC on 7001-7008)\n- 4× Observability (Prometheus, Grafana, Tempo)\n\n**Network**: All services on single bridge network `canvaschain`\n\n**Environment Variables** (shared across all services):\n```bash\nRUST_LOG=info,hyper=warn,tower=warn\nEVENT_BUS_URL=nats://event-bus:4222\nDATABASE_URL=postgres://canvas:canvas@db:5432/canvas\nCONSENSUS_NODE_URL=http://consensus-node:9944\nOTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT=http://tempo:4317\n```\n\n---\n\n### 12. WORKSPACE DEPENDENCIES (from `Cargo.toml`)\n\n**Core async runtime**:\n- `tokio` v1.32 (multi-threaded)\n\n**gRPC stack**:\n- `tonic` v0.9 (async gRPC server)\n- `prost` v0.11 (Protobuf serialization)\n\n**Serialization**:\n- `serde` v1.0, `serde_json` v1.0\n\n**Error handling**:\n- `anyhow` v1.0, `thiserror` v1.0\n\n**Event bus**:\n- `nats` v0.23 (async NATS client)\n\n**Cryptography**:\n- `ed25519-dalek` v1.0.1 (default)\n- `blst` v0.3 (optional BLS)\n- `pqcrypto` v0.7.1 (optional post-quantum)\n\n**Logging & tracing**:\n- `tracing` v0.1, `tracing-subscriber` v0.3\n\n**Feature Flags**:\n- `wallet_integration` (default)\n- `token_management` (default)\n- `governance`, `defi_protocols`, `smart_contracts`\n- `bls`, `post_quantum` (experimental)\n\n---\n\n### 13. CRITICAL FILES FOR F-NFT INTEGRATION PLANNING\n\n| File | Relevance to F-NFT |\n|------|-------------------|\n| `/app/project/docs/nft_evolution.md` | NFT state machine; F-NFT will need split/merge operations |\n| `/app/project/docs/governance_protocol.md` | DAO voting on F-NFT fractionalization parameters |\n| `/app/project/crates/ccs_proto/proto/marketplace.proto` | Order-book structure; F-NFT creates new order types |\n| `/app/project/services/minting_service/README.md` | Factory pattern; reuse for F-NFT contract deployment |\n| `/app/project/services/marketplace_service/README.md` | MVCC architecture; handle fractional shares in order-book |\n| `/app/project/services/staking_service/README.md` | Staking engine; F-NFT holders could earn staking rewards |\n| `/app/project/docs/adr/003-pluggable-cryptography.md` | Strategy pattern for multi-sig F-NFT wallet support |\n\n---\n\n### 14. KEY INSIGHTS FOR F-NFT ARCHITECTURE\n\n1. **NFT Evolution Already Supports Fragmentation**: The existing `Split/Merge` state machine is foundational; F-NFTs are a natural extension.\n\n2. **Factory Pattern Ready**: Minting service uses Factory + Proxy for gas-optimized deployment; reuse for F-NFT instrument contracts.\n\n3. **Event-Driven Design**: NATS JetStream enables F-NFT share transfers to cascade to staking, governance, marketplace services atomically.\n\n4. **Multi-Curve Ready**: Strategy pattern allows F-NFT multi-sig wallets with heterogeneous signature schemes.\n\n5. **Governance Hooks**: Governance service can gate F-NFT fractionalization via proposal voting.\n\n6. **Marketplace Extensions**: Add F-NFT fractional share order types (fixed-price, AMM, auction) to existing order-book.\n\n7. **Royalty Streaming**: Existing Superfluid-like infrastructure can stream royalties proportionally to F-NFT shareholders.\n\n---\n\nThis comprehensive exploration gives you the full blueprint of CanvasChain Symphony's architecture. Core NFT domain types and evolution/state-machine logic for the\n//! CanvasChain Symphony platform.\n//!\n//! This module purposefully remains *no_std*-friendly (with an opt-in\n//! `std` feature for clients that need heap allocation) so it can be\n//! reused inside on-chain WASM smart contracts as well as off-chain\n//! micro-services.\n//!\n//! Key design goals:\n//! 1. Multi-layer NFTs with independent ownership & royalty streams.\n//! 2. Fine-grained state transitions driven by user interaction,\n//!    governance votes, staking incentives and DeFi metrics.\n//! 3. Pluggable cryptography via Strategy pattern (`SignatureScheme`).\n//!\n//! NOTE: Heavy business logic such as VRF computation or signature\n//! verification lives in dedicated crates (`ccs_cryptography`, …).\n#![cfg_attr(not(feature = \"std\"), no_std)]\n\nextern crate alloc;\n\nuse alloc::{\n    string::{String, ToString},\n    vec::Vec,\n    collections::BTreeMap,\n};\nuse core::{\n    fmt::{self, Display},\n    time::Duration,\n};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, struct WalletAddress(pub String);\n\nimpl Display for WalletAddress {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        f.write_str(&self.0)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Token amount in {\n        self.0\n    }\n}\n\n/// Signature strategy interface (Strategy pattern).\n/// Implementations live in the `ccs_cryptography` crate.\npub trait SignatureScheme {\n    type PublicKey;\n    type Signature;\n\n    fn verify(\n        pk: &Self::PublicKey,\n        msg: &[u8],\n        sig: &Self::Signature,\n    ) -> bool;\n}\n\n/// High-level NFT error enumeration.\n#[derive(Debug, thiserror::Error, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum NftError {\n    #[error(\"invalid basis points: {0}\")]\n    InvalidBasisPoints(u16),\n\n    #[error(\"layer is frozen and can’t be modified:        expected: WalletAddress,\n        actual: WalletAddress,\n    },\n\n    #[error(\"invalid state transition from {from:?} to {to:?}\")]\n    InvalidStateTransition {\n        from: LayerState,\n
