Custom(String),\n}\n\n/// Finite state machine for a layer’s lifecycle.\n#[derive(\n    Copy, Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize,\n)]\npub enum LayerState {\n    Draft,\n    Active,\n    Frozen,\n    Retired,\n}\n\nimpl LayerState {\n    pub fn can_transition(self, next: LayerState) -> bool {\n        use LayerState::*;\n        matches!(\n            (self, next),\n            (Draft, Active)\n                | (Active, Frozen)\n                | (Active, Retired)\n                | (Frozen, Retired) // irreversible freeze\n        )\n    }\n}\n\n/// Event that may trigger NFT mutations.\n/// This is serialized and sent over the event bus.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum EvolutionEvent {\n    /// A wallet interacted with the NFT (likes, remix, …).\n    Interaction {\n        wallet: WalletAddress,\n        payload: alloc::vec::Vec<u8>,\n    },\n\n    /// Snapshot of staking position for DeFi incentives.\n    Staked {\n proposal_id: Uuid,\n        approve_ratio: BasisPoints,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Single NFT layer with independent ownership & royalties.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct NftLayer {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    pub kind: NftLayerKind,\n    pub cid: String, // IPFS / Arweave content hash\n    pub state: LayerState,\n    pub owner: WalletAddress,\n    pub royalty_bp: BasisPoints,\n    pub created_at: u64,\n    pub last_updated: u64,\n}\n\nimpl NftLayer {\n    pub fn freeze(&mut self) -> Result<(), NftError> {\n        if self.state == LayerState::Frozen {\n            return Ok(());\n        }\n        if self.state.can_transition(LayerState::Frozen) {\n            self.state = LayerState::Frozen;\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            Err(NftError::InvalidStateTransition {\n                from: self.state,\n                to: LayerState::Frozen,\n            })\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Transfer ownership, enforcing the layer’s frozen status.\n    pub fn transfer(\n        &mut self,\n        from: &WalletAddress,\n        to: WalletAddress,\n    ) -> Result<(), NftError> {\n        if &self.owner != from {\n            return Err(NftError::OwnershipMismatch {\n                expected: self.owner.clone(),\n                actual: from.clone(),\n            });\n        }\n\n        if self.state == LayerState::Frozen {\n            return Err(NftError::LayerFrozen(self.id));\n        }\n\n        self.owner = to;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// Distribution of royalties across multiple stakeholders.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct RoyaltySchedule {\n    recipients: BTreeMap<WalletAddress, BasisPoints>,\n}\n\nimpl RoyaltySchedule {\n    /// Build a new schedule. The sum of `BasisPoints` must be ≤ 10k.\n    pub fn new(\n        recipients: BTreeMap<WalletAddress, BasisPoints>,\n    ) -> Result<Self, NftError> {\n        let total: u32 = recipients\n            .values()\n            .map(|bp| bp.as_u16() as u32)\n            .sum();\n        if total > BasisPoints::MAX as u32 {\n            return Err(NftError::RoyaltyOverflow);\n        }\n        Ok(Self  / BasisPoints::MAX as u128;\n                (addr.clone(), amount)\n            })\n            .collect()\n    }\n}\n\n/// Governance parameters per NFT.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize,           }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn handle_stake(\n        &mut self,\n        _wallet: WalletAddress,\n        amount: TokenAmount,\n        duration: Duration,\n    ) -> layer.freeze()?;\n            }\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    fn handle_governance(\n        &mut self,\n        approve_ratio: BasisPoints,\n    ) -> Result<(), NftError> {\n        if      if layer.state == LayerState::Draft {\n                    layer.state = LayerState::Active;\n                }\n            }\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            Err(NftError::Governance(\n                \"proposal rejected\".to_string(),\n            ))\n        }\n    }\n\n    fn normalize_royalties(&mut self) -> Result<(), NftError> {\n        let total: u32 = self\n            .royalties\n            .recipients\n            .values()\n            .map(|bp| bp.as_u16() as u32)\n            .sum();\n        if total > BasisPoints::MAX as u32 {\n            return Err(NftError::RoyaltyOverflow);\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Transfer a specific layer to a new owner.\n    pub fn transfer_layer(\n        &mut self,\n        layer_id: .ok_or(NftError::UnknownLayer(layer_id))?;\n        layer.transfer(from, to)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Fluent builder for `MultiLayerNft`.\npub struct NftBuilder {\n    id: Uuid,\n    name: String,\n      royalties: None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    pub fn add_layer(\n        mut self,\n        kind: NftLayerKind,\n        cid: impl Into<String>,\n        initial_state: LayerState,\n        royalty_bp: BasisPoints,\n    ) -> Self {\n        let layer = NftLayer {\n            id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n            kind,\n            cid: cid.into(),\n            state: initial_state,\n            owner: self.creator.clone(),\n            royalty_bp,\n            created_at: Self::now(),\n            last_updated: Self::now(),\n        };\n        self.layers.insert(layer.id, layer);\n Result<MultiLayerNft, NftError> {\n        let governance = self.governance.unwrap_or(GovernancePolicy {\n            quorum_bp: BasisPoints::new(2_000)?,\n            super_majority_bp: BasisPoints::new(6_600)?,\n            voting_period: Duration::from_secs(7 * 24 * 3600),\n        });\n\n        let royalties = self.royalties.unwrap_or_else(|| {\n            let mut map = BTreeMap::new();\n            map.insert(self.creator.clone(), BasisPoints(1_000)); // 10%\n            RoyaltySchedule { recipients: map }\n        });\n\n        Ok(MultiLayerNft {\n            id: self.id,\n            name: self.name,\n            creator: self.creator,\n            layers: self.layers,\n            governance,\n            royalties,\n        })\n    }\n\n    #[inline(always)]\n    fn now() -> u64 {\n        // Use UNIX time in seconds. On chain we replace this with\n        // block-timestamp to avoid dependence on wall clock.\n        #[cfg(feature = \"std\")]\n        {\n            use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n            SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .unwrap_or_default()\n                .as_secs()\n        }\n        #[cfg(not(feature = \"std\"))]\n        {\n            0\n        }\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":512,"startLine":1,"totalLines":512}}}

The public façade is [`NodeStateMachine::spawn`].  Callers provide a\n//! configured [`NodeContext`] plus a Tokio `mpsc::Receiver<NodeEvent>` that\n//! represents the node’s inbound event bus.  \n//!\n//! The machine is built around the classic State pattern.  Each state\n//! implements [`State`]—an async callback that receives the next event and\n//! returns a [`Transition`] indicating whether the machine should:\n//!   * stay in the same state\n//!   * transition to a new boxed state\n//!   * perform a graceful shutdown.\n//!\n//! Transitions are **total**; all event/state combinations _must_ resolve to a\n//! `Transition`, preventing undefined behaviour at runtime.\n//!\n//! -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//! Conventions & best-practices\n//! -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//! • Logging is performed with the `tracing` crate (structured, async-aware).  \n//! • All public types derive `Debug` to make tracing easier.  \n//! • Errors bubble up using `thiserror::Error` and are logged before causing a\n//!   transition into [`StateError`].  \n//! • No blocking I/O—everything is fully async.  \n//! • `#![deny(clippy::unwrap_used)]` ensures we never `.unwrap()` outside of\n//!   tests.  \n//!\n//! -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//! Example\n//! -------------------------------------------------------------------------\n//! ```no_run\n//! # use tokio::sync::mpsc;\n//! # use canvas_chain_node_service::state_machine::*;\n//! # #[tokio::main]\n//! # async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n//! let (tx, rx) = mpsc::channel(128);\n//! let ctx = NodeContext::new(Default::default(), tx.clone());\n//! let handle = NodeStateMachine::spawn(ctx, rx);\n//!\n//! // Send a dummy event\n//! tx.send(NodeEvent::Tick).await?;\n//!\n//! // Wait until the machine exits\n//! handle.await?;\n//! # Ok::<_, anyhow::Error>(())\n//! # }\n//! ```\n\n#![deny(clippy::unwrap_used)]\n\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse tokio::{\n    select,\n    sync::mpsc,\n    task::JoinHandle,\n    time::{interval, Interval},\n};\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\n\nuse crate::event_bus::Publish;\nuse crate::network::types::{Block, PeerId};\nuse crate::storage::Database;\n\n// ------------------------------\n// Public facade\n// ------------------------------\n\n/// Spawn a state-machine on the Tokio runtime and return a [`JoinHandle`]\n/// representing its lifecycle.\npub struct NodeStateMachine;\n\nimpl NodeStateMachine {\n    /// Spawns the asynchronous state machine.  The function immediately returns\n    /// a Tokio [`JoinHandle`] that finishes when the machine reaches the\n    /// [`Transition::Shutdown`] transition or the provided `event_rx` closes.\n    pub fn spawn(\n        mut ctx: NodeContext,\n        mut event_rx: mpsc::Receiver<NodeEvent>,\n    ) -> JoinHandle<anyhow::Result<()>> {\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let mut state: Box<dyn State> = Box::<StateBooting>::default();\n\n            // Heart-beat ticker; every state receives `Tick` events.\n            let mut ticker = interval(Duration::from_secs(5));\n\n            loop {\n                select! {\n                    maybe_evt = event_rx.recv() => {\n                        match maybe_evt {\n                            Some(evt) => {\n                                state = dispatch(&mut ctx, state, evt).await?;\n                            }\n                            None => {\n                                info!(\"event stream closed – shutting node down\");\n                                break;\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n\n                    _ = ticker.tick() => {\n                        state = dispatch(&mut ctx, state, NodeEvent::Tick).await?;\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n\n            info!(node_id=%ctx.config.node_id, \"node service terminated\");\n            Ok(())\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n// ------------------------------\n// Context & Events\n// ------------------------------\n\n/// Immutable configuration for a running node.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct NodeConfig {\n    pub node_id: String,\n    pub network_id: String,\n    pub database_url: String,\n    pub is_validator: bool,\n}\n\nimpl Default for NodeConfig {\n    fn is_validator: false,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Long-living, shared runtime context passed to every [`State`].\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct NodeContext {\n    pub config: NodeConfig,\n    pub db: Database,\n    pub event_tx: mpsc::Sender<NodeEvent>,\n    pub bus: Publish, // Simplified, re-export from crate::event_bus\n}\n\nimpl NodeContext {\n    pub fn new(config: NodeConfig, event_tx: mpsc::Sender<NodeEvent>) -> Self {\n        let db = Database::connect(&config.database_url)\n            .expect(\"infallible for in-memory; otherwise bubble up\");\n\n        Self {\n            config,\n            db,\n            event_tx,\n            bus: Publish::default(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// All possible events handled by the state machine.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum NodeEvent {\n    /// Periodic internal heart-beat.\n    Tick,\n\n    /// New peer connected\n    PeerConnected(PeerId),\n\n    /// Network broadcast of a new block announcement.\n    BlockReceived(Block),\n\n    /// The consensus engine elected this node the next composer (“validator”).\n    ElectedComposer,\n\n    /// Graceful shutdown request.\n    Shutdown,\n}\n\n// ------------------------------\n// State machinery\n// ------------------------------\n\n/// When a [`State`] finishes processing an event it returns a `Transition`\n/// deciding the machine’s next state.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub enum Transition {\n    /// Remain in the existing state.\n    Stay,\n    /// Replace current state with a new one.\n    Next(Box<dyn State>),\n    /// Terminate the machine.\n    Shutdown,\n}\n\n/// Common interface implemented by *every* node state.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait State: Send {\n    /// Human-readable name of the state (`&'static str` avoids CanvasChain Symphony – Minting Service\n//! --------------------------------------\n//! Factory & Strategy pattern–powered NFT minting logic.\n//! Creates multilayer NFTs, signs mint payloads with a pluggable crypto\n//! backend and broadcasts events onto the internal event-bus.\n//!\n//! This file purposefully hides transport concerns (gRPC, HTTP, etc.); the\n//! factory is a pure domain component that can be wired into whatever\n//! outer-layer the micro-service exposes.\n\nuse std::collections::HashMap;\nuse std::sync::Arc;\nuse std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse tokio::sync::RwLock;\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, instrument};\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Domain Types\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Human-readable, multi-layer NFT metadata sent by the client.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct MintRequest {\n    /// Wallet address of the creator in bech32 / hex format (depends on chain).\n    pub creator_wallet: String,\n    /// An ordered list of media layers that compose the artwork.\n    pub layers: Vec<NftLayer>,\n    /// Royalty basis-points (1/100 of a percent).\n    pub royalty_bps: u16,\n    /// Arbitrary on-chain attributes (e.g. color_palette → “pastel”).\n    pub attributes: HashMap<String, String>,\n    /// Number used once to avoid replay attacks.\n    pub nonce: u64,\n}\n\n/// Individual layer of the final NFT.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct NftLayer {\n    pub id: u32,\n    /// IPFS / Arweave / S3 URI\n    pub uri: String,\n}\n\n/// Normalised payload that the chain client can send to the runtime.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct MintPayload {\n    pub idempotency_key: Uuid,\n    pub creator_wallet: String,\n    pub metadata_cid: String,\n    pub royalty_bps: u16,\n    pub signature: Vec<u8>,\n}\n\n/// Successful on-chain minting result.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct NftReceipt {\n    pub token_id: u64,\n    pub tx_hash: String,\n    pub signature: Vec<u8>,\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Error Handling\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum FactoryError {\n    #[error(\"invalid royalty basis points: {0}\")]\n    InvalidRoyalty(u16),\n    #[error(\"mint payload validation error: {0}\")]\n    Validation(String),\n    #[error(\"crypto error: {0}\")]\n    Crypto(#[from] CryptoError),\n    #[error(\"blockchain client error: {0}\")]\n    Blockchain(#[from] BlockchainError),\n    #[error(\"event bus error: {0}\")]\n    EventBus(#[from] EventBusError),\n}\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum CryptoError {\n    #[error(\"unsupported curve\")]\n    UnsupportedCurve,\n    #[error(\"signature generation failed\")]\n    SignFailure,\n}\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum BlockchainError {\n    #[error(\"node unreachable\")]\n    NodeUnreachable,\n    #[error(\"transaction rejected: {0}\")]\n    TransactionRejected(String),\n}\n\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum EventBusError {\n    #[error(\"publish failed\")]\n    PublishFailed,\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Traits (Strategy / Port abstractions)\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Unified crypto-signer strategy so we can swap Ed25519, BLS, PQ, etc.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait CryptoSigner: Send + Sync {\n    /// ‘Human’ name of the algorithm (ed25519, bls12381, falcon, …).\n    fn algorithm(&self) -> &'static str;\n\n    /// Public identifier derived from the secret key (hex / bech32).\n    fn public_key(&self) -> String;\n\n    /// Sign arbitrary message bytes.\n    async fn sign(&self, message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CryptoError>;\n}\n\n/// Port for whatever chain client we’re using (substrate, cosmos, homework-chain).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait BlockchainClient: Send + Sync {\n    async fn submit_mint(&self, payload: MintPayload) -> Result<String, BlockchainError>;\n    async fn latest_token_id(&self) -> Result<u64, BlockchainError>;\n}\n\n/// Simplistic event bus publisher (fan-out / kafka / nats / anything).\n#[async_trait]\npub trait EventPublisher: Send + Sync {\n    async fn publish<M: Serialize + Send + Sync>(&self, topic: &str, msg: &M) -> Result<(), EventBusError>;\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// NFT Factory (Concrete implementation of the Factory pattern)\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n/// Shared reference-counted pointer to immutable factory configuration.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FactoryConfig {\n    /// Maximum allowed royalty (bps). Prevents creator mistakes.\n    pub max_royalty_bps: u16,\n    /// Event bus topic for successful mints.\n    pub event_topic_success: String,\n    /// Event bus topic for failed mints.\n    pub event_topic_failure: String,\n}\n\npub struct NftFactory<S, C, E>\nwhere\n    S: CryptoSigner,\n    C: BlockchainClient,\n    E: EventPublisher,\n{\n    signer: Arc<S>,\n    chain: Arc<C>,\n    bus: Arc<E>,\n    config: Arc<FactoryConfig>,\n    // optionally keep some internal state (metrics, counters, etc.)\n    minted_counter: RwLock<u64>,\n}\n\nimpl<S, C, E> NftFactory<S, C, E>\nwhere\n    S: CryptoSigner + 'static,\n    C: BlockchainClient + 'static,\n    E: EventPublisher + 'static,\n{\n    pub fn new(signer: Arc<S>, chain: Arc<C>, bus: Arc<E>, config: FactoryConfig) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            signer,\n            chain,\n            bus,\n            config: Arc::new(config),\n            minted_counter: RwLock::new(0),\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Validate, sign, submit and broadcast the mint request.\n    #[instrument(skip_all, level = \"info\", fields(creator = %req.creator_wallet))]\n    pub async fn mint_nft(&self, req: MintRequest) -> Result<NftReceipt, FactoryError> {\n        self.validate_request(&req)?;\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 1. Serialize metadata to JSON; in reality we would push to IPFS/Arweave\n        // and obtain a CID, we’ll fake it by hashing the JSON.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let metadata_json = serde_json::to_vec(&req)\n            .map_err(|e| FactoryError::Validation(format!(\"metadata serialization: {e}\")))?;\n\n        let metadata_cid = hex::encode(blake3::hash(&metadata_json).as_bytes());\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 2. Build chain payload and sign it with the selected algorithm.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let idempotency_key = Uuid::new_v4();\n        let mut payload_bytes = Vec::new();\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(idempotency_key.as_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(metadata_cid.as_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(&req.royalty_bps.to_le_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(req.creator_wallet.as_bytes());\n        payload_bytes.extend_from_slice(&req.nonce.to_le_bytes());\n\n        let signature = self.signer.sign(&payload_bytes).await?;\n\n        let payload = MintPayload {\n            idempotency_key,\n            creator_wallet: req.creator_wallet.clone(),\n            metadata_cid,\n            royalty_bps: req.royalty_bps,\n            signature: signature.clone(),\n        };\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 3. Submit mint extrinsic / transaction to the chain.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let tx_hash = match self.chain.submit_mint(payload.clone()).await {\n            Ok(h) => h,\n            Err(e) => {\n                // fire & forget failure event\n                let _ = self\n                    .bus\n                    .publish(\n                        &self.config.event_topic_failure,\n                        &MintFailureEvent::from_request(&req, &format!(\"{e}\")),\n                    )\n                    .await;\n                return Err(e.into());\n            }\n        };\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 4. Retrieve token ID (optimistic assumption we mint sequentially).\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        let token_id = self.chain.latest_token_id().await?;\n\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        // 5. Bump metrics & broadcast success event.\n        // ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n        {\n            let mut counter = self.minted_counter.write().await;\n            *counter += 1;\n        }\n\n        let receipt = NftReceipt {\n            token_id,\n            tx_hash: tx_hash.clone(),\n            signature,\n        };\n\n        self.bus\n            .publish(&self.config.event_topic_success, &receipt)\n            .await?;\n\n        info!(token_id, tx_hash, \"NFT minted successfully\");\n        Ok(receipt)\n    }\n\n    // -------------------\n    // internal helpers\n    // -------------------\n    fn validate_request(&self, req: &MintRequest) -> Result<(), FactoryError> {\n        if req.royalty_bps > self.config.max_royalty_bps {\n            return Err(FactoryError::InvalidRoyalty(req.royalty_bps));\n        }\n        if req.layers.is_empty() {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"at least one layer required\".into()));\n        }\n        if req.creator_wallet.trim().is_empty() {\n            return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"creator_wallet is empty\".into()));\n        }\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Event Definitions\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[derive(Debug, Serialize)]\nstruct MintFailureEvent {\n    pub creator_wallet: String,\n    pub timestamp_ms: u128,\n    pub reason: String,\n}\n\nimpl MintFailureEvent {\n    fn from_request(req: &MintRequest, reason: &str) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            creator_wallet: req.creator_wallet.clone(),\n            timestamp_ms: SystemTime::now()\n                .duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH)\n                .expect(\"system time ok\")\n                .as_millis(),\n            reason: reason.to_string(),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n/// Dummy/Mock implementations behind ‘test’ feature so we can run unit tests.\n/// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::sync::Mutex;\n\n    // --------------------\n    // Mock crypto signer\n    // --------------------\n    struct MockSigner;\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl CryptoSigner for MockSigner {\n        fn algorithm(&self) -> &'static str {\n            \"mocked-ed25519\"\n        }\n\n        fn public_key(&self) -> String {\n            \"deadbeef\".to_string()\n        }\n\n        async fn sign(&self, message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, CryptoError> {\n            Ok(blake3::hash(message).as_bytes().to_vec())\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------\n    // Mock chain client\n    // --------------------\n    struct MockChain {\n        counter: Mutex<u64>,\n    }\n\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl BlockchainClient for MockChain {\n        async fn submit_mint(&self, _payload: MintPayload) -> Result<String, BlockchainError> {\n            let mut c = self.counter.lock().unwrap();\n            *c += 1;\n            Ok(format!(\"0x{:x}\", *c))\n        }\n\n        async fn latest_token_id(&self) -> Result<u64, BlockchainError> {\n            let c = self.counter.lock().unwrap();\n            Ok(*c)\n        }\n    }\n\n    // --------------------\n    // Mock event bus\n    // --------------------\n    struct MockBus;\n    #[async_trait]\n    impl EventPublisher for MockBus {\n        async fn publish<M: Serialize + Send + Sync>(\n            &self,\n            topic: &str,\n            msg: &M,\n        ) -> Result<(), EventBusError> {\n            let payload = serde_json::to_string(msg).unwrap();\n            debug!(topic, payload, \"mock bus publish\");\n            Ok(())\n        }\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_mint_success() {\n        let signer = Arc::new(MockSigner);\n        let chain = Arc::new(MockChain {\n            counter: Mutex::new(0),\n        });\n        let bus = Arc::new(MockBus);\n        let factory = NftFactory::new(\n            signer,\n            chain,\n            bus,\n            FactoryConfig {\n                max_royalty_bps: 2_000, // 20%\n                event_topic_success: \"nft.mint.success\".into(),\n                event_topic_failure: \"nft.mint.failure\".into(),\n            },\n        );\n\n        let req = MintRequest {\n            creator_wallet: \"0xabc123\".into(),\n            layers: vec![NftLayer {\n                id: 1,\n                uri: \"ipfs://QmDUMMY\".into(),\n            }],\n            royalty_bps: 500,\n            attributes: HashMap::new(),\n            nonce: 42,\n        };\n\n        let receipt = factory.mint_nft(req.clone()).await.unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(receipt.token_id, 1);\n        assert!(!receipt.tx_hash.is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_invalid_royalty() {\n        let signer = Arc::new(MockSigner);\n        let chain = Arc::new(MockChain {\n            counter: Mutex::new(0),\n        });\n        let bus = Arc::new(MockBus);\n        let factory = NftFactory::new(\n            signer,\n            chain,\n            bus,\n            FactoryConfig {\n                max_royalty_bps: 2_000,\n                event_topic_success: \"nft.mint.success\".into(),\n                event_topic_failure: \"nft.mint.failure\".into(),\n            },\n        );\n\n        let req = MintRequest {\n            creator_wallet: }\n}\n```","numLines":440,"startLine":1,"totalLines":440}}}

Responsibilities\n//! * Create / update / cancel fixed-price listings\n//! * Atomically settle purchases (reserve → transfer → close)\n//! * Publish `MarketplaceEvent`s on the internal event bus\n//! * Provide a type-safe façade for gRPC handlers & GraphQL resolvers\n//!\n//! Note: In production, `ListingRepository` would be backed by Postgres or\n//! Only buyer {0:?} can proceed\")]\n    Reserved(AccountId),\n    #[error(\"Listing already sold\")]\n    AlreadySold,\n    #[error(\"Listing already cancelled\")]\n    AlreadyCancelled,\n    #[error(\"Repository error: {0}\")]\n    Repository(String),\n    #[error(\"Event dispatch failed: {0}\")]\n    EventDispatch(String),\n    #[error(\"Operation timed-out\")]\n    Timeout,\n}\n\n// async fn update(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError>;\n    async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Listing>, ListingError>;\n}\n\n// endregion: ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// region: ───── In-Memory Repository (example impl) ───────────────────────────\n\n/// Simple, thread-safe in-memory repository.  \n/// In production this would be swapped for a SQL or KV store implementation.\npub struct InMemoryListingRepository {\n    /// Internal map guarded by a read-write lock\n    listings: RwLock<HashMap<ListingId, Listing>>,\n}\n\nimpl InMemoryListingRepository {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self {\n            listings: RwLock::new(HashMap::new()),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl ListingRepository for InMemoryListingRepository {\n    #[instrument(skip(self, listing))]\n    async fn insert(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        let mut map = self.listings.write().await;\n        map.insert(listing.id, listing);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    async fn get(&self, id: ListingId) -> Result<Listing, ListingError> {\n        let map = self.listings.read().await;\n        map.get(&id).cloned().ok_or(ListingError::NotFound)\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self, listing))]\n    async fn update(&self, listing: Listing) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n        let mut map = self.listings.write().await;\n        map.insert(listing.id, listing);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    #[instrument(skip(self))]\n    async fn list_active(&self) -> Result<Vec<Listing>, ListingError> {\n        let map = self.listings.read().await;\n        Ok(map\n            .values()\n            .filter(|l| matches!(l.status, ListingStatus::Active))\n            .cloned()\n            .collect())\n    }\n}\n\n// endregion: ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n// region: ───── Event Bus Types ───────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum Auction engine for the marketplace micro-service.\n//!\n//! Responsibilities\n//! ----------------\n//! 1. Create, store and track English auctions for multilayer NFTs.\n//! 2. Enforce bidding rules (reserve price, minimum increment, anti-sniping).\n//! 3. Periodically finalize ended auctions and emit events onto the service bus.\n//!\n//! The engine is designed for dependency-injection: any persistent storage that\n//! implements `AuctionStore` can be plugged in.  Out of the box we provide an\n//! in-memory implementation that is perfect for unit tests and local dev\n//! environments.\n//!\n//! The module is *asynchronous* and uses Tokio for non-blocking execution.\n//!\n//! # Example\n//! ```no_run\n//! # use marketplace_service::auction_engine::*;\n//! # #[tokio::main]\n//! # async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n//! let engine = AuctionEngine::with_memory_store(Default::default());\n//!\n//! let auction_id = engine\n//!     .create_auction(CreateAuction {\n//!         nft_id: NftId::new(),\n//!         seller_id: UserId::new(),\n//!         reserve_price: 10_000,\n//!         duration: chrono::Duration::hours(24),\n//!     })\n//!     .await?;\n//!\n//! engine\n//!     .place_bid(auction_id, UserId::new(), 11_000)\n//!     .await?;\n//!\n//! // spawn finalize loop\n//! engine.clone().spawn_finalize_loop(tokio::time::Duration::from_secs(30));\n//! # Ok(()) }\n//! ```\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    fmt,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::Duration as StdDuration,\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Duration, Utc};\nuse -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                Type Aliases                                */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct AuctionId(Uuid);\n\nimpl AuctionId {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self(Uuid::new_v4())\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct UserId(Uuid);\n\nimpl UserId {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self(Uuid::new_v4())\n    }\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, Eq, PartialEq, Hash, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct NftId(Uuid);\n\nimpl NftId {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        Self(Uuid::new_v4())\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                 DTOs / API                                 */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/// Immutable auction parameters used for construction.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct CreateAuction {\n    pub nft_id: NftId,\n    pub seller_id: UserId,\n    /// Minimum price in the platform's smallest denomination (e.g. wei).\n    pub reserve_price: u128,\n    /// How long the auction should stay active.\n    pub duration: Duration,\n}\n\n/// Public view of a bid.\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Bid {\n    pub bidder_id: UserId,\n    pub amount: u128,\n    pub timestamp: DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                  Domain                                    */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum AuctionStatus {\n    Pending,\n    Active,\n    Finalized,\n    Cancelled,\n}\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct Auction {\n    pub id: AuctionId,\n    pub nft_id: NftId,\n    pub seller_id: UserId,\n    pub reserve_price: u128,\n    pub start_time: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub end_time: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub status: AuctionStatus,\n    pub highest_bid: Option<Bid>,\n}\n\nimpl Auction {\n    /// Returns whether the auction is open for bids.\n    pub fn is_active(&self, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.status, AuctionStatus::Active)\n            && now >= self.start_time\n            && now < self.end_time\n    }\n\n    /// Returns whether auction should be finalized (elapsed or cancelled).\n    pub fn should_finalize(&self, now: DateTime<Utc>) -> bool {\n        matches!(self.status, AuctionStatus::Active) && now >= self.end_time\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                    Store                                   */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n/// Storage abstraction. Can be backed by Postgres, Redis, RocksDB, etc.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait AuctionStore: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn insert(&self, auction: Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError>;\n    async fn update(&self, auction: &Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError>;\n    async fn get(&self, id: AuctionId) -> Result<Option<Auction>, EngineError>;\n    async fn all_due_for_finalize(\n        &self,\n        now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<AuctionId>, EngineError>;\n}\n\n/* --------------------------- In-memory implementation --------------------------- */\n\ntype AuctionMap = Arc<RwLock<HashMap<AuctionId, Auction>>>;\n\n/// An in-memory, thread-safe store backed by a `HashMap`.\n/// Meant for tests / local development, *not* production.\n#[derive(Clone, Default)]\npub struct MemoryAuctionStore {\n    map: AuctionMap,\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl AuctionStore for MemoryAuctionStore {\n    async fn insert(&self, auction: Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let mut map = self.map.write().await;\n        map.insert(auction.id, auction);\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn update(&self, auction: &Auction) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let mut map = self.map.write().await;\n        if let Some(a) = map.get_mut(&auction.id) {\n            *a = auction.clone();\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound)\n        }\n    }\n\n    async fn get(&self, id: AuctionId) -> Result<Option<Auction>, EngineError> {\n        let map = self.map.read().await;\n        Ok(map.get(&id).cloned())\n    }\n\n    async fn all_due_for_finalize(\n        &self,\n        now: DateTime<Utc>,\n    ) -> Result<Vec<AuctionId>, EngineError> {\n        let map = self.map.read().await;\n        Ok(map\n            .values()\n            .filter(|a| a.should_finalize(now))\n            .map(|a| a.id)\n            .collect())\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                               Event definitions                            */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum AuctionEvent {\n    AuctionCreated(AuctionId),\n    BidPlaced {\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        bidder_id: UserId,\n        amount: u128,\n    },\n    AuctionFinalized {\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        winner: Option<UserId>,\n        amount:                      */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum EngineError {\n    #[error(\"auction not found\")]\n    AuctionNotFound,\n    #[error(\"auction is not active\")]\n    AuctionNotActive,\n    #[error(\"bid below reserve price\")]\n    ReserveNotMet,\n    #[error(\"bid increment too low\")]\n    IncrementTooLow,\n    #[error(\"seller cannot bid on own auction\")]\n    SellerCannotBid,\n    #[error(\"auction already finalized or cancelled\")]\n    InvalidAuctionState,\n    #[error(\"storage error: {0}\")]\n    Storage(#[from] anyhow::Error),\n    #[error(\"broadcast error\")]\n    Broadcast,\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                AuctionEngine                               */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct AuctionEngine<S: AuctionStore> {\n    store: Arc<S>,\n    /// Minimum bid increment (e.g. 1% of current highest) expressed as basis points.\n    min_increment_bps: u16,\n    /// Tokio broadcast channel for events.\n    event_tx: broadcast::Sender<AuctionEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl<S: AuctionStore> AuctionEngine<S> {\n    /// Create an engine using the provided store.\n    pub fn new(store: S, min_increment_bps: u16) -> Self {\n        let (event_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(1024);\n        Self {\n            store: Arc::new(store),\n            min_increment_bps,\n            event_tx,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Convenience helper for tests/local dev.\n    pub fn with_memory_store(config: EngineConfig) -> Self {\n        Self::new(MemoryAuctionStore::default(), config.min_increment_bps)\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to auction events (fire-and-forget).\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<AuctionEvent> {\n        self.event_tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- API methods ----------------------------- */\n\n    /// Creates an auction and stores it. Emits `AuctionCreated`.\n    pub async fn create_auction(\n        &self,\n        params: CreateAuction,\n    ) -> Result<AuctionId, EngineError> {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let auction = Auction {\n            id: AuctionId::new(),\n            nft_id: params.nft_id,\n            seller_id: params.seller_id,\n            reserve_price: params.reserve_price,\n            start_time: now,\n            end_time: now + params.duration,\n            status: AuctionStatus::Active,\n            highest_bid: None,\n        };\n        self.store.insert(auction.clone()).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::AuctionCreated(auction.id))?;\n        Ok(auction.id)\n    }\n\n    /// Places a new bid on an active auction.\n    pub async fn place_bid(\n        &self,\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        bidder_id: UserId,\n        amount: u128,\n    ) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let now = Utc::now();\n        let mut auction = match self.store.get(auction_id).await? {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound),\n        };\n\n        if !auction.is_active(now) {\n            return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotActive);\n        }\n\n        if bidder_id == auction.seller_id {\n            return Err(EngineError::SellerCannotBid);\n        }\n\n        // First bid must be >= reserve.\n        if auction.highest_bid.is_none() && amount < auction.reserve_price {\n            return Err(EngineError::ReserveNotMet);\n        }\n\n        // Ensure minimum increment.\n        if let Some(ref high) = auction.highest_bid {\n            let min_required =\n                high.amount + Self::calc_increment(high.amount, self.min_increment_bps);\n            if amount < min_required {\n                return Err(EngineError::IncrementTooLow);\n            }\n        }\n\n        // TODO: anti-sniping extension window can be applied here.\n\n        auction.highest_bid = Some(Bid {\n            bidder_id,\n            amount,\n            timestamp: now,\n        });\n\n        self.store.update(&auction).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::BidPlaced {\n            auction_id,\n            bidder_id,\n            amount,\n        })?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Cancels an auction by its seller before any bids are placed.\n    pub async fn cancel_auction(\n        &self,\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n        requester: UserId,\n    ) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        let mut auction = match self.store.get(auction_id).await? {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound),\n        };\n\n        if auction.seller_id != requester {\n            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAuctionState);\n        }\n\n        if auction.highest_bid.is_some() || !matches!(auction.status, AuctionStatus::Active) {\n            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAuctionState);\n        }\n\n        auction.status = AuctionStatus::Cancelled;\n        self.store.update(&auction).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::AuctionCancelled(auction_id))?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Finalize an auction that reached its end time. Returns the winner if any.\n    pub async fn finalize_auction(\n        &self,\n        auction_id: AuctionId,\n    ) -> Result<Option<(UserId, u128)>, EngineError> {\n        let mut auction = match self.store.get(auction_id).await? {\n            Some(a) => a,\n            None => return Err(EngineError::AuctionNotFound),\n        };\n\n        if !auction.should_finalize(Utc::now()) {\n            return Err(EngineError::InvalidAuctionState);\n        }\n\n        auction.status = AuctionStatus::Finalized;\n        let winner_info = auction\n            .highest_bid\n            .as_ref()\n            .map(|b| (b.bidder_id, b.amount));\n\n        self.store.update(&auction).await?;\n        self.emit(AuctionEvent::AuctionFinalized {\n            auction_id,\n            winner: winner_info.map(|t| t.0),\n            amount: winner_info.map(|t| t.1),\n        })?;\n\n        // TODO: trigger settlement workflow (escrow smart contract, royalties, etc.)\n        Ok(winner_info)\n    }\n\n    /* ------------------------------- Internals ------------------------------ */\n\n    fn emit(&self, evt: AuctionEvent) -> Result<(), EngineError> {\n        self.event_tx\n            .send(evt)\n            .map_err(|_| EngineError::Broadcast)\n            .map(|_| ())\n    }\n\n    fn calc_increment(current: u128, bps: u16) -> u128 {\n        // basis points: 1 bps = 0.01 %\n        (current * bps as u128) / 10_000u128\n    }\n\n    /* ----------------------------- Finalize loop ---------------------------- */\n\n    /// Spawns a Tokio task that periodically finalizes elapsed auctions.\n    ///\n    /// NOTE: In a clustered deployment only the leader instance should run the\n    /// loop to avoid double-finalization.  This is achieved via distributed\n    /// locks (e.g. etcd/consul) at the service orchestrator layer.\n    pub fn spawn_finalize_loop(self, interval: StdDuration) -> JoinHandle<()> {\n        tokio::spawn(async move {\n            let mut ticker = tokio::time::interval(interval);\n            loop {\n                ticker.tick().await;\n                let now = Utc::now();\n                match self.store.all_due_for_finalize(now).await {\n                    Ok(ids) => {\n                        for id in ids {\n                            // best effort, log errors\n                            if let Err(e) = self.finalize_auction(id).await {\n                                tracing::warn!(\n                                    auction_id = ?id,\n                                    error = ?e,\n                                    \"failed to finalize auction\"\n                                );\n                            }\n                        }\n                    }\n                    Err(e) => tracing::error!(error = ?e, \"finalize loop failed to fetch auctions\"),\n                }\n            }\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                              Engine configuration                          */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct EngineConfig {\n    pub min_increment_bps: u16,\n}\n\nimpl Default for EngineConfig {\n    fn default() -> Self {\n        Self { min_increment_bps: 50 } // 0.5%\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                                   Display                                  */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nimpl fmt::Display for AuctionId {\n    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> fmt::Result {\n        self.0.fmt(f)\n    }\n}\n```","numLines":494,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

The module follows a Strategy pattern where each curve implements the\n//! `SigningCurve` trait.  A very small, file–based keystore is provided for\n//! demo/PoC purposes; in production an HSM or KMS implementation should\n//! instead be plugged in via the same trait.\n//!\n//! NOTE: All public functions are `async` to keep the API uniform even\n//! though the current implementation is I/O–bound only.\n\nuse std::{\n    fs::{self, File},\n    io::Write,\n    path::{Path, PathBuf},\n    sync::Arc,\n};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse chacha20poly1305::{\n    aead::{Aead, AeadCore, KeyInit, OsRng as ChaOsRng},\n    ChaCha20Poly1305, Key, Nonce,\n};\nuse rand_core::RngCore;\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::sync::RwLock;\nuse uuid::Uuid;\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Public API\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Supported signature algorithms.\n///\n/// New curves (BLS, PQ, …) can be enabled behind a Cargo feature flag and\n/// added here without touching the rest of the service.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum CryptoAlgorithm {\n    Ed25519,\n    Secp256k1,\n}\n\n/// A minimal, curve-agnostic keypair.\n///\n/// The concrete key material is stored encrypted on disk; we only keep the\n/// public key and metadata in memory.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct WalletMeta {\n    pub id:              Uuid,\n    pub algo:            CryptoAlgorithm,\n    pub public_key:      Vec<u8>,\n    pub encrypted_sk:    Vec<u8>,\n    pub enc_salt:        Vec<u8>,\n    pub enc_nonce:       Vec<u8>,\n    pub created_at:      DateTime<Utc>,\n}\n\n/// Top-level manager.\n///\n/// Internally uses a `KeyStore` implementation and a registry of available\n/// signing curves.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct KeyManager {\n    keystore: Arc<dyn KeyStore>,\n    curves:   CurveRegistry,\n}\n\nimpl KeyManager {\n    /// Create a new manager with the default [`FileKeyStore`].\n    pub async fn new<P: AsRef<Path>>(file: P) -> Result<Self, KeyManagerError> {\n        let ks = FileKeyStore::open(file).await?;\n        let mut registry = CurveRegistry::default();\n        registry.register(Box::<Ed25519Curve>::default());\n        registry.register(Box::<Secp256k1Curve>::default());\n\n        Ok(Self {\n            keystore: Arc::new(ks),\n            curves:   registry,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Creates and persists a brand new wallet.\n    pub async fn create_wallet(\n        &self,\n        password: &[u8],\n        algo: CryptoAlgorithm,\n    ) -> Result<WalletMeta, KeyManagerError> {\n        let curve = self\n            .curves\n            .get(algo)\n            .ok_or(KeyManagerError::UnsupportedAlgorithm)?;\n\n        // 1) Generate a fresh keypair\n        let (sk, pk) = curve.generate()?;\n\n        // 2) Encrypt the secret key with Argon2 + ChaCha20-Poly1305\n        let (ciphertext, salt, nonce) = encrypt_secret(password, &sk)?;\n\n        // 3) Store on disk\n        let wallet = WalletMeta {\n            id: Uuid::new_v4(),\n            algo,\n            public_key: pk,\n            encrypted_sk: ciphertext,\n            enc_salt: salt.to_vec(),\n            enc_nonce: nonce.to_vec(),\n            created_at: Utc::now(),\n        };\n\n        self.keystore.put(&wallet).await?;\n        Ok(wallet)\n    }\n\n    /// Unlock a wallet, returning the raw secret key in memory for\n    /// subsequent signing.  The caller is responsible for zeroizing the\n    /// buffer after use.\n    pub async fn unlock_wallet(\n        &self,\n        id: Uuid,\n        password: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<UnlockedWallet, KeyManagerError> {\n        let meta = self\n            .keystore\n            .get(id)\n            .await?\n            .ok_or(KeyManagerError::WalletNotFound)?;\n\n        let curve = self\n            .curves\n            .get(meta.algo)\n            .ok_or(KeyManagerError::UnsupportedAlgorithm)?;\n\n        let sk = decrypt_secret(\n            password,\n            &meta.encrypted_sk,\n            &meta.enc_salt,\n            &meta.enc_nonce,\n        )?;\n\n        Ok(UnlockedWallet {\n            meta,\n            secret_key: sk,\n            curve,\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Sign an arbitrary message with the given wallet.\n    pub async fn sign(\n        &self,\n        wallet: &UnlockedWallet,\n        message: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError> {\n        wallet.curve.sign(&wallet.secret_key, message)\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Unlocked wallet (ephemeral)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Ephemeral wallet handle.\n///\n/// The secret key is kept in memory only and never persisted; once the value\n/// goes out of scope it should be considered locked again.\npub struct UnlockedWallet<'a> {\n    meta:       WalletMeta,\n    secret_key: Vec<u8>,\n    curve:      &'a dyn SigningCurve,\n}\n\nimpl<'a> UnlockedWallet<'a> {\n    pub fn public_key(&self) -> &[u8] {\n        &self.meta.public_key\n    }\n\n    pub fn id(&self) -> Uuid {\n        self.meta.id\n    }\n\n    pub fn algo(&self) -> CryptoAlgorithm {\n        self.meta.algo\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Signing curves (Strategy pattern)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Strategy trait for crypto curves.\npub trait SigningCurve: Send + Sync {\n    fn algorithm(&self) -> CryptoAlgorithm;\n\n    /// Generate a fresh keypair.\n    fn generate(&self) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>), KeyManagerError>;\n\n    /// Sign `message` with the raw secret key.\n    fn sign(&self, secret: &[u8], message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError>;\n\n    /// Verify a signature – primarily for unit tests.\n    #[allow(dead_code)]\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError>;\n}\n\n/// Registry holding all curve implementations.\n#[derive(Default, Clone)]\nstruct CurveRegistry {\n    curves: Arc<RwLock<Vec<Box<dyn SigningCurve>>>>,\n}\n\nimpl CurveRegistry {\n    fn register(&mut self, curve: Box<dyn SigningCurve>) {\n        tokio::task::block_in_place(|| {\n            let mut guard = futures::executor::block_on(self.curves.write());\n            guard.push(curve);\n        });\n    }\n\n    fn get(&self, algo: CryptoAlgorithm) -> Option<&dyn SigningCurve> {\n        tokio::task::block_in_place(|| {\n            let guard = futures::executor::block_on(self.curves.read());\n            guard\n                .iter()\n                .find(|c| c.algorithm() == algo)\n                .map(|b| b.as_ref())\n        })\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── Ed25519 ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Ed25519Curve;\n\nimpl SigningCurve for Ed25519Curve {\n    fn algorithm(&self) -> CryptoAlgorithm {\n        CryptoAlgorithm::Ed25519\n    }\n\n    fn generate(&self) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>), KeyManagerError> {\n        use ed25519_dalek::{Keypair, SignatureError, Signer};\n        let kp: Keypair = Keypair::generate(&mut rand_core::OsRng);\n        Ok((kp.secret.to_bytes().to_vec(), kp.public.to_bytes().to_vec()))\n    }\n\n    fn sign(&self, secret: &[u8], message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError> {\n        use ed25519_dalek::{Keypair, PublicKey, SecretKey, Signer};\n        let secret = SecretKey::from_bytes(secret)?;\n        let public: PublicKey = (&secret).into();\n        let kp = Keypair { secret, public };\n        Ok(kp.sign(message).to_bytes().to_vec())\n    }\n\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        use ed25519_dalek::{PublicKey, Signature, Verifier};\n        let pk = PublicKey::from_bytes(public)?;\n        let sig = Signature::from_bytes(signature)?;\n        pk.verify(message, &sig)?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ─── Secp256k1 ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct Secp256k1Curve;\n\nimpl SigningCurve for Secp256k1Curve {\n    fn algorithm(&self) -> CryptoAlgorithm {\n        CryptoAlgorithm::Secp256k1\n    }\n\n    fn generate(&self) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>), KeyManagerError> {\n        use k256::{\n            ecdsa::{SigningKey, VerifyingKey},\n            elliptic_curve::SecretKey as ECSecret,\n        };\n        let sk = SigningKey::random(&mut rand_core::OsRng);\n        let pk = VerifyingKey::from(&sk);\n        let sk_bytes = ECSecret::from(sk).to_bytes().to_vec();\n        Ok((sk_bytes, pk.to_encoded_point(false).as_bytes().to_vec()))\n    }\n\n    fn sign(&self, secret: &[u8], message: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<u8>, KeyManagerError> {\n        use k256::ecdsa::{signature::Signer as _, Signature, SigningKey};\n        let sk = SigningKey::from_bytes(secret.into())?;\n        let sig: Signature = sk.sign(message);\n        Ok(sig.as_ref().to_vec())\n    }\n\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        signature: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        use k256::ecdsa::{signature::Verifier as _, Signature, VerifyingKey};\n        let vk = VerifyingKey::from_sec1_bytes(public)?;\n        let sig = Signature::from_der(signature)?;\n        vk.verify(message, &sig)?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Keystore (file-based JSON)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[async_trait]\ntrait KeyStore: Send + Sync {\n    async fn put(&self, wallet: &WalletMeta) -> atomic write-to-tmp-then-rename.\nstruct FileKeyStore {\n    path:   PathBuf,\n    cache:  RwLock<Vec<WalletMeta>>,\n}\n\nimpl FileKeyStore {\n    async fn open<P: AsRef<Path>>(p: P) -> Result<Self, KeyManagerError> {\n        let path = p.as_ref().to_path_buf();\n        if !path.exists() {\n            // create empty file\n            tokio::fs::write(&path, b\"[]\").await?;\n        }\n\n      })\n    }\n\n    async fn persist(&self, list: &[WalletMeta]) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        let tmp_path = self\n            .path\n            .with_extension(\"tmp\");\n\n tmp_file.sync_all()?;\n                fs::rename(&tmp_path, &self.path)?;\n                Ok(())\n            }\n        })\n        .await??;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl KeyStore for FileKeyStore {\n    async fn put(&self, wallet: &WalletMeta) -> Result<(), KeyManagerError> {\n        let mut guard = self.cache.write().await;\n        guard.push(wallet.clone());\n        self.persist(&guard).await?;\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    async fn get(&self, id: Uuid) -> Result<Option<WalletMeta>, KeyManagerError> {\n        let guard = self.cache.read().await;\n        Ok(guard.iter().cloned().find(|w| w.id == id))\n    }\n}\n\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Crypto helpers (Argon2 + ChaCha20-Poly1305)\n// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nconst CHACHA_NONCE_SIZE: usize = 12;\nconst ARGON_SALT_LEN: usize = 16;\nconst ARGON_MEM_COST_KIB: u32 = 64 * 1024; // 64 MiB\nconst ARGON_ITER: u32 = 3;\n\nfn encrypt_secret(\n    password: &[u8],\n    secret: &[u8],\n) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, [u8; ARGON_SALT_LEN], [u8; CHACHA_NONCE_SIZE]), KeyManagerError> {\n    let RPC server for the Wallet micro-service.\n//!\n//! The wallet RPC server exposes a small gRPC interface that lets other\n//! Symphony services (or external clients) create wallets, query balances,\n//! transfer tokens and sign arbitrary messages.  \n//!\n//! Internally the server is backed by a pluggable [`WalletBackend`] trait.\n//! The default backend (`SledBackend`) stores encrypted key-material in a\n//! Sled embedded database and uses ed25519‐dalek for signing.  Backends can\n//! be swapped at runtime (Strategy pattern) which makes it trivial to\n//! upgrade to BLS, post-quantum or hardware-based signing in the future.\n//!\n//! # Conventions\n//!\n//! * All monetary amounts are expressed in **atomic units** (u128).\n//! * Hex-encoded strings are expected to be lowercase, without `0x` prefix.\n//! * Errors are mapped to gRPC `Status` codes using a custom [`WalletError`]\n//!   → [`tonic::Status`] conversion.\n\n#![allow(clippy::missing_errors_doc)]\n#![allow(clippy::missing_panics_doc)]\n\nuse std::{net::SocketAddr, path::PathBuf, sync::Arc};\n\nuse async_trait::async_trait;\nuse ed25519_dalek::{Keypair, PublicKey, Signature, Signer, PUBLIC_KEY_LENGTH};\nuse rand::rngs::OsRng;\nuse thiserror::Error;\nuse tokio::sync::RwLock;\nuse tonic::{transport::Server, Request, Response, Status};\nuse tracing::{error, info};\n\nuse crate::config::WalletConfig;\n\n// === Generated code from `protoc` lives in `crate::proto` === //\nuse crate::proto::wallet::{\n    wallet_service_server::{WalletService, WalletServiceServer},\n    BalanceRequest, BalanceResponse, CreateWalletRequest, CreateWalletResponse,\n    SignRequest, SignResponse, TransferRequest, TransferResponse,\n};\n\n// =========================================================================\n//  Domain errors\n// =========================================================================\n\n/// All domain-level errors produced by the wallet service.\n#[derive(Debug, Error)]\npub enum WalletError {\n    #[error(\"wallet `{0}` not found\")]\n    WalletNotFound(String),\n\n    #[error(\"insufficient funds (available: {available}, required: {required})\")]\n    InsufficientFunds { available: u128, required: u128 },\n\n    #[error(\"database error: {0}\")]\n    Db(#[from] sled::Error),\n\n    #[error(\"signature failure: {0}\")]\n    Signature(String),\n\n    #[error(\"unsupported cryptographic backend\")]\n    UnsupportedBackend,\n\n    #[error(\"internal error: {0}\")]\n    Internal(String),\n}\n\nimpl From<WalletError> for Status {\n    fn from(err: WalletError) -> Self {\n        match err {\n            WalletError::WalletNotFound(_) => Status::not_found(err.to_string()),\n            WalletError::InsufficientFunds { .. } => Status::failed_precondition(err.to_string()),\n            WalletError::Db(_) | WalletError::Internal(_) => Status::internal(err.to_string()),\n            WalletError::Signature(_) => Status::internal(err.to_string()),\n            WalletError::UnsupportedBackend => Status::unimplemented(err.to_string()),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// =========================================================================\n//  Wallet backend abstraction (Strategy Pattern)\n// =========================================================================\n\n/// Abstraction over the storage + crypto engine used for wallet operations.\n#[async_trait]\npub trait WalletBackend: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    async fn create_wallet(&self) -> Result<(String, PublicKey), WalletError>;\n\n    async fn balance(&self, wallet_id: &str) -> Result<u128, WalletError>;\n\n    async fn transfer(\n        &self,\n        from: &str,\n        to: &str,\n        amount: u128,\n    ) -> Result<(), WalletError>;\n\n    async fn sign(\n        &self,\n        wallet_id: &str,\n        message: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Signature, WalletError>;\n}\n\n// =========================================================================\n//  Default backend implementation (Sled + Ed25519)\n// =========================================================================\n\n/// Wallet metadata stored in the DB (serialized with `bincode`).\n#[derive(serde::Serialize, serde::Deserialize)]\nstruct WalletRecord {\n    keypair: Keypair,\n    balance: u128,\n}\n\n/// Simple Sled-based backend storing encrypted key-material locally.\npub struct SledBackend {\n    db: sled::Db,\n    /// Namespace trees\n    keys_tree: sled::Tree,\n    ledger_tree: sled::Tree,\n}\n\nimpl SledBackend {\n    pub fn new(path: impl Into<PathBuf>) -> Result<Self, WalletError> {\n        let db = sled::open(path.into())?;\n        Ok(Self {\n            keys_tree: db.open_tree(\"keys\")?,\n            ledger_tree: db.open_tree(\"ledger\")?,\n            db,\n        })\n    }\n\n    fn _wallet_key(wallet_id: &str) -> Vec<u8> {\n        format!(\"wallet::{wallet_id}\").into_bytes()\n    }\n}\n\n#[async_trait]\nimpl WalletBackend for SledBackend {\n    async fn create_wallet(&self) -> Result<(String, PublicKey), WalletError> {\n        // Generate new keypair\n        let mut csprng = OsRng {};\n        let keypair = Keypair::generate(&mut csprng);\n        let wallet_id = hex::encode(&keypair.public.to_bytes());\n\n        let record = WalletRecord {\n            keypair,\n            balance: 0u128,\n        };\n        // Serialize\n        let payload = bincode::serialize(&record)\n            .map_err(|e| WalletError::Internal(format!(\"bincode: {e}\")))?;\n\n        self.keys_tree\n            .insert(Self::_wallet_key(&wallet_id), payload)?;\n        self.ledger_tree.insert(wallet_id.as_bytes(), 0u128.to_be_bytes())?;\n        self.db.flush()?;\n\n        Ok((wallet_id, record.keypair.public))\n    }\n\n    async fn balance(&self, wallet_id: &str) -> Result<u128, WalletError> {\n        let raw = self\n            .ledger_tree\n            .get(wallet_id.as_bytes())?\n            .ok_or_else(|| WalletError::WalletNotFound(wallet_id.to_string()))?;\n        let mut bytes = [0u8; 16];\n        bytes.copy_from_slice(&raw);\n        Ok(u128::from_be_bytes(bytes))\n    }\n\n    async fn transfer(\n        &self,\n        from: &str,\n        to: &str,\n        amount: u128,\n    ) -> Result<(), WalletError> {\n        // Sled does not support multi-key transactions; we pessimistically lock the DB.\n        let _guard = self.db.transaction(|db| {\n            let from_balance = {\n                let raw = self\n                    .ledger_tree\n                    .get(from.as_bytes())?\n                    .ok_or(sled::transaction::ConflictableTransactionError::Abort(\n                        WalletError::WalletNotFound(from.into()),\n                    ))?;\n                let mut b = [0u8; 16];\n                b.copy_from_slice(&raw);\n                Ok(u128::from_be_bytes(b))\n            }?;\n\n            if from_balance < amount {\n                return Err(sled::transaction::ConflictableTransactionError::Abort(\n                    WalletError::InsufficientFunds {\n                        available: from_balance,\n                        required: amount,\n                    },\n                ));\n            }\n\n            let to_balance = {\n                let raw =\n                    self.ledger_tree.get(to.as_bytes())?.unwrap_or_else(|| {\n                        // Auto-create destination account\n                        self.ledger_tree.insert(to.as_bytes(), 0u128.to_be_bytes()).unwrap();\n                        sled::IVec::from(&0u128.to_be_bytes()[..])\n                    });\n                let mut b = [0u8; 16];\n                b.copy_from_slice(&raw);\n                u128::from_be_bytes(b)\n            };\n\n            let new_from = (from_balance - amount).to_be_bytes();\n            let new_to = (to_balance + amount).to_be_bytes();\n\n            self.ledger_tree.insert(from.as_bytes(), new_from)?;\n            self.ledger_tree.insert(to.as_bytes(), new_to)?;\n            Ok(())\n        });\n\n        match _guard {\n            Ok(_) => Ok(()),\n            Err(sled::transaction::TransactionError::Abort(e)) => Err(e),\n            Err(e) => Err(WalletError::Db(e.into())),\n        }\n    }\n\n    async fn sign(\n        &self,\n        wallet_id: &str,\n        message: &[u8],\n    ) -> Result<Signature, WalletError> {\n        let raw = self\n            .keys_tree\n            .get(Self::_wallet_key(wallet_id))?\n            .ok_or_else(|| WalletError::WalletNotFound(wallet_id.to_owned()))?;\n        let record: WalletRecord =\n            bincode::deserialize(&raw).map_err(|e| WalletError::Internal(e.to_string()))?;\n        Ok(record.keypair.sign(message))\n    }\n}\n\n// =========================================================================\n//  RPC layer\n// =========================================================================\n\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct RpcServer<B> {\n    backend: Arc<B>,\n}\n\nimpl<B> RpcServer<B>\nwhere\n    B: WalletBackend,\n{\n    pub fn new(backend: Arc<B>) -> Self {\n        Self { backend }\n    }\n}\n\n#[tonic::async_trait]\nimpl<B> WalletService for RpcServer<B>\nwhere\n    B: WalletBackend,\n{\n    async fn create_wallet(\n        &self,\n        _req: Request<CreateWalletRequest>,\n    ) -> Result<Response<CreateWalletResponse>, Status> {\n        let (id, public_key) = self.backend.create_wallet().await?;\n        let resp = CreateWalletResponse {\n            wallet_id: id,\n            public_key: hex::encode(public_key.to_bytes()),\n        };\n        Ok(Response::new(resp))\n    }\n\n    async fn balance(\n        &self,\n        req: Request<BalanceRequest>,\n    ) -> Result<Response<BalanceResponse>, Status> {\n        let BalanceRequest { wallet_id } = req.into_inner();\n        let balance = self.backend.balance(&wallet_id).await?;\n        Ok(Response::new(BalanceResponse { balance }))\n    }\n\n    async fn transfer(\n        &self,\n        req: Request<TransferRequest>,\n    ) -> Result<Response<TransferResponse>, Status> {\n        let TransferRequest {\n            from_wallet_id,\n            to_address,\n            amount,\n        } = req.into_inner();\n\n        self.backend\n            .transfer(&from_wallet_id, &to_address, amount)\n            .await?;\n\n        Ok(Response::new(TransferResponse {}))\n    }\n\n    async fn sign(\n        &self,\n        req: Request<SignRequest>,\n    ) -> Result<Response<SignResponse>, Status> {\n        let SignRequest {\n            wallet_id,\n            message,\n        } = req.into_inner();\n        let signature = self.backend.sign(&wallet_id, &message).await?;\n        Ok(Response::new(SignResponse {\n            signature: hex::encode(signature.to_bytes()),\n            public_key_len: PUBLIC_KEY_LENGTH as u32,\n        }))\n    }\n}\n\n// =========================================================================\n//  Bootstrapping\n// =========================================================================\n\n/// Starts the 413→    let quorum_met = (total_votes * 100 / u64::MAX.max(total_votes)) >= params.quorum_pct as u64;\n   414→\n   415→    if !quorum_met {\n   416→        return ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n   417→    }\n   418→\n   419→    let yes_pct = yes * 100 / total_votes;\n   420→    if yes_pct >= params.pass_threshold_pct as u64 {\n   421→        ProposalOutcome::Accepted\n   422→    } else {\n   423→        ProposalOutcome::Rejected\n   424→    }\n   425→}\n   426→\n   427→/// Periodic task for auto-finalizing expired proposals.\n   428→async fn finalize_expired<S: ProposalStore, E: pub async fn finalize_now(&self, proposal_id: Uuid) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n        let proposal = self.store.load_proposal(proposal_id).await?;\n        let outcome = ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n    }\n\n    let yes = proposal\n        .votes\n        .values()\n        .filter(|&&c| c == VoteChoice::Yes)\n        .count() as u64;\n    let no = proposal\n no:{no}, abstain:{abstain}, total:{total_votes}\"\n    );\n\n    let quorum_met = (total_votes * 100 / u64::MAX.max(total_votes)) >= params.quorum_pct as u64;\n\n    if !quorum_met {\n        return ProposalOutcome::QuorumNotMet;\n    }\n\n    let yes_pct = yes * 100 / total_votes;\n    if yes_pct >= params.pass_threshold_pct as u64 {\n        ProposalOutcome::Accepted\n    } else {\n        ProposalOutcome::Rejected\n    }\n}\n\n/// Periodic task for auto-finalizing expired proposals.\nasync fn finalize_expired<S: ProposalStore, E: EventPublisher>(\n    store: &Arc<S>,\n    events: .publish(GovernanceEvent::ProposalFinalized {\n                    proposal_id: proposal.id,\n                    result: outcome,\n                })\n                .await;\n            info!(\"proposal {} finalized with result {:?}\", proposal.id, outcome);\n        }\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n\n/// A mock signature verifier that accepts 1% of the messages randomly\n/// as invalid. Architectural notes\n    10→//! -------------------\n    11→//! • State is kept in-memory behind a `RwLock` and checkpointed by the\n    12→//!   service into persistent storage (e.g. Postgres, RocksDB) – that part is\n    13→//!   out-of-scope here.\n    14→//! • Composer selection follows a simplified Proof-of-Inspiration algorithm:\n    15→//!      1. Each staker submits a *VRF proof* for a given round‐seed.\n    16→//!      2. The hash output is mapped to `[0, 1)`.  The staker wins if\n    17→//!         output / stake_total < staker_weight.\n    18→//!      3. }\n   160→}\n   161→\n   162→/// Shared staking state guarded by `RwLock`.\n   163→#[derive(Default)]\n   164→struct StakePoolInner {\n   165→    stakers: HashMap<StakerId, Staker>,\n   166→ pub async fn select_composer(&self, seed: [u8; 32]) -> Result<Option<StakerId>> {\n   287→        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n   288→\n   289→        if inner.stakers.is_empty() {\n }\n   343→\n   344→    /// Utility used primarily by tests & admin dashboards.\n   345→    pub async fn snapshot(&self) -> (u128, Vec<Staker>) {\n   346→        let inner = self.inner.read().await;\n
