Core staking & composer–selection logic for the CanvasChain Symphony\n//! staking micro-service.  This file purposefully contains no gRPC or HTTP\n//! plumbing;  it is meant to be embedded by the service‐layer binary and\n//! driven by the surrounding transport / storage adapters.\n//!\n//! Architectural notes\n//! -------------------\n//! • State is kept in-memory behind a `RwLock` and checkpointed by the\n//!   service into persistent storage (e.g. Postgres, RocksDB) – that part is\n//!   out-of-scope here.\n//! • Composer selection follows a simplified Proof-of-Inspiration algorithm:\n//!      1. Each staker submits a *VRF proof* for a given round‐seed.\n//!      2. The hash output is mapped to `[0, 1)`.  The staker wins if\n//!         output / stake_total < staker_weight.\n//!      3. Ties are broken deterministically by lowest hash.\n//! • A Strategy pattern exposes pluggable `VrfProvider`s so that collectives\n//!   can experiment with different curves (Ed25519, BLS, PQ, …) without\n//!   recompilation of the other micro-services.\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::{Duration, SystemTime},\n};\n\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse rand::{rngs::OsRng, RngCore};\nuse sha2::{Digest, Sha256};\nuse tokio::{\n    sync::{broadcast, RwLock},\n    time,\n};\n\nuse thiserror::Error;\n\n/// Unique user/staker identifier (e.g. wallet address).\npub type StakerId = String;\n/// Result shorthand used throughout the stake logic.\npub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, StakingError>;\n\n/// Public event types that can be subscribed to by other micro-services\n/// through the event-bus (`broadcast::Sender`).\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub enum StakingEvent {\n    StakeAdded {\n        staker: StakerId,\n        amount: u128,\n        new_total: u128,\n    },\n    StakeWithdrawn {\n        staker: StakerId,\n        amount: u128,\n        new_total: u128,\n    },\n    ComposerSelected {\n        round: u64,\n        staker: StakerId,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Domain errors emitted by staking logic.\n#[derive(Error, Debug)]\npub enum StakingError {\n    #[error(\"insufficient stake: requested {requested}, available {available}\")]\n    InsufficientStake { requested: u128, available: u128 },\n    #[error(\"staker `{0}` is not registered\")]\n    UnknownStaker(StakerId),\n    #[error(\"VRF verification failed: {0}\")]\n    VrfFailed(String),\n    #[error(\"no stakers registered\")]\n    EmptyPool,\n    #[error(\"attempted to withdraw zero tokens\")]\n    ZeroWithdrawal,\n}\n\n/// Represents an individual staking participant.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Staker {\n    pub id: StakerId,\n    pub stake: u128,\n    pub last_active: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>, // curve is provider specific\n}\n\nimpl Staker {\n    fn new(id: StakerId, initial: u128, vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            id,\n            stake: initial,\n            last_active: Utc::now(),\n            vrf_pubkey,\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Abstraction for a Verifiable Random Function provider.\npub trait VrfProvider: Send + Sync + 'static {\n    /// Produces a VRF proof and output hash for `message` using the *private*\n    /// part of the provider implementation.\n    fn prove(&self, message: &[u8]) -> (Vec<u8> /*proof*/, Vec<u8> /*hash*/);\n\n    /// Verify a proof & hash produced by `prove`.\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public_key: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        proof: &[u8],\n        hash: &[u8],\n    ) -> std::result::Result<(), String>;\n}\n\n/// Fallback VRF provider that is *not* secure; meant only for local-dev and\n/// CI where cryptography isn’t the focus.  It deterministically hashes the\n/// message + static `private_key`.\npub struct Sha256Vrf {\n    private_key: Vec<u8>,\n}\n\nimpl Sha256Vrf {\n    pub fn new() -> Self {\n        let mut pk = vec![0u8; 32];\n        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut pk);\n        Self { private_key: pk }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl VrfProvider for Sha256Vrf {\n    fn prove(&self, message: &[u8]) -> (Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>) {\n        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n        hasher.update(&self.private_key);\n        hasher.update(message);\n        let hash = hasher.finalize().to_vec();\n        // `proof` is meaningless here ‑ we return the key for “verification”\n        (self.private_key.clone(), hash)\n    }\n\n    fn verify(\n        &self,\n        public_key: &[u8],\n        message: &[u8],\n        proof: &[u8],\n        hash: &[u8],\n    ) -> std::result::Result<(), String> {\n        if public_key != proof {\n            return Err(\"public key mismatch\".into());\n        }\n        let mut hasher = Sha256::new();\n        hasher.update(proof); // same as private_key in this dummy impl\n        hasher.update(message);\n        let expected = hasher.finalize();\n        if expected.as_slice() == hash {\n            Ok(())\n        } else {\n            Err(\"hash mismatch\".into())\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Shared staking state guarded by `RwLock`.\n#[derive(Default)]\nstruct StakePoolInner {\n    stakers: HashMap<StakerId, Staker>,\n    total_stake: u128,\n    current_round: u64,\n}\n\n/// Public handle for performing staking operations + composer selection.\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct StakePool {\n    inner: Arc<RwLock<StakePoolInner>>,\n    vrf: Arc<dyn VrfProvider>,\n    event_tx: broadcast::Sender<StakingEvent>,\n}\n\nimpl StakePool {\n    /// Create a new [`StakePool`] with the provided `VrfProvider`.\n    pub fn new(vrf: Arc<dyn VrfProvider>) -> Self {\n        let (event_tx, _) = broadcast::channel(256);\n        Self {\n            inner: Arc::default(),\n            vrf,\n            event_tx,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Subscribe to [`StakingEvent`]s.\n    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<StakingEvent> {\n        self.event_tx.subscribe()\n    }\n\n    /// Register a new staker or top-up their existing stake.\n    pub async fn add_stake(\n        &self,\n        staker_id: StakerId,\n        vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>,\n        amount: u128,\n    ) -> Result<()> {\n        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n\n        let entry = inner.stakers.entry(staker_id.clone()).or_insert_with(|| {\n            Staker::new(staker_id.clone(), 0, vrf_pubkey.clone())\n        });\n\n        entry.stake = entry.stake.saturating_add(amount);\n        entry.last_active = Utc::now();\n        inner.total_stake = inner.total_stake.saturating_add(amount);\n\n        self.event_tx.send(StakingEvent::StakeAdded {\n            staker: staker_id,\n            amount,\n            new_total: entry.stake,\n        }).ok();\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Withdraw stake.  Fails if the caller tries to withdraw more than they have.\n    pub async fn withdraw_stake(&self, staker_id: &str, amount: u128) -> Result<()> {\n        if amount == 0 {\n            return Err(StakingError::ZeroWithdrawal);\n        }\n\n        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n        let staker = inner\n            .stakers\n            .get_mut(staker_id)\n            .ok_or_else(|| StakingError::UnknownStaker(staker_id.into()))?;\n\n        if staker.stake < amount {\n            return Err(StakingError::InsufficientStake {\n                requested: amount,\n                available: staker.stake,\n            });\n        }\n\n        staker.stake -= amount;\n        staker.last_active = Utc::now();\n        inner.total_stake -= amount;\n\n        self.event_tx.send(StakingEvent::StakeWithdrawn {\n            staker: staker_id.into(),\n            amount,\n            new_total: staker.stake,\n        }).ok();\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Internal helper to draw a random seed for the next composer round.\n    fn next_round_seed() -> [u8; 32] {\n        let mut seed = [0u8; 32];\n        OsRng.fill_bytes(&mut seed);\n        seed\n    }\n\n    /// Schedule automatic composer selection every `interval`.\n    pub async fn run_composer_election(self: Arc<Self>, interval: Duration) {\n        let mut ticker = time::interval(interval);\n\n        loop {\n            ticker.tick().await;\n            let seed = Self::next_round_seed();\n            match self.select_composer(seed).await {\n                Ok(Some(winner)) => {\n                    tracing::info!(round = self.current_round().await, %winner, \"composer selected\");\n                }\n                Ok(None) => {\n                    // pool is empty\n                }\n                Err(e) => tracing::error!(\"failed to select composer: {e}\"),\n            };\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Return current round number without locking write.\n    pub async fn current_round(&self) -> u64 {\n        self.inner.read().await.current_round\n    }\n\n    /// Execute a single composer election round with the provided seed.\n    /// Returns `Ok(Some(staker_id))` on success, `Ok(None)` if pool empty.\n    /// State changes (round increment) are committed atomically.\n    pub async fn select_composer(&self, seed: [u8; 32]) -> Result<Option<StakerId>> {\n        let mut inner = self.inner.write().await;\n\n        if inner.stakers.is_empty() {\n            return Ok(None);\n        }\n\n        let message = seed; // serialized seed acts as VRF message\n        let mut best_candidate: Option<(StakerId, Vec<u8>)> = None;\n\n        for staker in inner.stakers.values() {\n            // Build domain-separated message: seed || staker-id\n            let mut msg_vec = message.to_vec();\n            msg_vec.extend(staker.id.as_bytes());\n\n            // Each candidate derives a VRF output (hash) – prove/verify may\n            // happen off-chain in a real system; we shortcut here.\n            let (_proof, hash) = self.vrf.prove(&msg_vec);\n\n            // Map first 16 bytes → u128 for uniform comparison\n            let mut buf = [0u8; 16];\n            buf.copy_from_slice(&hash[..16]);\n            let rnd = u128::from_be_bytes(buf);\n\n            // Calculate target: (rnd / 2^128) < (stake / total)\n            // Reorder: rnd * total < stake * 2^128\n            let left = rnd.saturating_mul(inner.total_stake);\n            let right = staker.stake.saturating_mul(u128::MAX);\n\n            let eligible = left < right;\n            if eligible {\n                // Keep the lowest hash to break ties deterministically\n                match &mut best_candidate {\n                    None => best_candidate = Some((staker.id.clone(), hash)),\n                    Some((_best_id, best_hash)) => {\n                        if hash < *best_hash {\n                            *best_hash = hash;\n                            *_best_id = staker.id.clone();\n                        }\n                    }\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        inner.current_round += 1;\n\n        if let Some((winner, _)) = &best_candidate {\n            self.event_tx\n                .send(StakingEvent::ComposerSelected {\n                    round: inner.current_round,\n                    staker: winner.clone(),\n                })\n                .ok();\n        }\n\n        Ok(best_candidate.map(|(id, _)| id))\n    }\n\n    /// Utility used primarily by tests & admin dashboards.\n    pub async fn snapshot(&self) -> (u128, Vec<Staker>) {\n        let inner = self.inner.read().await;\n        (inner.total_stake, inner.stakers.values().cloned().collect())\n    }\n}\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::collections::HashSet;\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn test_stake_add_withdraw() 2_000).await.unwrap();\n        let (total, _) = pool.snapshot().await;\n        assert_eq!(total, 3_000);\n\n        pool.withdraw_stake(\"bob\", 500).await.unwrap();\n        let (total, stakers) = This module exposes a small, but representative, subset of the public HTTP\n     5→//! interface that external clients (CLI, mobile wallets, browsers or embedded\n     6→//! installations) use to interact with the distributed CanvasChain back-end.\n     7→//!\n     8→//! Whenever a request arrives, we\n    10→//! perform cheap syntactic validation, enrich the tracing span with relevant\n    11→//! metadata and forward the call downstream.  Results are converted back to\n    12→//! idiomatic JSON before being returned to the caller.\n    13→//!\n    14→//! ┌──────────────────────────┐      gRPC       ┌──────────────────────────┐\n    15→//! │      HTTP  Clients       ├────────────────►│  CanvasChain Services    │\n    16→//! └──────────────────────────┘                 └──────────────────────────┘\n    17→//!\n    18→//! A note about (missing) authentication: In production we deploy the gateway\n    22→//! behind an API-Key aware service mesh that injects caller identity in the\n    23→//! request headers. 257→/// Serialises `ApiError` into an RFC7807 compliant JSON payload.\n   258→/// All unrecognised errors are mapped to `500 Internal Server Error` in order\n   259→/// to avoid bleeding sensitive \"about:blank\",\n   277→            \"title\":  title,\n   278→            \"status\": status.as_u16(),\n   279→            \"detail\": self.to_string(),\n   280→        });\n   281→\n   282→ BadRequest(String),\n    NotFound(String),\n    Upstream(GrpcStatus), // error from micro-service\n    Transport(tonic::transport::Error),\n    Unexpected(anyhow::Error),\n}\n\nimpl fmt::Display for \"{err}\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl std::error::Error for ApiError {}\n\nimpl From<GrpcStatus> for ApiError {\n    fn from(value: GrpcStatus) -> Self {\n        ApiError::Upstream(value)\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<tonic::transport::Error> for ApiError {\n    fn from(value: tonic::transport::Error) -> Self {\n        ApiError::Transport(value)\n    }\n}\n\nimpl From<anyhow::Error> for ApiError {\n    fn from(value: anyhow::Error) -> Self {\n        ApiError::Unexpected(value)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Serialises `ApiError` into an RFC7807 compliant JSON payload.\n/// All unrecognised errors are mapped to `500 Internal Server Error` in order\n/// to avoid bleeding sensitive information.\nimpl IntoResponse for ApiError {\n    fn into_response(self) -> Response {\n        let (status, title) = ApiError::Upstream(status) => {\n                match status.code() {\n                    tonic::Code::NotFound       => (StatusCode::NOT_FOUND, \"Not Found\"),\n                    tonic::Code::InvalidArgument=> (StatusCode::BAD_REQUEST, \"Bad Request\"),\n                    _                           => (StatusCode::BAD_GATEWAY, \"Upstream Failure\"),\n                }\n            }\n            _ => (StatusCode::INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, \"Internal Server Error\"),\n        };\n\n        let problem = serde_json::json!({\n            \"type\":   \"about:blank\",\n            \"title\":  title,\n            \"status\": status.as_u16(),\n            \"detail\": self.to_string(),\n        });\n\n        (status, Json(problem)).into_response()\n    }\n}\n\n// NOTE: protobuf stubs are generated at build-time by `tonic_build` in the\n//! `build.rs` of the workspace root and re-exported under `crate::pb`.\n\n#![allow(clippy::module_name_repetitions)]\n\nuse std::{\n    collections::HashMap,\n    fmt::Debug,\n    sync::Arc,\n    time::Duration,\n};\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse http::{HeaderValue, Request};\nuse once_cell::sync::Lazy;\nuse tokio::sync::RwLock;\nuse tonic::{\n    codegen::InterceptedService,\n    service::Interceptor,\n    transport::{Channel, Endpoint},\n    Status,\n};\nuse tracing::{debug, error, info, warn};\n\n/// Re-export generated protobuf stubs so callers do not need to depend on the\n/// exact module layout.\npub use crate::pb::{\n    composition::composer_client::ComposerClient,\n    marketplace::marketplace_client::MarketplaceClient,\n    minting::minter_client::MinterClient,\n    remixing::remixer_client::RemixerClient,\n    royalty::royalty_stream_client::RoyaltyStreamClient,\n};\n\n/// Enumeration of every Symphony micro-service reachable over gRPC.\n///\n/// Each variant maps to a build-time default address.  The effective address\n/// can be overridden at runtime with an environment variable of the same name.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]\npub enum ServiceTarget {\n    Composition,\n    Minting,\n    Remixing,\n    Marketplace,\n    Royalty,\n}\n\nimpl ServiceTarget {\n    /// Environment variable used to override the default address.\n    fn env_key(self) -> &'static str {\n        use ServiceTarget::*;\n        match self {\n            Composition => \"COMPOSITION_SERVICE_ADDR\",\n            Minting => \"MINTING_SERVICE_ADDR\",\n            Remixing => \"REMIXING_SERVICE_ADDR\",\n            Marketplace => \"MARKETPLACE_SERVICE_ADDR\",\n            Royalty => \"ROYALTY_SERVICE_ADDR\",\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Reasonable defaults used for local development.\n    fn default_addr(self) -> &'static str {\n        use ServiceTarget::*;\n        match self {\n            Composition => \"http://127.0.0.1:50051\",\n            Minting => \"http://127.0.0.1:50052\",\n            Remixing => \"http://127.0.0.1:50053\",\n            Marketplace => \"http://127.0.0.1:50054\",\n            Royalty => \"http://127.0.0.1:50055\",\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n/// Tiny helper that injects common request metadata such as authentication\n/// tokens or distributed-tracing identifiers.\n#[derive(Clone)]\nstruct MetadataInterceptor {\n    api_key: HeaderValue,\n}\n\nimpl MetadataInterceptor {\n    fn new<S: AsRef<str>>(api_key: S) -> Self {\n        Self {\n            api_key: HeaderValue::from_str(api_key.as_ref())\n                .expect(\"`api_key` must be a valid HTTP header value\"),\n        }\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Interceptor for MetadataInterceptor {\n    fn call(&mut self, mut req: Request<()>) -> std::result::Result<Request<()>, Status> {\n        // Insert auth and a per-request trace-id if present.\n        req.metadata_mut().insert(\"x-api-key\", self.api_key.clone());\n\n        if let Some(span) = tracing::Span::current().id() {\n            req.metadata_mut().insert(\n                \"x-trace-id\",\n                HeaderValue::from_str(&hex::encode(span.into_u64().to_be_bytes()))\n                    .unwrap_or_default(),\n            );\n        }\n\n        Ok(req)\n    }\n}\n\n/// Internal cache for already established channels.  Re-establishing a TCP/TLS\n/// connection on every request would be wasteful.\ntype ChannelCache = Arc<RwLock<HashMap<ServiceTarget, Channel>>>;\n\n/// Central entry-point for constructing **typed** gRPC stubs.\n///\n/// ```no_run\n/// # use services::api_gateway::grpc_client::GrpcClient;\n/// # #[tokio::main(flavor = \"current_thread\")] async fn main() -> anyhow::Result<()> {\n/// let client = GrpcClient::global().await?;\n/// let mut minter = client.minting().await?;\n/// let rsp = minter.mint_token(/* … */).await?;\n/// # Ok(()) }\n/// ```\n#[derive(Clone)]\npub struct GrpcClient {\n    channels: ChannelCache,\n    interceptor: MetadataInterceptor,\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                           Singleton initialisation                         */\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\nstatic GLOBAL_CLIENT: Lazy<Arc<GrpcClient>> = Lazy::new(|| {\n    // Lazy initialisation allows reading environment variables _after_ the\n    // binary has been launched (handy for containers).\n    let api_key = std::env::var(\"CANVAS_API_KEY\").unwrap_or_else(|_| \"local-dev-key\".to_owned());\n\n    Arc::new(\n        tokio::runtime::Handle::current()\n            .block_on(GrpcClient::new(api_key))\n            .expect(\"fatal: cannot initialise gRPC client\"),\n    )\n});\n\nimpl GrpcClient {\n    /// Build a new client from scratch.  You probably want [`GrpcClient::global`].\n    pub async fn new<S: Into<String>>(api_key: S) -> Result<Self> {\n        Ok(Self {\n            channels: Default::default(),\n            interceptor: MetadataInterceptor::new(api_key.into()),\n        })\n    }\n\n    /// Obtain a reference to the global singleton (`Arc`-wrapped).\n    pub async fn global() -> Result<Arc<Self>> {\n        Ok(GLOBAL_CLIENT.clone())\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                     Typed client constructors (public)                 */\n        Ok(MarketplaceClient::with_interceptor(chan, self.interceptor.clone()))\n    }\n\n    /// Royalty-stream micro-service (revenue split & live payouts).\n    pub async fn royalty(\n        &self,\n    ) -> Result<RoyaltyStreamClient<InterceptedService<Channel, MetadataInterceptor>>> {\n        let chan = self.channel(ServiceTarget::Royalty).await?;\n        Ok(RoyaltyStreamClient::with_interceptor(\n            chan,\n            self.interceptor.clone(),\n        ))\n    }\n\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n    /*                          Channel management (private)                  */\n    /* ---------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n\n    #[tracing::instrument(level = \"trace\", skip(self))]\n    async fn channel(&self, target: ServiceTarget) -> Result<Channel> {\n        // Hot path: look-up in the in-memory cache.\n        if let Some(chan) = self.channels.read().await.get(&target).cloned() {\n            return Ok(chan);\n        }\n\n        // Cold path: create a brand new connection.\n        let addr = std::env::var(target.env_key()).unwrap_or_else(|_| target.default_addr().into());\n\n        debug!(?target, %addr, \"establishing new gRPC channel\");\n\n        // Timeouts & TCP/TLS options.\n        let endpoint = Endpoint::from_shared(addr.clone())\n            .with_context(|| format!(\"invalid URI for {:?}\", target))?\n            .connect_timeout(Duration::from_secs(3))\n            .timeout(Duration::from_secs(8))\n            .tcp_nodelay(true);\n\n        // Tonic automatically selects TLS when the URI scheme is `https`.\n        let chan = endpoint.connect().await.with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"could not connect to {:?} service at {}\",\n                target,\n                addr.replace(\"://\", \"s://\") // hide credentials if any\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        // Cache connection for later reuse.\n        self.channels\n            .write()\n            .await\n            .insert(target, chan.clone());\n\n        info!(?target, \"gRPC channel ready\");\n\n        Ok(chan)\n    }\n}\n\n/* -------------------------------------------------------------------------- */\n/*                 // UUIDv7 for UX, unique per chain.\n  NftId  nft        = 2;\n  string seller     = 3;                 // Chain address of seller.\n  Money  ask_price  = 4;                 // If zero, listing is “auction-only”.\n  repeated Bid bids = 5; [Executive Summary](#1-executive-summary)\n2. [Impact Analysis](#2-impact-analysis)\n3. [Data Model Changes](#3-data-model-changes)\n4. [Component-Level Design](#4-component-level-design)\n5. Executive Summary\n\n### 1.1 Feature Overview\n\nFractional NFTs (F-NFTs) enable a single, high-value NFT to be \"sharded\" into multiple fungible tokens representing partial ownership. This feature aims to:\n\n- **Increase liquidity** for high-value NFTs\n- **Democratize access** to expensive digital art\n- **Enable collective ownership** and governance of valuable assets\n\n### 1.2 Architectural Approach\n\nThe F-NFT feature will be implemented by:\n\n1. **Extending the core NFT data model** in `ccs_common` with fractional ownership metadata\n2. **Introducing a new transaction type** (`Transaction::Fractionalize`) in the node service state machine\n3. **Creating dedicated gRPC endpoints** in the minting service for fractionalization operations\n4. **Modifying the marketplace** to support fractional share listings and trading\n5. **Updating the governance service** to handle fractional voting power\n6. **Extending the wallet service** to display and manage fractional holdings\n\n---\n\n## 2. Impact Analysis\n\n### 2.1 Impacted Services\n\n| Service | Impact Level | Description |\n|---------|--------------|-------------|\n| **node_service** | HIGH | New transaction types, state machine changes |\n| **minting_service** | HIGH | Core fractionalization logic, new gRPC endpoints |\n| **marketplace_service** | HIGH | Fractional share trading, listing modifications |\n| **wallet_service** | MEDIUM | Display fractional holdings, balance queries |\n| **governance_service** | MEDIUM | Fractional voting power calculations |\n| **royalty_service** | MEDIUM | Pro-rata royalty distribution to fraction holders |\n| **staking_service** | LOW | Stake-weighted calculations for F-NFT holders |\n| **api_gateway** | MEDIUM | New REST endpoints, gRPC client updates |\n\n### 2.2 Impacted Shared Crates\n\n| Crate | Files Affected | Changes Required |\n|-------|----------------|------------------|\n| **ccs_common** | `src/nft.rs` | New `FractionalInfo` struct, `NftState::Fractionalized` variant |\n| **ccs_proto** | `proto/minting.proto`, `proto/marketplace.proto`, new `proto/fractional.proto` | New message types, RPC definitions |\n| **ccs_db** | `src/state_db.rs` | New tables for fractional shares, ownership mapping |\n| **ccs_consensus** | `src/transaction.rs` | New `Fractionalize`, `RedeemFraction` transaction types |\n\n### 2.3 Critical Files to Modify\n\n#### Core Abstractions (Foundation Layer)\n- `/app/project/crates/ccs_common/src/nft.rs:271-411` - `MultiLayerNft` struct and `NftBuilder`\n- `/app/project/services/node_service/src/state_machine.rs:165-182` - `NodeEvent` enum\n- `/app/project/crates/ccs_proto/proto/common.proto` - Shared protobuf definitions\n\n#### Service-Specific Changes\n- `/app/project/services/minting_service/src/logic/nft_factory.rs:156-264` - `NftFactory` struct\n- `/app/project/services/marketplace_service/src/listing_manager.rs:36-74` - `Listing` struct\n- `/app/project/services/marketplace_service/src/auction_engine.rs:117-135` - `Auction` struct\n- `/app/project/services/wallet_service/src/rpc_server.rs:86-104` - `WalletBackend` trait\n- `/app/project/services/governance_service/src/proposal_engine.rs:66-77` - `Proposal` struct and voting logic\n- `/app/project/services/staking_service/src/staking_logic.rs:79-97` - `Staker` struct\n\n#### API Gateway\n- `/app/project/services/api_gateway/src/rest_api.rs:87-98` - Router configuration\n- `/app/project/services/api_gateway/src/grpc_client.rs:49-60` - `ServiceTarget` enum\n- `/app/project/docs/api/gateway_openapi.yaml` - OpenAPI specification\n\n#### Configuration\n- `/app/project/config/default.toml` - New `[fractional]` section\n\n---\n\n## 3. Data Model Changes\n\n### 3.1 Core NFT Model Extensions\n\n**File:** `/app/project/crates/ccs_common/src/nft.rs`\n\n```rust\n// NEW: Add after line 125 (after NftLayerKind)\n\n/// State of fractionalization for an NFT\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum FractionalizationState {\n    /// NFT has not been fractionalized\n    Whole,\n    /// NFT is currently fractionalized into shares\n    Fractionalized {\n        total_shares: u128,\n        vault_address: WalletAddress,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n    },\n    /// Previously fractionalized, now reconstituted\n    Reconstituted {\n        previous_fractionalization_id: Uuid,\n    },\n}\n\n/// Metadata for a fractional share token\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionalShareToken {\n    pub id: Uuid,\n    /// Reference to the parent NFT\n    pub parent_nft_id: Uuid,\n    /// Total number of shares the parent NFT was divided into\n    pub total_supply: u128,\n    /// Symbol for the fractional token (e.g., \"FNFT-001\")\n    pub symbol: String,\n    /// Decimals for fractional precision (typically 18)\n    pub decimals: u8,\n    /// Minimum shares required for governance participation\n    pub governance_threshold: u128,\n    /// Buyout parameters\n    pub buyout_config: BuyoutConfig,\n    /// Creation timestamp\n    pub created_at: u64,\n}\n\n/// Configuration for NFT buyout (reconstitution)\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct BuyoutConfig {\n    /// Minimum price per share for buyout initiation\n    pub reserve_price_per_share: TokenAmount,\n    /// Duration of buyout auction in seconds\n    pub auction_duration: u64,\n    /// Required quorum percentage for buyout approval (basis Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/crates/ccs_db/src/state_db.rs`\n\nNew tables required:\n\n```sql\n-- Fractional share tokens\nCREATE TABLE fractional_tokens (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n    parent_nft_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nfts(id),\n    total_supply NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL,\n    symbol VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    decimals SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 18,\n    governance_threshold NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    reserve_price_per_share NUMERIC(78, 0),\n    auction_duration_secs BIGINT,\n    approval_quorum_bp SMALLINT,\n    vault_address Fractional ownership records\nCREATE TABLE fractional_holdings (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n    holder_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    share_token_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fractional_tokens(id),\n    amount NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL,\n    locked_amount NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    acquired_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    updated_at gen_random_uuid(),\n    share_token_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fractional_tokens(id),\n    from_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    to_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    amount NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',\n    votes_for NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    votes_against NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n    settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\n-- Indexes for common queries\nCREATE INDEX idx_fractional_holdings_holder ON fractional_holdings(holder_address);\nCREATE INDEX idx_fractional_holdings_token ON fractional_holdings(share_token_id);\nCREATE INDEX idx_fractional_transfers_token ON fractional_transfers(share_token_id);\nCREATE INDEX idx_buyout_proposals_token ON buyout_proposals(share_token_id, status);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Component-Level Design\n\n### 4.1 Node Service Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/services/node_service/src/state_machine.rs`\n\n#### 4.1.1 New Event Types\n\nExtend `NodeEvent` enum (line 167):\n\n```rust\npub enum NodeEvent {\n    // ... existing variants ...\n\n    /// NFT fractionalization request\n    FractionalizeNft {\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        owner: WalletAddress,\n        total_shares: u128,\n        share_symbol: String,\n        buyout_config: BuyoutConfig,\n    },\n\n    /// Transfer fractional shares\n    TransferShares {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        from: WalletAddress,\n        to: WalletAddress,\n        amount: u128,\n    },\n\n    /// Initiate buyout of fractionalized NFT\n    InitiateBuyout {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        proposer: WalletAddress,\n        price_per_share: TokenAmount,\n    },\n\n    /// Vote on buyout proposal\n    VoteBuyout {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: WalletAddress,\n        approve: bool,\n        voting_shares: u128,\n    },\n\n    /// Execute successful buyout\n    ExecuteBuyout {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n    },\n\n    /// Reconstitute NFT (all shares returned)\n    ReconstituteFractionalized {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        redeemer: WalletAddress,\n    },\n}\n```\n\n#### 4.1.2 State Machine Transition Logic\n\nNew state handler in `StateActive`:\n\n```rust\n// Add to StateActive::on_event (line 327)\nNodeEvent::FractionalizeNft { nft_id, owner, total_shares, share_symbol, buyout_config } => {\n    info!(\n        nft_id = %nft_id,\n        owner = %owner,\n        shares = total_shares,\n        \"Processing NFT fractionalization\"\n    );\n\n    // 1. Verify NFT exists and is owned by requester\n    // 2. Verify NFT is not already fractionalized\n    // 3. Create vault account to hold the NFT\n    // 4. Generate fractional share token\n    // 5. Mint total_shares to the owner\n    // 6. Update NFT state to Fractionalized\n    // 7. Emit FractionalizationCompleted event\n\n    let result = ctx.db.fractionalize_nft(\n        nft_id,\n        owner,\n        total_shares,\n        share_symbol,\n        buyout_config\n    ).await?;\n\n    ctx.bus.publish(NodeEvent::FractionalizationCompleted(result))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to publish event: {e}\"))?;\n\n    Ok(Transition::Stay)\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Minting Service Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/services/minting_service/src/logic/nft_factory.rs`\n\n#### 4.2.1 New Request/Response Types\n\n```rust\n// Add after line 70\n\n/// Request to fractionalize an existing NFT\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionalizeRequest {\n    /// ID of the NFT to fractionalize\n    pub nft_id: Uuid,\n    /// Wallet address of the current owner\n    pub owner_wallet: String,\n    /// Number of fractional shares to create\n    pub total_shares: u128,\n    /// Symbol for the fractional token (e.g., \"MONA-F\")\n    pub share_symbol: String,\n    /// Minimum shares needed for governance participation\n    pub governance_threshold: u128,\n    /// Buyout configuration\n    pub buyout_config: BuyoutConfigRequest,\n    /// Nonce for replay protection\n    pub nonce: u64,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct BuyoutConfigRequest {\n    /// Reserve price per share in smallest token denomination\n    pub reserve_price_per_share: u128,\n    /// Auction duration in seconds\n    pub auction_duration_secs: u64,\n    /// Approval quorum in basis points (e.g., 5000 = 50%)\n    pub approval_quorum_bp: u16,\n}\n\n/// Response from fractionalization\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionalizeResponse {\n    /// ID of the created fractional share token\n    pub share_token_id: Uuid,\n    /// Transaction hash\n    pub tx_hash: String,\n    /// Vault address holding the original NFT\n    pub vault_address: String,\n    /// Total shares created\n    pub total_shares: u128,\n}\n```\n\n#### 4.2.2 New Factory Methods\n\n```rust\n// Add to NftFactory impl\n\n/// Fractionalize an existing NFT into fungible share tokens\n#[instrument(skip_all, level = \"info\", fields(nft_id = %req.nft_id))]\npub async fn fractionalize_nft(\n    &self,\n    req: FractionalizeRequest,\n) -> Result<FractionalizeResponse, FactoryError> {\n    // 1. Validate request\n    self.validate_fractionalize_request(&req)?;\n\n    // 2. Verify ownership via chain query\n    let nft = self.chain.get_nft(req.nft_id).await\n        .map_err(|e| FactoryError::Blockchain(e))?;\n\n    if nft.owner != req.owner_wallet {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\n            \"Only NFT owner can fractionalize\".into()\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // 3. Generate vault address for escrowing the NFT\n    let vault_address = self.generate_vault_address(&req.nft_id)?;\n\n    // 4. Build fractionalization payload\n    let share_token_id = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let payload = FractionalizePayload {\n        nft_id: req.nft_id,\n        share_token_id,\n        total_shares: req.total_shares,\n        share_symbol: req.share_symbol.clone(),\n        vault_address: vault_address.clone(),\n        buyout_config: req.buyout_config.clone(),\n        owner: req.owner_wallet.clone(),\n        nonce: req.nonce,\n    };\n\n    // 5. Sign the payload\n    let payload_bytes = self.serialize_fractionalize_payload(&payload)?;\n    let signature = self.signer.sign(&payload_bytes).await?;\n\n    // 6. Submit to chain\n    let tx_hash = self.chain.submit_fractionalize(payload, signature).await?;\n\n    // 7. Publish success event\n    self.bus.publish(\n        &self.config.event_topic_fractionalize,\n        &FractionalizeEvent {\n            nft_id: req.nft_id,\n            share_token_id,\n            total_shares: req.total_shares,\n            vault_address: vault_address.clone(),\n            tx_hash: tx_hash.clone(),\n        }\n    ).await?;\n\n    info!(\n        share_token_id = %share_token_id,\n        tx_hash = %tx_hash,\n        \"NFT fractionalized successfully\"\n    );\n\n    Ok(FractionalizeResponse {\n        share_token_id,\n        tx_hash,\n        vault_address,\n        total_shares: req.total_shares,\n    })\n}\n\nfn validate_fractionalize_request(&self, req: &FractionalizeRequest) -> Result<(), FactoryError> {\n    if req.total_shares == 0 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"total_shares must be > 0\".into()));\n    }\n    if req.total_shares > 1_000_000_000_000 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"total_shares exceeds maximum\".into()));\n    }\n    if req.share_symbol.is_empty() || req.share_symbol.len() > 12 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"invalid share_symbol\".into()));\n    }\n    if req.buyout_config.approval_quorum_bp > 10_000 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"invalid quorum percentage\".into()));\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 Marketplace Service impl\n\n/// Create a listing for fractional NFT shares\n#[instrument(skip(self))]\npub async fn create_fractional_listing(\n    &self,\n    owner: AccountId,\n    share_token_id: Uuid,\n    shares_amount: u128,\n    price_per_share: Decimal,\n) -> Result<ListingId, ListingError> {\n    // Validate the seller owns the shares\n    // (This would involve a call to the wallet service or chain query)\n\n    let total_price = price_per_share * Decimal::from(shares_amount);\n\n    let listing = Listing {\n        id: ListingId::new(),\n        owner,\n        nft_contract: String::new(), // Not applicable for fractional\n        token_id: share_token_id.to_string(),\n        price: total_price,\n        status: ListingStatus::Active,\n        created_at: Utc::now(),\n        updated_at: Utc::now(),\n        listing_type: ListingType::FractionalShares {\n            share_token_id,\n            shares_amount,\n        },\n        price_per_share: Some(price_per_share),\n    };\n\n    timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.insert(listing.clone()))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??;\n\n    self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::FractionalListingCreated {\n        listing_id: listing.id,\n        share_token_id,\n        shares_amount,\n        price_per_share,\n    })?;\n\n    Ok(listing.id)\n}\n\n/// Purchase fractional shares from a listing\n#[instrument(skip(self))]\npub async fn purchase_fractional_shares(\n    &self,\n    listing_id: ListingId,\n    buyer: AccountId,\n    shares_to_buy: u128,\n) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n    let mut listing = timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.get(listing_id))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??;\n\n    // Validate listing is for fractional shares\n    let (share_token_id, available_shares) = match &listing.listing_type {\n        ListingType::FractionalShares { share_token_id, shares_amount } => {\n            (*share_token_id, *shares_amount)\n        }\n        _ => return Err(ListingError::NotFractionalListing),\n    };\n\n    if shares_to_buy > available_shares {\n        return Err(ListingError::InsufficientShares);\n    }\n\n    // Calculate payment\n    let price_per_share = listing.price_per_share\n        .ok_or(ListingError::Repository(\"missing price_per_share\".into()))?;\n    let payment = price_per_share * Decimal::from(shares_to_buy);\n\n    // Update listing (partial fill support)\n    let remaining_shares = available_shares - shares_to_buy;\n    if remaining_shares == 0 {\n        listing.status = ListingStatus::Sold;\n    } else {\n        listing.listing_type = ListingType::FractionalShares {\n            share_token_id,\n            shares_amount: remaining_shares,\n        };\n        listing.price = price_per_share * Decimal::from(remaining_shares);\n    }\n    listing.updated_at = Utc::now();\n\n    timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.update(listing.clone()))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??;\n\n    self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::FractionalSharesPurchased {\n        listing_id,\n        buyer,\n        share_token_id,\n        shares_amount: shares_to_buy,\n        total_price: payment,\n    })?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\n**File:** Fractional Voting Power\n\n```rust\n// Add new voting power calculator\n\n/// Calculate voting power for fractional NFT holders\npub fn calculate_fractional_voting_power(\n    share_token_id: Uuid,\n    voter_shares: u128,\n    total_shares: u128,\n    base_voting_power: u64,\n) -> u64 {\n    if total_shares == 0 {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    // Voting power is proportional to share ownership\n    // scaled by the base voting power of the whole NFT\n    let share_ratio = voter_shares as f64 / total_shares as f64;\n    (base_voting_power as f64 * share_ratio) as u64\n}\n\n// Modify cast_vote method to handle fractional voting\npub async fn cast_vote(\n    &self,\n    proposal_id: Uuid,\n    voter: &str,\n    choice: VoteChoice,\n    signature: &[u8],\n    // NEW: Optional fractional voting parameters\n    fractional_vote: Option<FractionalVoteParams>,\n) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n    // ... existing signature verification ...\n\n    let voting_power = match fractional_vote {\n        Some(params) => {\n            // Verify voter owns the shares\n            // Calculate proportional voting power\n            calculate_fractional_voting_power(\n                params.share_token_id,\n                params.shares_to_vote,\n                params.total_supply,\n                params.base_voting_power,\n            )\n        }\n        None => {\n            // Standard voting power calculation\n            1 // or based on token holdings\n        }\n    };\n\n    // ... rest of voting logic with weighted power ...\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FractionalVoteParams {\n    pub share_token_id: Uuid,\n    pub shares_to_vote: u128,\n    pub total_supply: u128,\n    pub base_voting_power: u64,\n}\n```\n\n### 4.6 Staking Service Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/services/staking_service/src/staking_logic.rs`\n\n#### 4.6.1 Fractional Stake Tracking\n\n```rust\n// Modify Staker struct (line 79) to include fractional holdings\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Staker {\n    pub id: StakerId,\n    pub stake: u128,\n    pub last_active: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>,\n    // NEW: Fractional NFT holdings that count toward stake\n    pub fractional_stakes: Vec<FractionalStake>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FractionalStake {\n    pub share_token_id: Uuid,\n    pub shares_held: u128,\n    /// Stake value derived from share ownership\n    pub stake_value: u128,\n}\n\nimpl Staker {\n    /// Calculate total effective stake including fractional holdings\n    pub fn effective_stake(&self) -> u128 {\n        let fractional_stake: u128 = self.fractional_stakes\n            .iter()\n            .map(|fs| fs.stake_value)\n            .sum();\n\n        self.stake.saturating_add(fractional_stake)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Core User Flows\n\n### 5.1 Flow A: Owner Fractionalizing Their NFT\n\n#### Sequence of Operations\n\n1. **User initiates fractionalization** via API Gateway\n2. **API Gateway** validates request and forwards to Minting Service\n3. **Minting Service** verifies ownership and creates fractionalization payload\n4. **Minting Service** submits transaction to Node Service\n5. **Node Service** validates and processes the transaction:\n   - Creates vault address for NFT escrow\n   - Generates fractional share token\n   - Mints total shares to owner\n   - Updates NFT state to `Fractionalized`\n6. **Node Service** publishes `FractionalizationCompleted` event\n7. **Event listeners** in Wallet, Marketplace, and Governance services update their state\n\n#### Detailed Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌───────────┐\n│  Client  │     │ API Gateway │     │   Minting   │     │    Node     │     │ Event Bus │\n└────┬─────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └─────┬─────┘\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │ POST /v1/nfts/   │                   │                   │                  │\n     │ {nft_id}/        │                   │                   │                  │\n     │ fractionalize    │                   │                   │                  │\n     │─────────────────►│                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │ gRPC:             │                   │                  │\n     │                  │ Fractionalize     │                   │                  │\n     │                  │──────────────────►│                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ 1. Validate owner │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ 2. Build payload  │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ 3. Sign payload   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ gRPC: Submit      │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ FractionalizeNft  │                  │\n     │                  │                   │──────────────────►│                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 1. Create vault  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 2. Lock NFT      │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 3. Mint shares   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 4. Update state  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ NATS: nft.       │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ fractionalized   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │─────────────────►│\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │◄──────────────────│                  │\n     │                  │                   │   tx_hash, token  │                  │\n     │                  │◄──────────────────│                   │                  │\n     │                  │  FractionalizeRsp │                   │                  │\n     │◄─────────────────│                   │                   │                  │\n     │    201 Created   │                   │                   │                  │\n     │   {share_token}  │                   │                   │                  │\n```\n\n### 5.2 Flow B: User Purchasing Fractional Shares\n\n#### Sequence of Operations\n\n1. **User browses fractional listings** via Marketplace API\n2. **User initiates purchase** specifying listing and share amount\n3. **API Gateway** forwards to Marketplace Service\n4. **Marketplace Service** validates listing and calculates price\n5. **Marketplace Service** initiates payment verification\n6. **Marketplace Service** submits share transfer to Node Service\n7. **Node Service** processes transfer transaction:\n   - Debits shares from seller\n   - Credits shares to buyer\n   - Handles royalty distribution\n8. **Node Service** publishes `SharesTransferred` event\n9. **Wallet Service** updates both buyer and seller balances\n\n#### Detailed Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐\n│  Client  │     │ API Gateway │     │ Marketplace │     │    Node     │     │   Wallet    │\n└────┬─────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ GET /v1/         │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ marketplace/     │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ fractional       │                   │                   │                   │\n     │─────────────────►│                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ gRPC:             │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ QueryFractional   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │──────────────────►│                   │                   │\n     │                  │◄──────────────────│                   │                   │\n     │◄─────────────────│  listings[]       │                   │                   │\n     │    200 OK        │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ POST /v1/        │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ marketplace/     │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ buy-fractional   │                   │                   │   │ gRPC: Purchase    │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ FractionalShares  │                   │                   │\n     │                  │──────────────────►│ Validate       │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ 2. Calc price     │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ 3. Reserve shares │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ gRPC: Transfer    │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ SharesWithPayment │                   │\n     │                  │                   │──────────────────►│                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 1. Pay royalties │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ gRPC: Update     │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ Holdings         │\n     │                  │                   │                   │────────────────► │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │◄──────────────────│                   │\n     │                  │◄──────────────────│    tx_hash        │                   │\n     │◄─────────────────│  PurchaseResponse │                   │                   │\n     │    200 OK        │                   │                   │                   │\n     │  {tx_hash,       │                   │                   │                   │\n     │   shares}        │                   │                   │                   │\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. uint64 total_shares_low = 4;\n  string share_symbol = 5;\n  uint64 governance_threshold_high = 6;\n  uint64 governance_threshold_low = 7;\n  BuyoutConfig buyout_config = 8;\n  uint64 nonce = 9;\n  bytes signature = 10;\n}\n\nmessage FractionalizeResponse {\n  string share_token_id = 1;\n  string tx_hash = 2;\n  string vault_address = 3;\n}\n\nmessage TransferSharesRequest {\n  string share_token_id = 1;\n  string from_address = 2;\n  string to_address = 3;\n  uint64 amount_high = 4;\n  uint64 amount_low = 5;\n  uint64 nonce = 6;\n  bytes signature = 7;\n}\n\nmessage TransferSharesResponse {\n  string tx_hash = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetFractionalTokenRequest {\n  string share_token_id = 1;\n}\n\nmessage GetFractionalTokenResponse {\n  FractionalToken token = 1;\n}\n\nmessage QueryHoldingsRequest {\n  string holder_address = 1;\n  ccs.common.CursorPaging paging = 2;\n}\n\nmessage QueryHoldingsResponse {\n  repeated FractionalHolding holdings = 1;\n  string next_cursor = 2;\n}\n\nmessage InitiateBuyoutRequest {\n  string share_token_id = 1;\n  string proposer_address = 2;\n  uint64 price_per_share_high = 3;\n  uint64 price_per_share_low = 4;\n  uint64 nonce = 5;\n  bytes signature = 6;\n}\n\nmessage InitiateBuyoutResponse {\n  string proposal_id = 1;\n  string tx_hash = 2;\n}\n\nmessage VoteBuyoutRequest {\n  string proposal_id = 1;\n  string voter_address = 2;\n  bool approve = 3;\n  uint64 shares_to_vote_high = 4;\n  uint64 shares_to_vote_low = 5;\n  bytes signature = 6;\n}\n\nmessage VoteBuyoutResponse {\n  string tx_hash = 1;\n}\n\nmessage ReconstituteFractionalRequest {\n  string share_token_id = 1;\n  string redeemer_address = 2;\n  bytes signature = 3;\n}\n\nmessage ReconstituteFractionalResponse {\n  string nft_id = 1;\n  string tx_hash = 2;\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// SERVICE\n// ============================================================================\n\nservice FractionalService {\n  // Fractionalize an existing NFT\n  rpc Fractionalize(FractionalizeRequest) returns (FractionalizeResponse);\n\n  // Transfer fractional shares\n  rpc TransferShares(TransferSharesRequest) returns (TransferSharesResponse);\n\n  // Get fractional token details\n  rpc GetFractionalToken(GetFractionalTokenRequest) returns (GetFractionalTokenResponse);\n\n  // Query holdings for an address\n  rpc QueryHoldings(QueryHoldingsRequest) returns (QueryHoldingsResponse);\n\n  // Initiate a buyout proposal\n  rpc InitiateBuyout(InitiateBuyoutRequest) returns (InitiateBuyoutResponse);\n\n  // Vote on a buyout proposal\n  rpc VoteBuyout(VoteBuyoutRequest) returns (VoteBuyoutResponse);\n\n  // Reconstitute NFT (redeem all shares for original)\n  rpc ReconstituteFractional(ReconstituteFractionalRequest) returns (ReconstituteFractionalResponse);\n\n  // Health check\n  rpc Ping(google.protobuf.Empty) returns (google.protobuf.Empty);\n}\n```\n\n### 6.2 API Gateway REST Endpoints\n\n**File:** Event Bus Integration\n\n### 7.1 New NATS Subjects\n\nFollowing the existing subject hierarchy pattern from ADR-002:\n\n```\nnft.fractional.v1.fractionalized     # NFT was fractionalized\nnft.fractional.v1.shares.transferred # Shares transferred between addresses\nnft.fractional.v1.shares.locked      # Shares locked for governance\nnft.fractional.v1.shares.unlocked    # Shares unlocked after vote\nnft.fractional.v1.buyout.initiated   # Buyout proposal created\nnft.fractional.v1.buyout.voted       # Vote cast on buyout\nnft.fractional.v1.buyout.executed    # Buyout completed\nnft.fractional.v1.reconstituted      # NFT reconstituted from shares\n```\n\n### 7.2 Event Payloads\n\n```rust\n// Add to crate event types\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum FractionalEvent {\n    Fractionalized {\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        owner: WalletAddress,\n        total_shares: u128,\n        vault_address: WalletAddress,\n        tx_hash: String,\n        timestamp: u64,\n    },\n\n    SharesTransferred {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        from: WalletAddress,\n        to: WalletAddress,\n        amount: u128,\n        tx_hash: String,\n        timestamp: u64,\n    },\n\n    BuyoutInitiated {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        proposer: WalletAddress,\n        price_per_share: TokenAmount,\n        expires_at: u64,\n        tx_hash: String,\n    },\n\n    BuyoutVoted {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: WalletAddress,\n        approve: bool,\n        voting_shares: u128,\n        tx_hash: String,\n    },\n\n    BuyoutExecuted {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        winner: WalletAddress,\n        total_price: TokenAmount,\n        tx_hash: String,\n    },\n\n    Reconstituted {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        redeemer: WalletAddress,\n        tx_hash: String,\n        timestamp: u64,\n    },\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Security Considerations\n\n### 8.1 Attack Vectors & Mitigations\n\n| Attack Vector | Risk | Mitigation |\n|--------------|------|------------|\n| **Sybil voting** | Attacker creates many addresses to game buyout votes | Require minimum governance threshold; weight votes by shares held |\n| **Flash loan attacks** | Borrow shares, vote, return shares | Implement voting lockup period; snapshot-based voting |\n| **Front-running buyouts** | MEV bots front-run buyout execution | Use commit-reveal scheme for buyout proposals |\n| **Dust share attacks** | Create many tiny listings to spam marketplace | Minimum listing size; listing fees |\n| **Oracle manipulation** | Manipulate price feeds affecting buyout pricing | Use TWAP; multi-oracle aggregation |\n| **Reconstitution griefing** | Collect 99.9% shares, block reconstitution | Forced buyout mechanism with supermajority |\n\n### 8.2 Signature Requirements\n\nAll state-changing operations require:\n1. Valid signature from the transaction initiator\n2. Nonce for replay protection\n3. Timestamp within acceptable drift window\n\n### 8.3 Invariants to Enforce\n\n1. **Share supply invariant**: Sum of all holdings must equal total supply\n2. **Locked share invariant**: Locked shares cannot be transferred\n3. **Vault invariant**: NFT must remain in vault while fractionalized\n4. **Reconstitution invariant**: Only holder of 100% shares can reconstitute\n\n---\n\n## 9. Migration Strategy\n\n### 9.1 Database Migration\n\n1. Create new tables with `IF NOT EXISTS` guards\n2. Add `fractionalization` column to existing `nfts` table as nullable JSON\n3. Backfill existing NFTs with `Whole` state\n4. Create indexes for performance\n\n### 9.2 Service Deployment Order\n\n1. **Phase 1**: Deploy `ccs_common` and `ccs_proto` changes\n2. **Phase 2**: Deploy database migrations\n3. **Phase 3**: Deploy `node_service` with new transaction types (feature-flagged)\n4. **Phase 4**: Deploy `minting_service` with fractionalization logic\n5. **Phase 5**: Deploy `marketplace_service`, `wallet_service` updates\n6. **Phase 6**: Deploy `api_gateway` with new endpoints\n7. **Phase 7**: Enable feature flag; monitor for issues\n8. **Phase 8**: Deploy `governance_service`, `staking_service` updates\n\n### 9.3 Feature Flag\n\nAll F-NFT functionality should be gated by a feature flag:\n\n```rust\n// In each service\nif config.fractional.enabled {\n    // Register F-NFT routes/handlers\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. [Executive Summary](#1-executive-summary)\n2. [Impact Analysis](#2-impact-analysis)\n3. [Data Model Changes](#3-data-model-changes)\n4. [Component-Level Design](#4-component-level-design)\n5. [Security Considerations](#8-security-considerations)\n9. [Migration Strategy](#9-migration-strategy)\n10. [Appendix: Sequence Diagrams](#10-appendix-sequence-diagrams)\n\n---\n\n## 1. Executive Summary\n\n### 1.1 Feature Overview\n\nFractional NFTs (F-NFTs) enable a single, high-value NFT to be \"sharded\" into multiple fungible tokens representing partial ownership. This feature aims to:\n\n- **Increase liquidity** for high-value NFTs\n- **Democratize access** to expensive digital art\n- **Enable collective ownership** and governance of valuable assets\n\n### 1.2 Architectural Approach\n\nThe F-NFT feature will be implemented by:\n\n1. **Extending the core NFT data model** in `ccs_common` with fractional ownership metadata\n2. **Introducing a new transaction type** (`Transaction::Fractionalize`) in the node service state machine\n3. **Creating dedicated gRPC endpoints** in the minting service for fractionalization operations\n4. **Modifying the marketplace** to support fractional share listings and trading\n5. **Updating the governance service** to handle fractional voting power\n6. **Extending the wallet service** to display and manage fractional holdings\n\n---\n\n## 2. Impact Analysis\n\n### 2.1 Impacted Services\n\n| Service | Impact Level | Description |\n|---------|--------------|-------------|\n| **node_service** | HIGH | New transaction types, state machine changes |\n| **minting_service** | HIGH | Core fractionalization logic, new gRPC endpoints |\n| **marketplace_service** | HIGH | Fractional share trading, listing modifications |\n| **wallet_service** | MEDIUM | Display fractional holdings, balance queries |\n| **governance_service** | MEDIUM | Fractional voting power calculations |\n| **royalty_service** | MEDIUM | Pro-rata royalty distribution to fraction holders |\n| **staking_service** | LOW | Stake-weighted calculations for F-NFT holders |\n| **api_gateway** | MEDIUM | New REST endpoints, gRPC client updates |\n\n### 2.2 Impacted Shared Crates\n\n| Crate | Files Affected | Changes Required |\n|-------|----------------|------------------|\n| **ccs_common** | `src/nft.rs` | New `FractionalInfo` struct, `NftState::Fractionalized` variant |\n| **ccs_proto** | `proto/minting.proto`, `proto/marketplace.proto`, new `proto/fractional.proto` | New message types, RPC definitions |\n| **ccs_db** | `src/state_db.rs` | New tables for fractional shares, ownership mapping |\n| **ccs_consensus** | `src/transaction.rs` | New `Fractionalize`, `RedeemFraction` transaction types |\n\n### 2.3 Critical Files to Modify\n\n#### Core Abstractions (Foundation Layer)\n- `/app/project/crates/ccs_common/src/nft.rs:271-411` - `MultiLayerNft` struct and `NftBuilder`\n- `/app/project/services/node_service/src/state_machine.rs:165-182` - `NodeEvent` enum\n- `/app/project/crates/ccs_proto/proto/common.proto` - Shared protobuf definitions\n\n#### Service-Specific Changes\n- `/app/project/services/minting_service/src/logic/nft_factory.rs:156-264` - `NftFactory` struct\n- `/app/project/services/marketplace_service/src/listing_manager.rs:36-74` - `Listing` struct\n- `/app/project/services/marketplace_service/src/auction_engine.rs:117-135` - `Auction` struct\n- `/app/project/services/wallet_service/src/rpc_server.rs:86-104` - `WalletBackend` trait\n- `/app/project/services/governance_service/src/proposal_engine.rs:66-77` - `Proposal` struct and voting logic\n- `/app/project/services/staking_service/src/staking_logic.rs:79-97` - `Staker` struct\n\n#### API Gateway\n- `/app/project/services/api_gateway/src/rest_api.rs:87-98` - Router configuration\n- `/app/project/services/api_gateway/src/grpc_client.rs:49-60` - `ServiceTarget` enum\n- `/app/project/docs/api/gateway_openapi.yaml` - Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/crates/ccs_db/src/state_db.rs`\n\nNew tables required:\n\n```sql\n-- Fractional share tokens\nCREATE TABLE fractional_tokens (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY,\n    parent_nft_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES nfts(id),\n    total_supply NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL,\n    symbol VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL UNIQUE,\n    decimals SMALLINT NOT NULL DEFAULT 18,\n    governance_threshold NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    reserve_price_per_share NUMERIC(78, 0),\n    auction_duration_secs BIGINT,\n    approval_quorum_bp SMALLINT,\n    vault_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    reconstituted_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    CONSTRAINT valid_decimals CHECK (decimals >= 0 AND decimals <= 18)\n);\n\n-- Fractional ownership records\nCREATE TABLE fractional_holdings (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n    holder_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    share_token_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fractional_tokens(id),\n    amount NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL,\n    locked_amount NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    acquired_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    updated_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW(),\n    UNIQUE(holder_address, share_token_id)\n);\n\n-- Fractional transfer history\nCREATE TABLE fractional_transfers (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n    share_token_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fractional_tokens(id),\n    from_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    to_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    amount NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL,\n    tx_hash VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\n-- Buyout proposals\nCREATE TABLE buyout_proposals (\n    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),\n    share_token_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES fractional_tokens(id),\n    proposer_address VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,\n    price_per_share NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL,\n    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'pending',\n    votes_for NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    votes_against NUMERIC(78, 0) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n    expires_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL,\n    settled_at TIMESTAMPTZ,\n    created_at TIMESTAMPTZ NOT NULL DEFAULT NOW()\n);\n\n-- Indexes for common queries\nCREATE INDEX idx_fractional_holdings_holder ON fractional_holdings(holder_address);\nCREATE INDEX idx_fractional_holdings_token ON fractional_holdings(share_token_id);\nCREATE INDEX idx_fractional_transfers_token ON fractional_transfers(share_token_id);\nCREATE INDEX idx_buyout_proposals_token ON buyout_proposals(share_token_id, status);\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. Verify NFT exists and is owned by requester\n    // 2. Verify NFT is not already fractionalized\n    // 3. Create vault account to hold the NFT\n    // 4. Generate fractional share token\n    // 5. Mint total_shares to the owner\n    // 6. Update NFT state to Fractionalized\n    // 7. Emit FractionalizationCompleted event\n\n    let result = ctx.db.fractionalize_nft(\n        nft_id,\n        owner,\n        total_shares,\n        share_symbol,\n        buyout_config\n    ).await?;\n\n    ctx.bus.publish(NodeEvent::FractionalizationCompleted(result))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"failed to publish event: {e}\"))?;\n\n    Ok(Transition::Stay)\n}\n```\n\n### 4.2 Minting Service Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/services/minting_service/src/logic/nft_factory.rs`\n\n#### 4.2.1 New Request/Response Types\n\n```rust\n// Add after line 70\n\n/// Request to fractionalize an existing NFT\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionalizeRequest {\n    /// ID of the NFT to fractionalize\n    pub nft_id: Uuid,\n    /// Wallet address of the current owner\n    pub owner_wallet: String,\n    /// Number of fractional shares to create\n    pub total_shares: u128,\n    /// Symbol for the fractional token (e.g., \"MONA-F\")\n    pub share_symbol: String,\n    /// Minimum shares needed for governance participation\n    pub governance_threshold: u128,\n    /// Buyout configuration\n    pub buyout_config: BuyoutConfigRequest,\n    /// Nonce for replay protection\n    pub nonce: u64,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct BuyoutConfigRequest {\n    /// Reserve price per share in smallest token denomination\n    pub reserve_price_per_share: u128,\n    /// Auction duration in seconds\n    pub auction_duration_secs: u64,\n    /// Approval quorum in basis points (e.g., 5000 = 50%)\n    pub approval_quorum_bp: u16,\n}\n\n/// Response from fractionalization\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct FractionalizeResponse {\n    /// ID of the created fractional share token\n    pub share_token_id: Uuid,\n    /// Transaction hash\n    pub tx_hash: String,\n    /// Vault address holding the original NFT\n    pub vault_address: String,\n    /// Total shares created\n    pub total_shares: u128,\n}\n```\n\n#### 4.2.2 New Factory Methods\n\n```rust\n// Add to NftFactory impl\n\n/// Fractionalize an existing NFT into fungible share tokens\n#[instrument(skip_all, level = \"info\", fields(nft_id = %req.nft_id))]\npub async fn fractionalize_nft(\n    &self,\n    req: FractionalizeRequest,\n) -> Result<FractionalizeResponse, FactoryError> {\n    // 1. Validate request\n    self.validate_fractionalize_request(&req)?;\n\n    // 2. Verify ownership via chain query\n    let nft = self.chain.get_nft(req.nft_id).await\n        .map_err(|e| FactoryError::Blockchain(e))?;\n\n    if nft.owner != req.owner_wallet {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\n            \"Only NFT owner can fractionalize\".into()\n        ));\n    }\n\n    // 3. Generate vault address for escrowing the NFT\n    let vault_address = self.generate_vault_address(&req.nft_id)?;\n\n    // 4. Build fractionalization payload\n    let share_token_id = Uuid::new_v4();\n    let payload = FractionalizePayload {\n        nft_id: req.nft_id,\n        share_token_id,\n        total_shares: req.total_shares,\n        share_symbol: req.share_symbol.clone(),\n        vault_address: vault_address.clone(),\n        buyout_config: req.buyout_config.clone(),\n        owner: req.owner_wallet.clone(),\n        nonce: req.nonce,\n    };\n\n    // 5. Sign the payload\n    let payload_bytes = self.serialize_fractionalize_payload(&payload)?;\n    let signature = self.signer.sign(&payload_bytes).await?;\n\n    // 6. Submit to chain\n    let tx_hash = self.chain.submit_fractionalize(payload, signature).await?;\n\n    // 7. Publish success event\n    self.bus.publish(\n        &self.config.event_topic_fractionalize,\n        &FractionalizeEvent {\n            nft_id: req.nft_id,\n            share_token_id,\n            total_shares: req.total_shares,\n            vault_address: vault_address.clone(),\n            tx_hash: tx_hash.clone(),\n        }\n    ).await?;\n\n    info!(\n        share_token_id = %share_token_id,\n        tx_hash = %tx_hash,\n        \"NFT fractionalized successfully\"\n    );\n\n    Ok(FractionalizeResponse {\n        share_token_id,\n        tx_hash,\n        vault_address,\n        total_shares: req.total_shares,\n    })\n}\n\nfn validate_fractionalize_request(&self, req: &FractionalizeRequest) -> Result<(), FactoryError> {\n    if req.total_shares == 0 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"total_shares must be > 0\".into()));\n    }\n    if req.total_shares > 1_000_000_000_000 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"total_shares exceeds maximum\".into()));\n    }\n    if req.share_symbol.is_empty() || req.share_symbol.len() > 12 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"invalid share_symbol\".into()));\n    }\n    if req.buyout_config.approval_quorum_bp > 10_000 {\n        return Err(FactoryError::Validation(\"invalid quorum percentage\".into()));\n    }\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\n### 4.3 Marketplace Service pub token_id: String,\n    pub price: Decimal,\n    pub status: ListingStatus,\n    pub created_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub updated_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    // NEW: Listing type\n    pub listing_type: impl\n\n/// Create a listing for fractional NFT shares\n#[instrument(skip(self))]\npub async fn create_fractional_listing(\n    &self,\n    owner: AccountId,\n    share_token_id: Uuid,\n    shares_amount: u128,\n    price_per_share: Decimal,\n) -> Result<ListingId, ListingError> {\n    // Validate the seller owns the shares\n    // (This would involve a call to the wallet service or chain query)\n\n    let total_price = price_per_share * Decimal::from(shares_amount);\n\n    let listing = Listing {\n        id: ListingId::new(),\n        owner,\n        nft_contract: String::new(), // Not applicable for fractional\n        token_id: share_token_id.to_string(),\n        price: total_price,\n        status: ListingStatus::Active,\n        created_at: Utc::now(),\n        updated_at: Utc::now(),\n        listing_type: ListingType::FractionalShares {\n            share_token_id,\n            shares_amount,\n        },\n        price_per_share: Some(price_per_share),\n    };\n\n    timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.insert(listing.clone()))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??;\n\n    self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::FractionalListingCreated {\n        listing_id: listing.id,\n        share_token_id,\n        shares_amount,\n        price_per_share,\n    })?;\n\n    Ok(listing.id)\n}\n\n/// Purchase fractional shares from a listing\n#[instrument(skip(self))]\npub async fn purchase_fractional_shares(\n    &self,\n    listing_id: ListingId,\n    buyer: AccountId,\n    shares_to_buy: u128,\n) -> Result<(), ListingError> {\n    let mut listing = timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.get(listing_id))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??;\n\n    // Validate listing is for fractional shares\n    let (share_token_id, available_shares) = match &listing.listing_type {\n        ListingType::FractionalShares { share_token_id, shares_amount } => {\n            (*share_token_id, *shares_amount)\n        }\n        _ => return Err(ListingError::NotFractionalListing),\n    };\n\n    if shares_to_buy > available_shares {\n        return Err(ListingError::InsufficientShares);\n    }\n\n    // Calculate payment\n    let price_per_share = listing.price_per_share\n        .ok_or(ListingError::Repository(\"missing price_per_share\".into()))?;\n    let payment = price_per_share * Decimal::from(shares_to_buy);\n\n    // Update listing (partial fill support)\n    let remaining_shares = available_shares - shares_to_buy;\n    if remaining_shares == 0 {\n        listing.status = ListingStatus::Sold;\n    } else {\n        listing.listing_type = ListingType::FractionalShares {\n            share_token_id,\n            shares_amount: remaining_shares,\n        };\n        listing.price = price_per_share * Decimal::from(remaining_shares);\n    }\n    listing.updated_at = Utc::now();\n\n    timeout(DB_OP_TIMEOUT, self.repo.update(listing.clone()))\n        .await\n        .map_err(|_| ListingError::Timeout)??;\n\n    self.dispatch_event(MarketplaceEvent::FractionalSharesPurchased {\n        listing_id,\n        buyer,\n        share_token_id,\n        shares_amount: shares_to_buy,\n        total_price: payment,\n    })?;\n\n    Ok(())\n}\n```\n\n**File:** `/app/project/services/marketplace_service/src/auction_engine.rs`\n\n#### 4.3.3 Extend Auction Engine\n\n```rust\n// Modify CreateAuction struct (line 93)\n\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct CreateAuction
