Fractional Voting Power\n\n```rust\n// Add new voting power calculator\n\n/// Calculate voting power for fractional NFT holders\npub fn calculate_fractional_voting_power(\n    share_token_id: Uuid,\n    voter_shares: u128,\n    total_shares: u128,\n    base_voting_power: u64,\n) -> u64 {\n    if total_shares == 0 {\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    // Voting power is proportional to share ownership\n    // scaled by the base voting power of the whole NFT\n    let share_ratio = voter_shares as f64 / total_shares as f64;\n    (base_voting_power as f64 * share_ratio) as u64\n}\n\n// Modify cast_vote method to handle fractional voting\npub async fn cast_vote(\n    &self,\n    proposal_id: Uuid,\n    voter: &str,\n    choice: VoteChoice,\n    signature: &[u8],\n    // NEW: Optional fractional voting parameters\n    fractional_vote: Option<FractionalVoteParams>,\n) -> Result<(), ProposalError> {\n    // ... existing signature verification ...\n\n    let voting_power = match fractional_vote {\n        Some(params) => {\n            // Verify voter owns the shares\n            // Calculate proportional voting power\n            calculate_fractional_voting_power(\n                params.share_token_id,\n                params.shares_to_vote,\n                params.total_supply,\n                params.base_voting_power,\n            )\n        }\n        None => {\n            // Standard voting power calculation\n            1 // or based on token holdings\n        }\n    };\n\n    // ... rest of voting logic with weighted power ...\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FractionalVoteParams {\n    pub share_token_id: Uuid,\n    pub shares_to_vote: u128,\n    pub total_supply: u128,\n    pub base_voting_power: u64,\n}\n```\n\n### 4.6 Staking Service Changes\n\n**File:** `/app/project/services/staking_service/src/staking_logic.rs`\n\n#### 4.6.1 Fractional Stake Tracking\n\n```rust\n// Modify Staker struct (line 79) to include fractional holdings\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct Staker {\n    pub id: StakerId,\n    pub stake: u128,\n    pub last_active: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub vrf_pubkey: Vec<u8>,\n    // NEW: Fractional NFT holdings that count toward stake\n    pub fractional_stakes: Vec<FractionalStake>,\n}\n\n#[derive(Debug, Clone)]\npub struct FractionalStake {\n    pub share_token_id: Uuid,\n    pub shares_held: u128,\n    /// Stake value derived from share ownership\n    pub stake_value: u128,\n}\n\nimpl Staker {\n    /// Calculate total effective stake including fractional holdings\n    pub fn effective_stake(&self) -> u128 {\n        let fractional_stake: u128 = self.fractional_stakes\n            .iter()\n            .map(|fs| fs.stake_value)\n            .sum();\n\n        self.stake.saturating_add(fractional_stake)\n    }\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 5. Core User Flows\n\n### 5.1 Flow A: Owner Fractionalizing Their NFT\n\n#### Sequence of Operations\n\n1. **User initiates fractionalization** via API Gateway\n2. **API Gateway** validates request and forwards to Minting Service\n3. **Minting Service** verifies ownership and creates fractionalization payload\n4. **Minting Service** submits transaction to Node Service\n5. **Node Service** validates and processes the transaction:\n   - Creates vault address for NFT escrow\n   - Generates fractional share token\n   - Mints total shares to owner\n   - Updates NFT state to `Fractionalized`\n6. **Node Service** publishes `FractionalizationCompleted` event\n7. **Event listeners** in Wallet, Marketplace, and Governance services update their state\n\n#### Detailed Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌───────────┐\n│  Client  │     │ API Gateway │     │   Minting   │     │    Node     │     │ Event Bus │\n└────┬─────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └─────┬─────┘\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │ POST /v1/nfts/   │                   │                   │                  │\n     │ {nft_id}/        │                   │                   │                  │\n     │ fractionalize    │                   │                   │                  │\n     │─────────────────►│                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │ gRPC:             │                   │                  │\n     │                  │ Fractionalize     │                   │                  │\n     │                  │──────────────────►│                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ 1. Validate owner │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ 2. Build payload  │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ 3. Sign payload   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ gRPC: Submit      │                  │\n     │                  │                   │ FractionalizeNft  │                  │\n     │                  │                   │──────────────────►│                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 1. Create vault  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 2. Lock NFT      │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 3. Mint shares   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 4. Update state  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ NATS: nft.       │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ fractionalized   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │─────────────────►│\n     │                  │                   │                   │                  │\n     │                  │                   │◄──────────────────│                  │\n     │                  │                   │   tx_hash, token  │                  │\n     │                  │◄──────────────────│                   │                  │\n     │                  │  FractionalizeRsp │                   │                  │\n     │◄─────────────────│                   │                   │                  │\n     │    201 Created   │                   │                   │                  │\n     │   {share_token}  │                   │                   │                  │\n```\n\n### 5.2 Flow B: User Purchasing Fractional Shares\n\n#### Sequence of Operations\n\n1. **User browses fractional listings** via Marketplace API\n2. **User initiates purchase** specifying listing and share amount\n3. **API Gateway** forwards to Marketplace Service\n4. **Marketplace Service** validates listing and calculates price\n5. **Marketplace Service** initiates payment verification\n6. **Marketplace Service** submits share transfer to Node Service\n7. **Node Service** processes transfer transaction:\n   - Debits shares from seller\n   - Credits shares to buyer\n   - Handles royalty distribution\n8. **Node Service** publishes `SharesTransferred` event\n9. **Wallet Service** updates both buyer and seller balances\n\n#### Detailed Flow\n\n```\n┌──────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐     ┌─────────────┐\n│  Client  │     │ API Gateway │     │ Marketplace │     │    Node     │     │   Wallet    │\n└────┬─────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘     └──────┬──────┘\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ GET /v1/         │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ marketplace/     │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ fractional       │                   │                   │                   │\n     │─────────────────►│                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ gRPC:             │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ QueryFractional   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │──────────────────►│                   │                   │\n     │                  │◄──────────────────│                   │                   │\n     │◄─────────────────│  listings[]       │                   │                   │\n     │    200 OK        │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ POST /v1/        │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ marketplace/     │                   │                   │                   │\n     │ buy-fractional   │                   │                   │                   │\n     │─────────────────►│                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ gRPC: Purchase    │                   │                   │\n     │                  │ FractionalShares  │                   │                   │\n     │                  │──────────────────►│                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ 1. Validate       │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ 2. Calc price     │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ 3. Reserve shares │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ gRPC: Transfer    │                   │\n     │                  │                   │ SharesWithPayment │                   │\n     │                  │                   │──────────────────►│                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 1. Debit seller  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 2. Credit buyer  │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ 3. Pay royalties │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ gRPC: Update     │\n     │                  │                   │                   │ Holdings         │\n     │                  │                   │                   │────────────────► │\n     │                  │                   │                   │                   │\n     │                  │                   │◄──────────────────│                   │\n     │                  │◄──────────────────│    tx_hash        │                   │\n     │◄─────────────────│  PurchaseResponse │                   │                   │\n     │    200 OK        │                   │                   │                   │\n     │  {tx_hash,       │                   │                   │                   │\n     │   shares}        │                   │                   │                   │\n```\n\n---\n\n## 6. \"nft.shares.transferred\"\nbuyout_initiated = \"nft.buyout.initiated\"\nbuyout_voted = \"nft.buyout.voted\"\nbuyout_executed = \"nft.buyout.executed\"\nreconstituted = \"nft.reconstituted\"\n\n# Rate limiting\n[fractional.rate_limits]\nfractionalize_per_hour = 10\ntransfer_per_minute = 100\nbuyout_per_day = 5\n```\n\n---\n\n## 7. Event Bus Integration\n\n### 7.1 New NATS Subjects\n\nFollowing the existing subject hierarchy pattern from ADR-002:\n\n```\nnft.fractional.v1.fractionalized     # NFT was fractionalized\nnft.fractional.v1.shares.transferred # Shares transferred between addresses\nnft.fractional.v1.shares.locked      # Shares locked for governance\nnft.fractional.v1.shares.unlocked    # Shares unlocked after vote\nnft.fractional.v1.buyout.initiated   # Buyout proposal created\nnft.fractional.v1.buyout.voted       # Vote cast on buyout\nnft.fractional.v1.buyout.executed    # Buyout completed\nnft.fractional.v1.reconstituted      # NFT reconstituted from shares\n```\n\n### 7.2 Event Payloads\n\n```rust\n// Add to crate event types\n\n#[derive(Clone, Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub enum FractionalEvent {\n    Fractionalized {\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        owner: WalletAddress,\n        total_shares: u128,\n        vault_address: WalletAddress,\n        tx_hash: String,\n        timestamp: u64,\n    },\n\n    SharesTransferred {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        from: WalletAddress,\n        to: WalletAddress,\n        amount: u128,\n        tx_hash: String,\n        timestamp: u64,\n    },\n\n    BuyoutInitiated {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        proposer: WalletAddress,\n        price_per_share: TokenAmount,\n        expires_at: u64,\n        tx_hash: String,\n    },\n\n    BuyoutVoted {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        voter: WalletAddress,\n        approve: bool,\n        voting_shares: u128,\n        tx_hash: String,\n    },\n\n    BuyoutExecuted {\n        proposal_id: Uuid,\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        winner: WalletAddress,\n        total_price: TokenAmount,\n        tx_hash: String,\n    },\n\n    Reconstituted {\n        share_token_id: Uuid,\n        nft_id: Uuid,\n        redeemer: WalletAddress,\n        tx_hash: String,\n        timestamp: u64,\n    },\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Security Considerations\n\n### 8.1 Attack Vectors & Mitigations\n\n| Attack Vector | Risk | Mitigation |\n|--------------|------|------------|\n| **Sybil voting** | Attacker creates many addresses to game buyout votes | Require minimum governance threshold; weight votes by shares held |\n| **Flash loan attacks** | Borrow shares, vote, return shares | Implement voting lockup period; snapshot-based voting |\n| **Front-running buyouts** | MEV bots front-run buyout execution | Use commit-reveal scheme for buyout proposals |\n| **Dust share attacks** | Create many tiny listings to spam marketplace | Minimum listing size; listing fees |\n| **Oracle manipulation** | Manipulate price feeds affecting buyout pricing | Use TWAP; multi-oracle aggregation |\n| **Reconstitution griefing** | Collect 99.9% shares, block reconstitution | Forced buyout mechanism with supermajority |\n\n### 8.2 Signature Requirements\n\nAll state-changing operations require:\n1. Valid signature from the transaction initiator\n2. Nonce for replay protection\n3. Timestamp within acceptable drift window\n\n### 8.3 Invariants to Enforce\n\n1. **Share supply invariant**: Sum of all holdings must equal total supply\n2. **Locked share invariant**: Locked shares cannot be transferred\n3. **Vault invariant**: NFT must remain in vault while fractionalized\n4. **Reconstitution invariant**: Only holder of 100% shares can reconstitute\n\n---\n\n## 9. Migration Strategy\n\n### 9.1 Database Migration\n\n1. Create new tables with `IF NOT EXISTS` guards\n2. Add `fractionalization` column to existing `nfts` table as nullable JSON\n3. Backfill existing NFTs with `Whole` state\n4. Create indexes for performance\n\n### 9.2 Service Deployment Order\n\n1. **Phase 1**: Deploy `ccs_common` and `ccs_proto` changes\n2. **Phase 2**: Deploy database migrations\n3. **Phase 3**: Deploy `node_service` with new transaction types (feature-flagged)\n4. **Phase 4**: Deploy `minting_service` with fractionalization logic\n5. **Phase 5**: Deploy `marketplace_service`, `wallet_service` updates\n6. **Phase 6**: Deploy `api_gateway` with new endpoints\n7. **Phase 7**: Enable feature flag; monitor for issues\n8. **Phase 8**: Deploy `governance_service`, `staking_service` updates\n\n### 9.3 Feature Flag\n\nAll F-NFT functionality should be gated by a feature flag:\n\n```rust\n// In each service\nif config.fractional.enabled {\n    // Register F-NFT routes/handlers\n}\n```\n\n---\n\n## 10. Appendix: Sequence Diagrams\n\n### 10.1 Complete Fractionalization Flow\n\n```\nUser        Gateway      Minting      Node       Wallet      EventBus\n │            │            │           │           │            │\n │ POST       │            │           │           │            │\n │ /fraction  │            │           │           │            │\n │───────────>│            │           │  │            │           │\n```\n\n---\n\n## Summary\n\nThis architectural plan provides a comprehensive roadmap for implementing Fractional NFTs in the CanvasChain Symphony platform. Key points:\n\n1. **Foundation**: Changes start in `ccs_common/src/nft.rs` with new data structures and extend to all services\n2. **State Management**: The `node_service` state machine handles all critical state transitions atomically\n3. **Service Communication**: All inter-service calls follow the existing gRPC + NATS patterns per ADR-002\n4. **Security**: Multiple attack vectors are addressed with signature requirements and voting lockups\n5. **Migration**: Phased deployment with feature flags enables safe rollout\n\nThe implementation team should follow the component-level designs closely, ensuring that:\n- All state changes are validated by the consensus nodes\n- Events are published for all significant state transitions\n- The existing architecture patterns (Factory, Strategy, Observer) are maintained\n- Tests are written for each new transaction type and API endpoint\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}
